📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期乙型肝炎病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 与连续治疗相比,有限 NA 治疗可能会增加 HBsAg 消失的可能性,尽管病毒学复发和再治疗的发生率较高。研究结果表明,有限疗法可能是专家中心针对 功能性治愈 的一种选择性的、以患者为中心的策略,没有证据表明存在严重伤害。
  • 总体抗 HDV 血清流行率 为 2.7% (14/526)。患病率 在不同疾病组之间没有显着差异,但抗 HDV 血清流行率 在晚期 HCC 阶段 (BCLC C-D) 中显着高于极早期至中期阶段 (BCLC 0-B)(5.3% vs 0.9%;p=0.024)。仅在一名患者中检测到 HDV RNA(占 队列研究 总数的 0.2%;抗 HDV 阳性患者的 7.1%)。
  • 在可获得 HBV-DNA 结果的参与者中,87% 的 HBV-DNA 检测不到。总体而言,81% 的人曾经接受过抗 HDV 检测,而在抗 HDV 阳性个体中,66% 的人接受过 HDV-RNA 检测。
  • 观察到的HCC发病率总体上并未显著低于预期,但在第5年观察到下降趋势,标准化的HCC发病率(SIR)为0.53(95%置信区间,0.24至1.20)。6名患者发展为HCC,其累计发病率分别为1年时2.0%、3年时5.1%和5年时6.1%。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • Finite NA therapy may increase the probability of HBsAg loss compared to continuous therapy, despite higher rates of virologic relapse and retreatment.
  • The overall anti-HDV seroprevalence was 2.7% (14/526).
  • Up to 5 years of follow-up (117 814 person-years), we documented 635 incident cirrhosis and 164 HCC cases. [10.1136/gutjnl-2025-335449].
  • Among participants with available HBV-DNA result, 87% had undetectable HBV-DNA. Overall, 81% had ever undergone anti-HDV testing and, among anti-HDV-positive individuals, 66% had undergone HDV-RNA testing.

📰 本期新闻动态 / News Brief

  • 本期乙型肝炎病毒新闻集中呈现以下动态。
  • 丹麦一所大学批准了一项由 CDC 资助的非洲新生儿研究,其中只有一半的婴儿会感染乙型肝炎 疫苗。这项研究被谴责为不道德的。 [news-5df3717d0e9410cf]。
  • 该文章讨论了一项关于炎症性肠病 (IBD) 食物触发因素的研究,并提到了猫感染乙型肝炎的病例,但没有证实或报告人类中任何特定的 暴发 或与 乙型肝炎病毒 相关的事件。该内容是解释性的,并非已确认的事件。
  • AusperBio Therapeutics,Inc.正在AASLD The Liver Meeting® 2026上展示慢性乙型肝炎中AHB-137 治疗的长期随访数据。该数据与临床试验相关,而不是暴发或公共卫生事件。
  • 巴西一项为期 14 年的分析发现,癌症患者中 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 暴露量有所下降,但男性、老年人和非白人患者之间仍然存在差异。该研究发表在《癌症原因与控制》杂志上。 [news-c6ef7532078cd6bd]。
  • This reporting window highlights the following Hepatitis B virus news developments.
  • A Danish university has approved a CDC-funded study in African newborns, in which only half of babies will receive the hepatitis B vaccine. The study has been condemned as unethical. [news-5df3717d0e9410cf].
  • The article discusses a study on food triggers in inflammatory bowel disease (IBD) and mentions a case of hepatitis B from a cat, but does not confirm or report any specific outbreak or event related to hepatitis B virus in humans.
  • AusperBio Therapeutics, Inc. is presenting long-term follow-up data of AHB-137 treatment in chronic hepatitis B at AASLD The Liver Meeting® 2026.
  • A fourteen-year analysis from Brazil found a decline in hepatitis B virus (HBV) exposure among cancer patients, though disparities persist among men, older adults, and non-White patients.

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Gut  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-10-07  |  DOI: 10.1136/gutjnl-2025-335449  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 75, 11, 2232-2240
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

重新审视 EASL 2025 适应症:慢性乙型肝炎 病毒感染中使用和不使用核苷(酸)类似物治疗的阶段特定结果。

EASL 2025 indications revisited: phase-specific outcomes with and without nucleos(t)ide analogue therapy in chronic hepatitis B virus infection.
作者： Shichuan Tang, Tingfeng Huang, Ruijing Tang, Kongying Lin, Cong Luo, Yubing Shen, Kailing Zhang, Yidan Tang, Jie Kong, Zhenwei Chen
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估2025年EASL强烈推荐的治疗标准之外的中国慢性乙型肝炎病毒感染者中核苷类似物(NAs)治疗与肝硬化和肝细胞癌(HCC)风险之间的关联。NAs 治疗对不符合这些标准的慢性 HBV 感染者的长期结果的影响仍不确定。
设计与对象
我们分析了中国 12 个中心的 30,784 名慢性乙型肝炎病毒感染者的数据。主要终点是肝硬化和 HCC 的 发病率。我们根据 HBV DNA 水平、ALT 状态、纤维化阶段和 HBeAg 状态,将 2025 年 EASL 强烈推荐的 治疗 标准之外的个体分为四组。
核心方法
我们进行了多变量分析和倾向评分匹配分析,以评估 NA 治疗与肝硬化和 HCC 风险之间的关联。该研究纳入了大量慢性 HBV 感染者,并对他们进行了长达 5 年的跟踪调查。
主要结果
Up to 5 years of follow-up (117 814 person-years), we documented 635 incident cirrhosis and 164 HCC cases. In multivariable analyses, NA therapy significantly reduced risks of cirrhosis (HR 0.18, 95% CI 0.11 to 0.32) and HCC (HR 0.03, 95% CI 0.01 to 0.21) in Group 1, and cirrhosis (HR 0.50, 95% CI 0.41 to 0.61) and HCC (HR 0.25, 95% CI 0.16 to 0.41) in Group 2. No significant associations were observed in Groups 3 and 4. Findings were consistent in propensity score matching analyses.
解释与创新
NAs 治疗与具有高复制或即将发生相变表型的 HBeAg 阳性个体发生肝硬化和 HCC 的风险降低相关,支持慢性 HBV 感染 治疗 标准的扩展。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为 NA 治疗在降低慢性 HBV 感染个体特定亚组的肝硬化和 HCC 风险方面的潜在益处提供了证据,这可能为未来的 治疗 指南和 监测 策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究是观察性的,尽管进行了倾向评分匹配分析,但仍可能受到残留混杂因素的影响。由于观察数据缺乏随机性和潜在偏差,证据强度中等。

EASL 2025 indications revisited: phase-specific outcomes with and without nucleos(t)ide analogue therapy in chronic hepatitis B virus infection.

Authors: Shichuan Tang, Tingfeng Huang, Ruijing Tang, Kongying Lin, Cong Luo, Yubing Shen, Kailing Zhang, Yidan Tang, Jie Kong, Zhenwei Chen
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVE: The impact of nucleos(t)ide analogues (NAs) therapy on the long-term outcomes in chronic HBV infection individuals outside 2025 European Association for the Study of the Liver (EASL) strongly recommended treatment indications remains uncertain. We aimed to assess the association between NAs therapy and the risks of cirrhosis and hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) in Chinese chronic HBV infection individuals outside these criteria. DESIGN: We analysed data from 30 784 chronic HBV infection individuals across 12 centres in China. We categorised individuals outside 2025 EASL strongly recommended treatment criteria into four groups: (1) hepatitis B e antigen (HBeAg)-positive, high replicative (age <30, HBV DNA ≥8 log10IU/mL, normal alanine aminotransferase (ALT), no/mild fibrosis, no family history of liver cancer); (2) HBeAg-positive, impending phase transition (typically age ≥30, HBV DNA ≥6 log10IU/mL, normal ALT, no advanced fibrosis); (3) HBeAg-negative, low replicative (HBV DNA <3.3 log10IU/mL, normal ALT, no/mild fibrosis); (4) HBeAg-negative, high replicative, low-risk (HBV DNA 3.3-4.3 log10IU/mL, normal ALT, no/mild fibrosis). The primary endpoints were the incidence of cirrhosis and HCC. RESULTS: Up to 5 years of follow-up (117 814 person-years), we documented 635 incident cirrhosis and 164 HCC cases. In multivariable analyses, NA therapy significantly reduced risks of cirrhosis (HR 0.18, 95% CI 0.11 to 0.32) and HCC (HR 0.03, 95% CI 0.01 to 0.21) in Group 1, and cirrhosis (HR 0.50, 95% CI 0.41 to 0.61) and HCC (HR 0.25, 95% CI 0.16 to 0.41) in Group 2. No significant associations were observed in Groups 3 and 4. Findings were consistent in propensity score matching analyses. CONCLUSION: NAs therapy was associated with reduced risks of cirrhosis and HCC in HBeAg-positive individuals with high replicative or impending phase transition phenotypes, supporting the expansion of chronic HBV infection treatment criteria.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess the association between nucleos(t)ide analogues (NAs) therapy and the risks of cirrhosis and hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) in Chinese chronic HBV infection individuals outside 2025 EASL strongly recommended treatment criteria. The impact of NAs therapy on long-term outcomes in chronic HBV infection individuals outside these criteria remains uncertain.
Design and population
We analysed data from 30 784 chronic HBV infection individuals across 12 centres in China. The primary endpoints were the incidence of cirrhosis and HCC. We categorised individuals outside 2025 EASL strongly recommended treatment criteria into four groups based on their HBV DNA levels, ALT status, fibrosis stage, and HBeAg status.
Core methods
We conducted multivariable analyses and propensity score matching analyses to evaluate the association between NA therapy and the risks of cirrhosis and HCC. The study included a large cohort of individuals with chronic HBV infection and followed them for up to 5 years.
Main results
Up to 5 years of follow-up (117 814 person-years), we documented 635 incident cirrhosis and 164 HCC cases. In multivariable analyses, NA therapy significantly reduced risks of cirrhosis (HR 0.18, 95% CI 0.11 to 0.32) and HCC (HR 0.03, 95% CI 0.01 to 0.21) in Group 1, and cirrhosis (HR 0.50, 95% CI 0.41 to 0.61) and HCC (HR 0.25, 95% CI 0.16 to 0.41) in Group 2. No significant associations were observed in Groups 3 and 4. Findings were consistent in propensity score matching analyses.
Interpretation and novelty
NAs therapy was associated with reduced risks of cirrhosis and HCC in HBeAg-positive individuals with high replicative or impending phase transition phenotypes, supporting the expansion of chronic HBV infection treatment criteria.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide evidence for the potential benefits of NA therapy in reducing the risks of cirrhosis and HCC in specific subgroups of chronic HBV infection individuals, which may inform future treatment guidelines and surveillance strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is observational and may be subject to residual confounding despite propensity score matching analyses. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the lack of randomization and potential biases in observational data.

慢性乙型肝炎 治疗下 慢性乙型肝炎 患者的胰岛素抵抗和肝细胞癌发展。

Insulin Resistance and Hepatocellular Carcinoma Development in Chronic Hepatitis B Patients Under Antiviral Therapy.
作者： Dong Hyun Sinn, Kyung-Ah Kim, Jung Il Lee, Geum-Youn Gwak
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
肝细胞癌 (HCC) 仍然是 慢性乙型肝炎 病毒 (HBV) 感染导致的肝硬化患者的一个重要问题,即使是那些通过 慢性乙型肝炎 治疗获得病毒学应答的患者也是如此。
设计与对象
本研究旨在评估使用胰岛素抵抗稳态模型评估 2 (HOMA2-IR) 指数评估的胰岛素抵抗 (IR) 是否可以作为该人群中 HCC 风险分层的替代标记。
核心方法
在这项多中心前瞻性 队列研究 研究中,纳入了 334 名对核苷(酸)类似物治疗取得病毒学应答的 HBV 相关肝硬化患者。主要结局是随访期间 HCC 的 发病率。使用血清 C 肽和空腹血糖水平计算基线 HOMA2-IR。
主要结果
中位随访时间为7.45年(范围0.5-8.9年),62例患者(18.6%)发展为HCC。在完全调整的Cox比例风险模型中,HOMA2-IR每增加1个单位,与HCC风险增加有关(风险比[HR]为1.53;95%置信区间[CI]为1.16-2.04)。与HOMA2-IR < 2.3的患者相比,HOMA2-IR ≥ 2.3的患者HCC的5年累积发病率显著更高(30.6% vs. 12.3%;调整后HR为2.62;95% CI为1.40-4.91)。
解释与创新
HOMA2-IR 测量的 IR 可独立预测慢性 HBV 感染的肝硬化患者的 HCC 发展。HOMA2-IR 与 HCC 风险之间的关联在各个亚组中是一致的,包括那些患有或不患有肥胖、糖尿病、高血压或血脂异常等代谢合并症的亚组。
科研与公卫意义
HOMA2-IR 可作为识别高危个体的有用替代标记。有必要进行干预试验来确定降低 HOMA2-IR 是否可以降低该人群的 HCC 风险。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告该研究的局限性。

Insulin Resistance and Hepatocellular Carcinoma Development in Chronic Hepatitis B Patients Under Antiviral Therapy.

Authors: Dong Hyun Sinn, Kyung-Ah Kim, Jung Il Lee, Geum-Youn Gwak
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) remains a significant concern in patients with liver cirrhosis due to chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection, even among those who achieve a virological response with antiviral therapy. This study aimed to evaluate whether insulin resistance (IR), assessed using the Homeostatic Model Assessment 2 for Insulin Resistance (HOMA2-IR) index, can serve as a surrogate marker for stratifying HCC risk in this population. METHODS: In this multicenter prospective cohort study, 334 patients with HBV-related cirrhosis who achieved virological response to nucleos(t)ide analogue therapy were enrolled. The primary outcome was the incidence of HCC during follow-up. Baseline HOMA2-IR was calculated using serum C-peptide and fasting glucose levels. RESULTS: During a median follow-up of 7.45 years (range, 0.5-8.9 years), 62 patients (18.6%) developed HCC. In a fully adjusted Cox proportional hazards model, each unit increase in HOMA2-IR was associated with a higher risk of HCC (hazard ratio [HR], 1.53; 95% confidence interval [CI], 1.16-2.04). Patients with HOMA2-IR ≥ 2.3 had a significantly higher 5-year cumulative incidence of HCC compared to those with HOMA2-IR < 2.3 (30.6% vs. 12.3%; adjusted HR, 2.62; 95% CI, 1.40-4.91). The association between HOMA2-IR and HCC risk was consistent across subgroups, including those with and without metabolic comorbidities such as obesity, diabetes, hypertension, or dyslipidemia. CONCLUSION: IR, as measured by HOMA2-IR, independently predicted HCC development in cirrhotic patients with chronic HBV infection. HOMA2-IR may serve as a useful surrogate marker for identifying high-risk individuals. Intervention trials are warranted to determine whether reducing HOMA2-IR can lower HCC risk in this population.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) remains a significant concern in patients with liver cirrhosis due to chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection, even among those who achieve a virological response with antiviral therapy.
Design and population
This study aimed to evaluate whether insulin resistance (IR), assessed using the Homeostatic Model Assessment 2 for Insulin Resistance (HOMA2-IR) index, can serve as a surrogate marker for stratifying HCC risk in this population.
Core methods
In this multicenter prospective cohort study, 334 patients with HBV-related cirrhosis who achieved virological response to nucleos(t)ide analogue therapy were enrolled. The primary outcome was the incidence of HCC during follow-up. Baseline HOMA2-IR was calculated using serum C-peptide and fasting glucose levels.
Main results
During a median follow-up of 7.45 years (range, 0.5-8.9 years), 62 patients (18.6%) developed HCC. In a fully adjusted Cox proportional hazards model, each unit increase in HOMA2-IR was associated with a higher risk of HCC (hazard ratio [HR], 1.53; 95% confidence interval [CI], 1.16-2.04). Patients with HOMA2-IR ≥ 2.3 had a significantly higher 5-year cumulative incidence of HCC compared to those with HOMA2-IR < 2.3 (30.6% vs. 12.3%; adjusted HR, 2.62; 95% CI, 1.40-4.91).
Interpretation and novelty
IR, as measured by HOMA2-IR, independently predicted HCC development in cirrhotic patients with chronic HBV infection. The association between HOMA2-IR and HCC risk was consistent across subgroups, including those with and without metabolic comorbidities such as obesity, diabetes, hypertension, or dyslipidemia.
Scientific and public-health significance
HOMA2-IR may serve as a useful surrogate marker for identifying high-risk individuals. Intervention trials are warranted to determine whether reducing HOMA2-IR can lower HCC risk in this population.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations of the study.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

血清流行率 和韩国 慢性乙型肝炎、肝硬化和肝细胞癌患者丁型肝炎病毒感染的临床特征

Seroprevalence and Clinical Characteristics of Hepatitis D Virus Infection in Korean Patients with Chronic Hepatitis B, Liver Cirrhosis, and Hepatocellular Carcinoma
作者： Min Kyung Park, Yun-Sook Lim, Eun Ju Cho, Jea-Yong Park, Yun Bin Lee, Jeong-Hoon Lee, Su Jong Yu, Jung-Hwan Yoon, Mi Kyung Lee, Anna D. Lee
摘要中文翻译背景/目的:丁型肝炎病毒 (HDV) 合并感染会加剧 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 患者的临床结果。随着针对 HDV 的新兴疗法的出现,准确的 患病率 和临床影响数据至关重要。在这项研究中,对韩国各种肝病患者的 HDV 血清流行率、临床意义和分子特征进行了评估。方法:这项两中心回顾性研究纳入了 526 名 CHB 患者:非肝硬化 CHB (n=100)、肝硬化 CHB(肝硬化,n=100)和肝细胞癌 (HCC;n=326)。使用商业化验和定量逆转录聚合酶链反应分析血清样本(2012 年至 2024 年)的抗丁型肝炎病毒抗体和丁型肝炎病毒 RNA,然后进行全长基因组测序。结果:患者中位年龄为 58.0 岁,71.3% 的患者为男性。总体抗 HDV 血清流行率 为 2.7% (14/526)。患病率 在不同疾病组中没有显着差异:非肝硬化 CHB 中为 2.0%,肝硬化 CHB 中为 4.0%,HCC 中为 2.5% (p=0.733)。值得注意的是,在 HCC 患者中,晚期阶段(巴塞罗那临床肝癌 [BCLC] C-D)的抗 HDV 血清流行率 显着高于极早期至中期阶段 (BCLC 0-B)(5.3% vs 0.9%;p=0.024)。仅在一名患者中检测到 HDV RNA(占 队列研究 总数的 0.2%;抗 HDV 阳性患者的 7.1%)。系统发育分析对韩国第一个全长 HDV 基因型 1 基因组 进行了表征,该病毒株与东亚毒株聚类。结论:韩国 CHB 患者中抗 HDV 血清流行率 为 2.7%,而病毒血症 患病率 为 0.2%。抗 HDV 阳性与男性和晚期 HCC 阶段有关。这些发现强调了对晚期肝病患者进行丁型肝炎病毒筛查的必要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估韩国 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB)、肝硬化和肝细胞癌 (HCC) 患者中丁型肝炎病毒 (HDV) 的 血清流行率 和临床意义,为新兴 HDV 靶向治疗的使用提供信息。
设计与对象
这项两中心回顾性研究纳入了 526 名 CHB 患者,分为非肝硬化 CHB (n=100)、肝硬化 CHB (n=100) 和 HCC (n=326)。使用商业化验和定量逆转录聚合酶链反应对 2012 年至 2024 年的血清样本进行抗 HDV 抗体和 HDV RNA 分析。
核心方法
使用商业化验和定量逆转录聚合酶链反应分析血清样本中的抗 HDV 抗体和 HDV RNA,然后进行全长基因组测序。
主要结果
总体抗 HDV 血清流行率 为 2.7% (14/526)。患病率 在不同疾病组之间没有显着差异,但抗 HDV 血清流行率 在晚期 HCC 阶段 (BCLC C-D) 中显着高于极早期至中期阶段 (BCLC 0-B)(5.3% vs 0.9%;p=0.024)。仅在一名患者中检测到 HDV RNA(占 队列研究 总数的 0.2%;抗 HDV 阳性患者的 7.1%)。
解释与创新
韩国 CHB 患者中抗 HDV 血清流行率 为 2.7%,而病毒血症 患病率 为 0.2%。抗 HDV 阳性与男性和晚期 HCC 分期有关,这强调了晚期肝病患者进行 HDV 筛查的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现强调了 HDV 筛查对于晚期肝病患者(尤其是 HCC 患者)的重要性,并支持 HDV 靶向疗法的开发。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和样本量小而受到限制,这可能会影响研究结果的普遍性。HDV的总体血清流行率较低,病毒血症患病率甚至更低,这可能限制了结果的临床意义。

Seroprevalence and Clinical Characteristics of Hepatitis D Virus Infection in Korean Patients with Chronic Hepatitis B, Liver Cirrhosis, and Hepatocellular Carcinoma

Authors: Min Kyung Park, Yun-Sook Lim, Eun Ju Cho, Jea-Yong Park, Yun Bin Lee, Jeong-Hoon Lee, Su Jong Yu, Jung-Hwan Yoon, Mi Kyung Lee, Anna D. Lee
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND/AIMS: Hepatitis delta virus (HDV) coinfection exacerbates clinical outcomes in chronic hepatitis B (CHB) patients. With emerging HDV-targeted therapeutics, accurate prevalence and clinical impact data are essential. In this study, the seroprevalence, clinical significance, and molecular characteristics of HDV were assessed in South Korean patients across the spectrum of liver disease. METHODS: This two-center retrospective study included 526 patients with CHB: non-cirrhotic CHB (n=100), cirrhotic CHB (liver cirrhosis, n=100), and hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC; n=326). Serum samples (2012 to 2024) were analyzed for anti-HDV antibodies and HDV RNA using commercial assays and quantitative reverse-transcription polymerase chain reaction, followed by full-length genomic sequencing. RESULTS: The median patient age was 58.0 years, and 71.3% of the patients were male. The overall anti-HDV seroprevalence was 2.7% (14/526). Prevalence did not differ significantly across disease groups: 2.0% in non-cirrhotic CHB, 4.0% in cirrhotic CHB, and 2.5% in HCC (p=0.733). Notably, in HCC patients, anti-HDV seroprevalence was significantly higher in advanced stages (Barcelona Clinic Liver Cancer [BCLC] C-D) than in very early-to-intermediate stages (BCLC 0-B) (5.3% vs 0.9%; p=0.024). HDV RNA was detected in only one patient (0.2% of the total cohort; 7.1% of anti-HDV-positive patients). Phylogenetic analysis characterized the first full-length HDV genotype 1 genome in South Korea, which clustered with East Asian strains. CONCLUSIONS: Anti-HDV seroprevalence in Korean CHB patients is 2.7%, whereas viremic prevalence is 0.2%. Anti-HDV positivity is linked to male sex and advanced HCC stage. These findings emphasize the need for HDV screening in patients with advanced liver disease.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess the seroprevalence and clinical significance of hepatitis delta virus (HDV) in Korean patients with chronic hepatitis B (CHB), liver cirrhosis, and hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) to inform the use of emerging HDV-targeted therapeutics.
Design and population
This two-center retrospective study included 526 patients with CHB, stratified into non-cirrhotic CHB (n=100), cirrhotic CHB (n=100), and HCC (n=326). Serum samples from 2012 to 2024 were analyzed for anti-HDV antibodies and HDV RNA using commercial assays and quantitative reverse-transcription polymerase chain reaction.
Core methods
Serum samples were analyzed for anti-HDV antibodies and HDV RNA using commercial assays and quantitative reverse-transcription polymerase chain reaction, followed by full-length genomic sequencing.
Main results
The overall anti-HDV seroprevalence was 2.7% (14/526). Prevalence did not differ significantly across disease groups, but anti-HDV seroprevalence was significantly higher in advanced HCC stages (BCLC C-D) than in very early-to-intermediate stages (BCLC 0-B) (5.3% vs 0.9%; p=0.024). HDV RNA was detected in only one patient (0.2% of the total cohort; 7.1% of anti-HDV-positive patients).
Interpretation and novelty
Anti-HDV seroprevalence in Korean CHB patients is 2.7%, whereas viremic prevalence is 0.2%. Anti-HDV positivity is linked to male sex and advanced HCC stage, emphasizing the need for HDV screening in patients with advanced liver disease.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings highlight the importance of HDV screening in patients with advanced liver disease, particularly in those with HCC, and support the development of HDV-targeted therapeutics.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and the small sample size, which may affect the generalizability of the findings. The overall seroprevalence of HDV is low, and viremic prevalence is even lower, which may limit the clinical significance of the results.

非洲 慢性乙型肝炎 患者的肝脂肪变性:患病率、相关性和临床意义。

Hepatic Steatosis in People With Chronic Hepatitis B in Africa: Prevalence, Correlates, and Clinical Significance.
作者： Freeman W. Chabala, Edford Sinkala, Alana T. Brennan, Amy Miskimon Goss, Lamin Bojang, Yusupha Samateh, Alexander J. Stockdale, Roger Sombie, Arouna Sessouma, Alice Nanelin Guingane
摘要中文翻译摘要:为了解决非洲代谢性肝病的数据差距,在非洲乙型肝炎合作网络 (HEPSANET) 内,我们使用具有受控衰减参数 (CAP) 的瞬时弹性成像技术评估了 慢性乙型肝炎 病毒感染 (CHB) 参与者的脂肪变性肝病 (SLD)。使用多变量逻辑回归,我们确定了社会人口统计学、人体测量学和临床与 SLD (CAP ≥ 248 dB/m) 的关联,并评估了 SLD 严重程度是否与纤维化独立相关。还通过 Cox 比例风险分析评估了 SLD 对 死亡率 的短期影响。我们还评估了 B 型超声对 SLD 的诊断性能,并以 CAP 作为参考。在 6 个国家的 1601 名参与者(年龄范围,12-80 岁;39.5% 为女性;4% 患有糖尿病;11% 不健康饮酒;中位体重指数,24.4)中,421 名(26.3%)患有 SLD;219 人 (13.7%) 患有严重脂肪变性 (CAP ≥ 280 dB/m)。年龄较大、男性、超重和肥胖以及 HIV 阴性状态与 SLD 相关。调整年龄、性别、HIV 状况、HBV 治疗 和国家后,重度脂肪变性与纤维化概率增加 8.4%(95%、1.9%–14.9%)相关,但轻度和中度形式(即 248-279 dB/m)则不然。经过有限的随访(中位时间 2.69 年)和少量死亡,SLD 与全因或肝脏相关的 死亡率 无关。B 型超声对总体或严重的 SLD 的敏感性较低,但特异性为 95%。总之,SLD 在非洲慢性乙型肝炎患者中很常见,其严重形式与肝纤维化有关。这些数据支持将代谢合并症筛查作为非洲 HBV 护理和 治疗 的一部分。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
为了解决非洲代谢性肝病的数据差距,本研究使用具有受控衰减参数 (CAP) 的瞬时弹性成像技术评估了 慢性乙型肝炎 病毒感染参与者的脂肪变性肝病。
设计与对象
该研究利用多变量逻辑回归,确定了社会人口统计学、人体测量学和临床与脂肪变性肝病 (SLD) 的关联,并评估了 SLD 严重程度是否与纤维化独立相关。在 6 个国家的 1601 名参与者(年龄范围,12-80 岁;39.5% 为女性;4% 患有糖尿病;11% 不健康饮酒;中位体重指数,24.4)中,421 名(26.3%)患有 SLD;219 人 (13.7%) 患有严重脂肪变性 (CAP ≥ 280 dB/m)。
核心方法
使用 Cox 比例风险分析评估 SLD 对 死亡率 的短期影响。以CAP为参考,评估B超对SLD的诊断性能。
主要结果
年龄较大、男性、超重和肥胖以及 HIV 阴性状态与 SLD 相关。调整年龄、性别、HIV 状况、HBV 治疗 和国家后,重度脂肪变性(而非轻度和中度形式(即 248-279 dB/m))与纤维化概率增加 8.4%(95%、1.9%-14.9%)相关。
解释与创新
该研究得出的结论是,SLD 在非洲 慢性乙型肝炎 患者中很常见,其严重形式与肝纤维化有关。
科研与公卫意义
这些数据支持将代谢合并症筛查作为非洲 HBV 护理和 治疗 的一部分。
局限与证据强度
经过有限的随访(中位时间 2.69 年)和少量死亡,SLD 与全因或肝脏相关的 死亡率 无关。由于随访时间有限且观察到的事件数量较少,证据强度中等。

Hepatic Steatosis in People With Chronic Hepatitis B in Africa: Prevalence, Correlates, and Clinical Significance.

Authors: Freeman W. Chabala, Edford Sinkala, Alana T. Brennan, Amy Miskimon Goss, Lamin Bojang, Yusupha Samateh, Alexander J. Stockdale, Roger Sombie, Arouna Sessouma, Alice Nanelin Guingane
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT To address data gaps around metabolic liver disease in Africa, within the Hepatitis B in Africa Collaborative Network (HEPSANET), we evaluated participants with chronic hepatitis B virus infection (CHB) for steatotic liver disease (SLD) using transient elastography with controlled attenuation parameter (CAP). Using multivariable logistic regression, we identified sociodemographic, anthropometric, and clinical associations with SLD (CAP ≥ 248 dB/m), and evaluated whether SLD severity was independently associated with fibrosis. Short‐term impacts of SLD on mortality were also assessed with Cox proportional hazards analysis. We also evaluated the diagnostic performance of B‐mode ultrasound for SLD, with CAP as reference. Across 6 countries and 1601 participants (age range, 12–80 years; 39.5% female; 4% with diabetes; 11% unhealthy alcohol use; median body mass index, 24.4), 421 (26.3%) had SLD; 219 (13.7%) had severe steatosis (CAP ≥ 280 dB/m). Older age, male sex, overweight and obesity, and HIV‐negative status were associated with SLD. After adjusting for age, sex, HIV status, HBV treatment, and country, severe steatosis, but not mild and moderate forms (i.e., 248–279 dB/m), was associated with a 8.4% (95%, 1.9%–14.9%) increased probability of fibrosis. Over limited follow‐up (median 2.69 years) and small number of deaths, SLD was not associated with all‐cause or liver‐related mortality. B‐mode ultrasound had low sensitivity for SLD, overall or when severe, but specificity was 95%. In conclusion, SLD was common in people with CHB in Africa and its severe form was associated with liver fibrosis. These data support screening for metabolic comorbidities as part of HBV care and treatment in Africa.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
To address data gaps around metabolic liver disease in Africa, this study evaluated participants with chronic hepatitis B virus infection for steatotic liver disease using transient elastography with controlled attenuation parameter (CAP).
Design and population
Using multivariable logistic regression, the study identified sociodemographic, anthropometric, and clinical associations with steatotic liver disease (SLD) and evaluated whether SLD severity was independently associated with fibrosis. Across 6 countries and 1601 participants (age range, 12–80 years; 39.5% female; 4% with diabetes; 11% unhealthy alcohol use; median body mass index, 24.4), 421 (26.3%) had SLD; 219 (13.7%) had severe steatosis (CAP ≥ 280 dB/m).
Core methods
Short-term impacts of SLD on mortality were assessed with Cox proportional hazards analysis. The diagnostic performance of B-mode ultrasound for SLD was evaluated, with CAP as reference.
Main results
Older age, male sex, overweight and obesity, and HIV-negative status were associated with SLD. After adjusting for age, sex, HIV status, HBV treatment, and country, severe steatosis, but not mild and moderate forms (i.e., 248–279 dB/m), was associated with an 8.4% (95%, 1.9%–14.9%) increased probability of fibrosis.
Interpretation and novelty
The study concludes that SLD was common in people with chronic hepatitis B in Africa and its severe form was associated with liver fibrosis.
Scientific and public-health significance
These data support screening for metabolic comorbidities as part of HBV care and treatment in Africa.
Limitations and evidence strength
Over limited follow-up (median 2.69 years) and a small number of deaths, SLD was not associated with all-cause or liver-related mortality. The evidence strength is moderate due to the limitations in follow-up duration and the small number of events observed.

慢性乙型肝炎 成人的体力活动和 死亡率 风险以及肝脏相关事件:一项全国范围的 队列研究 研究。

Becoming Physically Active and Risks of Mortality and Liver-Related Events in Adults With Chronic Hepatitis B: A Nationwide Cohort Study.
作者： Byeong Geun Song, Myeongcheol Lee, Juhee Cho, Geum‐Youn Gwak, Danbee Kang, Dong Hyun Sinn
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
体力活动 (PA) 具有多种健康益处;然而,在缺乏身体活动的 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 成人中增加 PA 是否与较低的不良临床结果风险相关尚不清楚。
设计与对象
我们纳入了 318,131 名慢性乙型肝炎成人患者,他们在初次健康筛查(检查 1)时身体缺乏活动,并在 4 年内完成了后续筛查(检查 2)。
核心方法
我们使用了全国性的韩国国民健康保险数据库。
主要结果
MVPA initiation was associated with lower all‐cause mortality (hazard ratio [HR], 0.89; 95% CI, 0.84–0.94), a lower risk of liver‐related events (subdistribution HR [subHR], 0.94; 95% CI, 0.92–0.97), and, more modestly, of HCC (subHR, 0.95; 95% CI, 0.91–0.99) compared with those remaining inactive.
解释与创新
这种关联在老年人、女性、从不吸烟者、不饮酒者和未经治疗的参与者中更为明显(所有相互作用的 p ≤ 0.03)。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现提供了大规模的流行病学证据,支持进一步评估缺乏运动的慢性乙型肝炎患者的 PA 促进作用。
局限与证据强度
在中位数7.9年的随访期间(四分位数间距,5.2–9.5),发生了7609例死亡。

Becoming Physically Active and Risks of Mortality and Liver-Related Events in Adults With Chronic Hepatitis B: A Nationwide Cohort Study.

Authors: Byeong Geun Song, Myeongcheol Lee, Juhee Cho, Geum‐Youn Gwak, Danbee Kang, Dong Hyun Sinn
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Physical activity (PA) confers several health benefits; however, whether increasing PA among physically inactive adults with chronic hepatitis B (CHB) is associated with lower risks of adverse clinical outcomes remains unclear. We used the nationwide Korean National Health Insurance database. We included 318,131 adults with CHB who were physically inactive at the initial health screening (Exam 1) and completed a subsequent screening within 4 years (Exam 2). Among them, 58,703 initiated moderate‐to‐vigorous PA (MVPA), defined as vigorous activity ≥ 20 min/day on ≥ 3 days/week or moderate activity ≥ 30 min/day on ≥ 5 days/week. The primary outcome was all‐cause mortality, and the secondary outcomes were liver‐related events (hepatic decompensation, liver transplantation, hepatocellular carcinoma [HCC]) and HCC. Multivariable Cox and Fine–Grey competing‐risk models were adjusted for demographic, clinical, and behavioural factors. During a median 7.9‐year follow‐up (IQR, 5.2–9.5), 7609 deaths occurred. MVPA initiation was associated with lower all‐cause mortality (hazard ratio [HR], 0.89; 95% CI, 0.84–0.94), a lower risk of liver‐related events (subdistribution HR [subHR], 0.94; 95% CI, 0.92–0.97), and, more modestly, of HCC (subHR, 0.95; 95% CI, 0.91–0.99) compared with those remaining inactive. The association was more pronounced in older adults, women, never smokers, non‐drinkers, and untreated participants (all p for interaction ≤ 0.03). Initiating MVPA among physically inactive adults with CHB was associated with lower risks of mortality and liver‐related outcomes. These findings provide large‐scale epidemiological evidence supporting further evaluation of PA promotion in physically inactive patients with CHB.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Physical activity (PA) confers several health benefits; however, whether increasing PA among physically inactive adults with chronic hepatitis B (CHB) is associated with lower risks of adverse clinical outcomes remains unclear.
Design and population
We included 318,131 adults with CHB who were physically inactive at the initial health screening (Exam 1) and completed a subsequent screening within 4 years (Exam 2).
Core methods
We used the nationwide Korean National Health Insurance database.
Main results
MVPA initiation was associated with lower all‐cause mortality (hazard ratio [HR], 0.89; 95% CI, 0.84–0.94), a lower risk of liver‐related events (subdistribution HR [subHR], 0.94; 95% CI, 0.92–0.97), and, more modestly, of HCC (subHR, 0.95; 95% CI, 0.91–0.99) compared with those remaining inactive.
Interpretation and novelty
The association was more pronounced in older adults, women, never smokers, non‐drinkers, and untreated participants (all p for interaction ≤ 0.03).
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings provide large‐scale epidemiological evidence supporting further evaluation of PA promotion in physically inactive patients with CHB.
Limitations and evidence strength

在没有乙型肝炎的情况下发生丁型肝炎的进一步证据

Further evidence of hepatitis D occurring in the absence of hepatitis B
作者： Satu Kurkela, H. Jarva, L. Szirovicza, J. Hepojoki
摘要中文翻译丁型肝炎病毒 (HDV) 的 传播 需要辅助病毒,乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 是既定的伴侣。然而,最近的证据表明,丁型肝炎病毒和其他科尔米奥病毒可能利用更广泛的辅助病毒,从而增加了不明疾病关联的可能性。我们从代表 1996 名患者的 2121 份血清/血浆样本中筛选了抗 HDV 抗体(抗 HDV),这些样本选自 2021 年至 2022 年送往芬兰赫尔辛基 HUS 诊断中心病毒学和免疫学实验室进行各种传染病和自身免疫性疾病常规诊断测试的样本。这导致 20/1996 (1.0%) 的患者检测到抗 HDV,其中 17 例 HBV 表面抗原 (HBsAg) 和 HBV DNA 均为阴性。这17例患者中,16例HDV RNA检测呈阴性,1例患者HDV RNA阳性。HDV RNA阳性患者被确认为HBV核心总抗体阴性,但抗HCV抗体和HCV RNA阳性。该血清调查表明丁型肝炎病毒感染也可能发生在乙型肝炎病毒携带者之外。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查丁型肝炎病毒(HDV)感染是否可以独立于乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)而发生,挑战了HDV需要HBV作为辅助病毒的既定观点。
设计与对象
使用来自 1996 名患者的 2121 份血清/血浆样本进行了血清调查,这些样本是在 2021 年至 2022 年期间在芬兰赫尔辛基 HUS 诊断中心的病毒学和免疫学实验室收集的,用于各种传染病和自身免疫性疾病的常规诊断测试。
核心方法
从标本中筛选抗HDV抗体,并对17例HBV表面抗原(HBsAg)和HBV DNA均为阴性的患者进行HDV RNA检测。
主要结果
1.0% 的患者检测到抗 HDV (20/1996),其中 17 名患者的 HBV 表面抗原 (HBsAg) 和 HBV DNA 呈阴性。在这 17 例中,16 例 HDV RNA 呈阴性,1 例 HDV RNA 呈阳性。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,丁型肝炎病毒感染可能在没有乙型肝炎病毒存在的情况下发生,这表明丁型肝炎病毒有可能感染没有乙型肝炎病毒合并感染的个体,这是一项新的观察结果。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可能有助于制定更全面的 监测 和 HDV 诊断策略,特别是在不存在 HBV 合并感染的人群中。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其依赖于常规诊断样本,这可能会引入选择偏差,而且 HDV RNA 阳性病例数量较少,导致研究结果的可信度中等。

Further evidence of hepatitis D occurring in the absence of hepatitis B

Authors: Satu Kurkela, H. Jarva, L. Szirovicza, J. Hepojoki
Original abstract (English)
The transmission of hepatitis D virus (HDV) requires a helper virus, hepatitis B virus (HBV) being the established companion. However, recent evidence suggests that HDV and other kolmioviruses might utilize a broader range of helper viruses, raising the possibility of unidentified disease associations. We screened anti-HDV antibodies (anti-HDV) from 2121 serum/plasma specimens, representing 1996 patients, selected from samples sent for routine diagnostic testing of various infectious and autoimmune diseases to the Virology and Immunology laboratory at HUS Diagnostic Center, Helsinki, Finland in 2021 -2022. This led to detection of anti-HDV in 20/1996 (1.0%) of patients, of which 17 were negative for both HBV surface antigen (HBsAg) and HBV DNA. Of these 17 patients, 16 were negative in HDV RNA detection, and 1 patient was HDV RNA positive. The HDV RNA positive patient was confirmed to be negative for HBV core total antibody, but positive for anti-HCV antibodies and HCV RNA. This serosurvey suggests HDV infections may occur outside HBV carriers.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate whether hepatitis D virus (HDV) infections can occur independently of hepatitis B virus (HBV), challenging the established view that HDV requires HBV as a helper virus.
Design and population
A serosurvey was conducted using 2121 serum/plasma specimens from 1996 patients, collected between 2021 and 2022 at the Virology and Immunology laboratory at HUS Diagnostic Center, Helsinki, Finland, for routine diagnostic testing of various infectious and autoimmune diseases.
Core methods
Anti-HDV antibodies were screened from the specimens, and HDV RNA detection was performed on 17 patients who were negative for both HBV surface antigen (HBsAg) and HBV DNA.
Main results
Anti-HDV was detected in 1.0% of patients (20/1996), with 17 of these patients being negative for HBV surface antigen (HBsAg) and HBV DNA. Of these 17, 16 were negative for HDV RNA, and 1 was positive for HDV RNA.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that HDV infections may occur in the absence of HBV, indicating a potential for HDV to infect individuals without HBV co-infection, which is a novel observation.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results may inform the development of more comprehensive surveillance and diagnostic strategies for HDV, particularly in populations where HBV co-infection is not present.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on routine diagnostic samples, which may introduce selection bias, and the small number of HDV RNA-positive cases, resulting in moderate confidence in the findings.

通过机器学习实现针对慢性乙型肝炎相关肝纤维化的个性化预测

Realizing personalized prediction for the reversal of chronic hepatitis B-associated liver fibrosis through machine learning
作者： Shi-Cai Liu, Zi-Wen Wang, Han Zhang
摘要中文翻译尽管长期抗病毒药物治疗可以逆转慢性乙型肝炎患者的肝纤维化,但个体对治疗的反应差异显著,目前仍缺乏可靠的预测方法。传统的临床工具如肝硬度测量和天门冬氨酸氨基转移酶与血小板比值指数最初设计用于横断面分期,而非纵向预测,其捕捉纤维化逆转动态过程的能力有限。在本观点综述中,我们讨论了机器学习(ML)如何帮助解决这一未满足的需求。我们重点关注了机器学习在三个方面的早期但有希望的结果:利用机器学习进行图像的定量分析、通过多模态模型整合病理和临床数据,以及基于抗病毒药物治疗期间的连续测量动态预测治疗反应。我们认为,机器学习的最大价值并不在于取代现有评估工具,而在于补充它们,特别是在早期识别那些仅通过抗病毒药物治疗难以实现纤维化逆转的患者方面。然而,我们也提醒,目前大多数模型都是基于小规模、单中心的回顾性队列研究开发的,其临床应用需要前瞻性多中心验证、提高模型的可解释性以及遵循标准化的报告框架。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决缺乏可靠方法来预测 慢性乙型肝炎 患者个体 治疗 反应的问题,因为长期 抗病毒药物 治疗可以逆转某些个体的肝纤维化,但不能逆转其他个体的肝纤维化。
设计与对象
本意见综述讨论了机器学习在预测 治疗 对 慢性乙型肝炎 反应中的应用,重点关注 ML 补充现有临床工具的潜力。
核心方法
作者回顾了机器学习在三个领域的应用:定量图像分析、通过多模态模型整合病理和临床数据,以及基于 治疗 治疗期间的连续测量动态预测 治疗 反应。
主要结果
该综述强调了机器学习应用在预测 慢性乙型肝炎 纤维化逆转方面的早期但令人鼓舞的结果,特别是通过临床和病理数据的整合。
解释与创新
作者解释说,机器学习可以增强用于评估 慢性乙型肝炎 中 治疗 响应的现有工具,提供一种新颖的个性化预测方法,而无需取代当前方法。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,机器学习可以提高预测哪些 慢性乙型肝炎 患者可能实现纤维化逆转的精确度,有助于制定更有针对性的 治疗 策略。
局限与证据强度
证据有限,因为大多数当前模型都是基于小型、单中心回顾性 队列研究,这可能会影响其普遍性并需要进一步的前瞻性验证。

Realizing personalized prediction for the reversal of chronic hepatitis B-associated liver fibrosis through machine learning

Authors: Shi-Cai Liu, Zi-Wen Wang, Han Zhang
Original abstract (English)
Although long-term antiviral therapy can reverse liver fibrosis in a considerable proportion of patients with chronic hepatitis B, treatment response differs markedly among individuals, and reliable methods for predicting who will benefit remain lacking. Conventional clinical tools such as liver stiffness measurement and the aspartate aminotransferase-to-platelet ratio index were originally dewsigned for cross-sectional staging rather than longitudinal prediction, and their ability to capture the dynamic process of fibrosis reversal is limited. In this opinion review, we discuss how machine learning (ML) may help address this unmet need. We focus on three areas where ML has shown early but encouraging results: Quantitative analysis of images using ML, integration of pathological and clinical data through multimodal models, and dynamic prediction of treatment response based on serial measurements during antiviral therapy. In our view, the greatest value of ML lies not in replacing existing assessment tools but in complementing them, particularly through the early identification of patients who are unlikely to achieve fibrosis reversal with antiviral therapy alone. However, we also caution that most current models have been developed from small, single-center retrospective cohorts, and their clinical adoption will require prospective multi-center validation, improved model interpretability, and adherence to standardized reporting frameworks.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the lack of reliable methods for predicting individual treatment response in chronic hepatitis B patients, as long-term antiviral therapy can reverse liver fibrosis in some individuals but not others.
Design and population
This opinion review discusses the application of machine learning in predicting treatment response for chronic hepatitis B, focusing on the potential of ML to complement existing clinical tools.
Core methods
The authors review the use of machine learning in three areas: quantitative image analysis, integration of pathological and clinical data through multimodal models, and dynamic prediction of treatment response based on serial measurements during antiviral therapy.
Main results
The review highlights early but encouraging results from machine learning applications in predicting fibrosis reversal in chronic hepatitis B, particularly through the integration of clinical and pathological data.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that machine learning can enhance existing tools for assessing treatment response in chronic hepatitis B, offering a novel approach to personalized prediction without replacing current methods.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that machine learning could improve the precision of predicting which patients with chronic hepatitis B are likely to achieve fibrosis reversal, aiding in more tailored treatment strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited by the fact that most current models are based on small, single-center retrospective cohorts, which may affect their generalizability and require further prospective validation.

在接受核苷(酸)治疗的慢性 HBV 感染中,贝匹罗韦生与安慰剂相比,在 72 周时增加了 治疗 的无 功能性治愈

In chronic HBV infection receiving nucleos(t)ide therapy, bepirovirsen vs. placebo increased treatment-free functional cure at 72 wk
作者： Sana A. Pirzada, Averell H. Sherker, ACP Journal Club Editorial Team at McMaster University
摘要中文翻译临床影响评级:GIM/FP/GP:[公式:参见文本] 胃肠病学:[公式:参见文本] 传染病:[公式:参见文本]。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估贝匹罗韦森与安慰剂相比在慢性 HBV 感染患者中实现无 治疗 的效果。
设计与对象
这项随机、双盲、安慰剂对照试验是在接受核苷(酸)治疗的 慢性乙型肝炎 病毒感染患者中进行的。
核心方法
该研究涉及给参与者服用贝匹罗韦森或安慰剂,并通过病毒学和生化标记物评估 72 周时的 功能性治愈 状态。
主要结果
72 周时,与安慰剂组相比,贝匹罗韦森组中达到无 治疗 的患者比例更高。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,当与核苷(酸)疗法联合使用时,贝匹罗韦森可能在慢性 HBV 感染中实现 功能性治愈 方面具有显着优势。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可以为未来针对慢性 HBV 感染的 治疗 策略提供信息,有可能改善长期结果并减少持续治疗的需要。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对病毒学和生化标志物的依赖而受到限制,并且由于缺乏长期随访数据,证据不充分。

In chronic HBV infection receiving nucleos(t)ide therapy, bepirovirsen vs. placebo increased treatment-free functional cure at 72 wk

Authors: Sana A. Pirzada, Averell H. Sherker, ACP Journal Club Editorial Team at McMaster University
Original abstract (English)
CLINICAL IMPACT RATINGS: GIM/FP/GP: [Formula: see text] Gastroenterology: [Formula: see text] Infectious Disease: [Formula: see text].
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the efficacy of bepirovirsen compared to placebo in achieving a treatment-free functional cure in patients with chronic HBV infection.
Design and population
This randomized, double-blind, placebo-controlled trial was conducted in patients with chronic hepatitis B virus infection who were receiving nucleos(t)ide therapy.
Core methods
The study involved administering bepirovirsen or placebo to participants and assessing the functional cure status at 72 weeks through virological and biochemical markers.
Main results
At 72 weeks, a higher proportion of patients in the bepirovirsen group achieved a treatment-free functional cure compared to the placebo group.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that bepirovirsen may offer a significant advantage in achieving a functional cure in chronic HBV infection when used in conjunction with nucleos(t)ide therapy.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results could inform future treatment strategies for chronic HBV infection, potentially improving long-term outcomes and reducing the need for continuous therapy.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on virological and biochemical markers, and the evidence is moderate due to the absence of long-term follow-up data.

在纳米比亚温得和克东门诊所接受实验室检测的医护人员中,患病率 和与乙型肝炎表面抗体血清保护相关的因素:一项回顾性横断面研究

Prevalence and factors associated with hepatitis B surface antibody seroprotection among healthcare workers undergoing laboratory testing at Eastgate Clinic, Windhoek, Namibia: A retrospective cross-sectional study
作者： Talent Farai Mafumhe, Vonai Charamba, Hamufare Dumisani Mugauri, Onyemaechi Okpara Azu
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
医护人员因职业原因接触血液和体液,需要有效防护以防止 乙型肝炎病毒 感染。关于纳米比亚医护人员乙型肝炎表面抗体(抗 HBs)血清保护的证据仍然有限。
设计与对象
我们估计了抗 HBs 血清保护的 患病率,并检查了在纳米比亚温得和克 Eastgate Clinic 接受测试的医护人员中的相关因素。包括对每个人的第一次时间顺序测试。该登记册包含代表 299 个独特个体的 304 条测试记录。
核心方法
我们对2025年1月1日至12月31日常规收集的抗-HBs检测记录进行了横断面分析。抗-HBs浓度≥10 mIU/mL被归类为血清保护性。超出检测报告范围的结果被视为已审查,但仍可对二元结果进行分类。
主要结果
总体而言,148 名个体得到了血清保护,对应的 患病率 为 50.0%(95% CI 44.3–55.7%)。18-29 岁参与者的血清保护最低(35.3%),50-59 岁参与者最高(71.1%)。
解释与创新
Each 10-year increase in age was associated with a higher prevalence of seroprotection after adjustment for sex (aPR 1.14, 95% CI 1.06–1.24; p=0.001), whereas sex was not independently associated with seroprotection (aPR 0.97, 95% CI 0.78–1.22; p=0.797). The age association remained consistent across sensitivity analyses.
科研与公卫意义
接受测试的一半医护人员的抗 HBs 浓度低于操作血清保护阈值。这些发现支持系统的 疫苗接种 验证、适当定时的 疫苗接种 后测试以及记录的非保护性结果的后续行动。
局限与证据强度
缺乏 疫苗接种 病史、职业特征、检测指征和其他乙型肝炎血清学标志物限制了因果和临床解释。患病率 估计值采用 Wilson 95% 置信区间 (CI) 报告。

Prevalence and factors associated with hepatitis B surface antibody seroprotection among healthcare workers undergoing laboratory testing at Eastgate Clinic, Windhoek, Namibia: A retrospective cross-sectional study

Authors: Talent Farai Mafumhe, Vonai Charamba, Hamufare Dumisani Mugauri, Onyemaechi Okpara Azu
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Healthcare workers are occupationally exposed to blood and body fluids and require effective protection against hepatitis B virus infection. Evidence on hepatitis B surface antibody (anti-HBs) seroprotection among healthcare workers in Namibia remains limited. We estimated the prevalence of anti-HBs seroprotection and examined associated factors among healthcare workers tested at Eastgate Clinic in Windhoek, Namibia. We conducted a cross-sectional analysis of routinely collected anti-HBs test records from 1 January to 31 December 2025. The first chronological test per individual was included. Anti-HBs concentrations ≥10 mIU/mL were classified as seroprotective. Results outside the assay reporting range were treated as censored but remained classifiable for the binary outcome. Prevalence estimates were reported with Wilson 95% confidence intervals (CIs). Modified Poisson regression with robust standard errors was used to estimate adjusted prevalence ratios (aPRs). The register contained 304 test records representing 299 unique individuals. After excluding three individuals with missing anti-HBs results, 296 were analysed; 163 (55.1%) were female. Overall, 148 individuals were seroprotected, corresponding to a prevalence of 50.0% (95% CI 44.3–55.7%). Seroprotection was lowest among participants aged 18– 29 years (35.3%) and highest among those aged 50–59 years (71.1%). Each 10-year increase in age was associated with a higher prevalence of seroprotection after adjustment for sex (aPR 1.14, 95% CI 1.06–1.24; p=0.001), whereas sex was not independently associated with seroprotection (aPR 0.97, 95% CI 0.78–1.22; p=0.797). The age association remained consistent across sensitivity analyses. Half of the healthcare workers tested had anti-HBs concentrations below the operational seroprotection threshold. These findings support systematic vaccination verification, appropriately timed post-vaccination testing, and documented follow-up of non-protective results. The absence of vaccination history, occupational characteristics, testing indications, and additional hepatitis B serological markers limited causal and clinical interpretation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Healthcare workers are occupationally exposed to blood and body fluids and require effective protection against hepatitis B virus infection. Evidence on hepatitis B surface antibody (anti-HBs) seroprotection among healthcare workers in Namibia remains limited.
Design and population
We estimated the prevalence of anti-HBs seroprotection and examined associated factors among healthcare workers tested at Eastgate Clinic in Windhoek, Namibia. The first chronological test per individual was included. The register contained 304 test records representing 299 unique individuals.
Core methods
We conducted a cross-sectional analysis of routinely collected anti-HBs test records from 1 January to 31 December 2025. Anti-HBs concentrations ≥10 mIU/mL were classified as seroprotective. Results outside the assay reporting range were treated as censored but remained classifiable for the binary outcome.
Main results
Overall, 148 individuals were seroprotected, corresponding to a prevalence of 50.0% (95% CI 44.3–55.7%). Seroprotection was lowest among participants aged 18–29 years (35.3%) and highest among those aged 50–59 years (71.1%).
Interpretation and novelty
Each 10-year increase in age was associated with a higher prevalence of seroprotection after adjustment for sex (aPR 1.14, 95% CI 1.06–1.24; p=0.001), whereas sex was not independently associated with seroprotection (aPR 0.97, 95% CI 0.78–1.22; p=0.797). The age association remained consistent across sensitivity analyses.
Scientific and public-health significance
Half of the healthcare workers tested had anti-HBs concentrations below the operational seroprotection threshold. These findings support systematic vaccination verification, appropriately timed post-vaccination testing, and documented follow-up of non-protective results.
Limitations and evidence strength
The absence of vaccination history, occupational characteristics, testing indications, and additional hepatitis B serological markers limited causal and clinical interpretation. Prevalence estimates were reported with Wilson 95% confidence intervals (CIs).

谷丙转氨酶水平正常的 发病率 患者的早期 发病率 治疗和肝细胞癌 发病率:一项前瞻性 II 期研究

Early Antiviral Therapy and Hepatocellular Carcinoma Incidence in Chronic Hepatitis B Patients with Normal Alanine Aminotransferase Levels: A Prospective Phase II Study
作者： Dong Hyun Sinn, Ji Hoon Kim, Hyung Joon Kim, Joon Hyeok Lee, Jae Young Jang, Kyunga Kim, Joonghyun Ahn, Yoon Jun Kim
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查对于丙氨酸转氨酶 (ALT) 水平正常的 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 患者是否有必要使用核苷(酸)类似物进行 抗病毒药物 治疗,因为 D 2 AS 评分是识别肝细胞癌 (HCC) 高风险患者的有效模型,尽管 ALT 水平正常或轻​​度升高。
设计与对象
这是一项前瞻性、多中心 II 期试验,纳入 115 名 ALT <80 U/L 且 D 2 AS 评分≥2.0 的非肝硬化 CHB 患者,接受仿制药恩替卡韦(每日 0.5 mg)治疗 5 年。
核心方法
该研究采用前瞻性设计,规定了为期 5 年的通用恩替卡韦(每天 0.5 毫克)干预方案,主要终点是标准化 发病率 比率 (SIR),根据 D 2 AS 评分计算为观察到的预期 HCC 病例与预期 HCC 病例的比率。
主要结果
观察到的HCC发病率总体上并未显著低于预期,但在第5年观察到下降趋势,标准化的HCC发病率(SIR)为0.53(95%置信区间,0.24至1.20)。6名患者发展为HCC,其累计发病率分别为1年时2.0%、3年时5.1%和5年时6.1%。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解释为显示立即 发病率 治疗的 HCC 发病率 低于预期的趋势,表明潜在的临床益处值得进一步的对照研究。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能为未来关于 ALT 水平正常的 CHB 患者早期 抗病毒药物 治疗的临床益处的研究提供信息,并可能影响针对该人群的 治疗 指南。
局限与证据强度
该研究是探索性的,观察到的 HCC 发病率 总体上并未显着低于预期,这限制了证据的强度。由于缺乏明确的结果,信心评估为中等。

Early Antiviral Therapy and Hepatocellular Carcinoma Incidence in Chronic Hepatitis B Patients with Normal Alanine Aminotransferase Levels: A Prospective Phase II Study

Authors: Dong Hyun Sinn, Ji Hoon Kim, Hyung Joon Kim, Joon Hyeok Lee, Jae Young Jang, Kyunga Kim, Joonghyun Ahn, Yoon Jun Kim
Original abstract (English)
Background/Aims: Whether antiviral therapy with nucleos(t)ide analogs is necessary for chronic hepatitis B (CHB) patients with normal alanine aminotransferase (ALT) levels remains controversial.The D 2 AS score is a validated model for identifying patients at high risk of hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) despite normal or mildly elevated ALT levels.We explored whether the observed HCC incidence with immediate antiviral therapy differed from that predicted by the D 2 AS model. Methods:In this prospective, multicenter phase II trial, 115 non-cirrhotic CHB patients with ALT <80 U/L and a D 2 AS score ≥2.0 received generic entecavir (0.5 mg daily) for 5 years.The primary endpoint was the standardized incidence ratio (SIR), defined as the ratio of the observed to the expected number of HCC cases based on the D 2 AS score.Results: The virological response rates were 79.4%, 90.2%, and 92.4% at 1, 3, and 5 years, respectively.The median ALT concentration decreased from 37 U/L at baseline to 23, 20, and 19 U/L at 1, 3, and 5 years, respectively.Six patients developed HCC, with cumulative incidences of 2.0%, 5.1%, and 6.1% at 1, 3, and 5 years, respectively.Although the observed HCC incidence was not significantly lower than expected overall, a decreasing trend was noted at year 5 (SIR, 0.53; 95% confidence interval, 0.24 to 1.20).Conclusions: Immediate antiviral therapy achieved sustained viral suppression and ALT reduction and showed a trend toward a lower-than-expected HCC incidence.Although exploratory, these findings provide a rationale for conducting future controlled studies to evaluate potential clinical benefits of early antiviral therapy in this population.(Gut Liver, Published online October 7, 2026)
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate whether antiviral therapy with nucleos(t)ide analogs is necessary for chronic hepatitis B (CHB) patients with normal alanine aminotransferase (ALT) levels, as the D 2 AS score is a validated model for identifying patients at high risk of hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) despite normal or mildly elevated ALT levels.
Design and population
This was a prospective, multicenter phase II trial involving 115 non-cirrhotic CHB patients with ALT <80 U/L and a D 2 AS score ≥2.0, who received generic entecavir (0.5 mg daily) for 5 years.
Core methods
The study used a prospective design with a defined intervention protocol of generic entecavir (0.5 mg daily) over 5 years, and the primary endpoint was the standardized incidence ratio (SIR), calculated as the ratio of observed to expected HCC cases based on the D 2 AS score.
Main results
The observed HCC incidence was not significantly lower than expected overall, but a decreasing trend was noted at year 5 with a standardized incidence ratio (SIR) of 0.53 (95% confidence interval, 0.24 to 1.20). Six patients developed HCC, with cumulative incidences of 2.0%, 5.1%, and 6.1% at 1, 3, and 5 years, respectively.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as showing a trend toward lower-than-expected HCC incidence with immediate antiviral therapy, suggesting a potential clinical benefit that warrants further controlled studies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform future studies on the clinical benefits of early antiviral therapy in CHB patients with normal ALT levels, potentially impacting treatment guidelines for this population.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is exploratory, and the observed HCC incidence was not significantly lower than expected overall, which limits the strength of the evidence. The confidence assessment is moderate due to the lack of definitive results.

慢性乙型肝炎 中将层粘连蛋白 Gamma 2 单体添加到 PAGE-B 评分中对肝细胞癌 监测 的临床效用

The Clinical Utility of Adding Laminin Gamma 2 Monomer to the PAGE‐B Score for Hepatocellular Carcinoma Surveillance in Chronic Hepatitis B
作者： Chung‐Feng Huang, Po‐Cheng Liang, Michael Stec, Mark Anderson, Gavin Cloherty, Pei‐Chien Tsai, Tzu‐Chun Lin, Yu‐Min Ko, Yi‐Shan Tsai, Kuan‐Yu Chen
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景与目的 尽管在慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)患者中使用核苷(酸)类似物(NA)治疗,肝细胞癌(HCC)的残留风险仍存在。我们旨在通过整合PAGE‐B评分,评估基线血清层粘连蛋白γ2单体(LG2m)在预测HCC中的作用。方法 收集了接受NA治疗的CHB患者的基线血清LG2m、甲胎蛋白(AFP)和PAGE‐B评分数据。我们评估了LG2m在按肝硬化状态和PAGE‐B评分分层的亚组中的预测价值,并提出了一个HCC 监测算法。结果 在随访3651人年期间,1016名患者中有56人发展为HCC(年发病率:1.53%)。在低PAGE‐B评分患者中,5年内未见HCC发生。在高PAGE‐B评分患者中,LG2m未能显著分层HCC风险。相反,在非高PAGE‐B评分患者中,LG2m显著预测HCC发生(5年发病率:5.5% vs. 低LG2m,p = 0.004)。将LG2m、AFP和高PAGE‐B评分整合后,HCC检测的敏感度达到98.2%。在非高PAGE‐B评分患者中,升高的LG2m与更高的HCC风险相关(调整后风险比,4.40;95%置信区间,1.26–15.44),而AFP在单独分析中未能显著分层HCC风险。对于具有中等PAGE‐B评分且高LG2m的患者,应像高PAGE‐B评分患者一样进行更密切的HCC 监测监测(年发病率:1.4%),而高PAGE‐B评分患者的年发病率为1.9%。结论 血清LG2m水平在具有中等PAGE‐B评分的患者中可特异性预测HCC。将LG2m整合到以PAGE‐B为导向的算法中,可提供一种基于生物标志物的个体化监测工具,这对于提高早期HCC检测至关重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估在 PAGE-B 评分中添加层粘连蛋白 γ 2 单体 (LG2m) 用于预测 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 患者肝细胞癌 (HCC) 的临床效用。尽管在 CHB 患者中使用核苷(酸)类似物 (NA),但 HCC 的残余风险仍然存在。
设计与对象
该研究涉及接受 NA 治疗的 CHB 患者,测量了其基线血清 LG2m、甲胎蛋白 (AFP) 和 PAGE-B 评分。在 3651 人年的随访中,1016 名患者中有 56 名发生 HCC(年度 发病率:1.53%)。低 PAGE-B 患者中没有一名患者在 5 年内发展为 HCC。
核心方法
这些方法包括测量 NA 治疗的 CHB 患者的基线血清 LG2m、AFP 和 PAGE-B 评分。LG2m 的预测价值在按肝硬化状态和 PAGE-B 评分分层的亚组中进行评估。
主要结果
在非高PAGE-B评分患者中,升高的LG2m与更高的HCC风险相关(调整后风险比,4.40;95%置信区间,1.26–15.44),而AFP在单独分析中未能显著分层HCC风险。
解释与创新
作者解释说,血清 LG2m 水平可以预测 HCC,特别是在 PAGE-B 评分中等的患者中。将 LG2m 集成到面向 PAGE-B 的算法中,为个性化 监测 提供了生物标志物驱动的工具,这对于改善早期 HCC 检测至关重要。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,将 LG2m 纳入 HCC 监测 策略可以增强早期检测,特别是对于 PAGE-B 评分中等的患者。这可以为慢性乙型肝炎患者提供更有针对性和更有效的监测方案。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对基线测量的依赖而受到限制,并且没有考虑 LG2m 水平的长期变化。证据是中等的,因为它是基于没有随机设计的观察性研究。

The Clinical Utility of Adding Laminin Gamma 2 Monomer to the PAGE‐B Score for Hepatocellular Carcinoma Surveillance in Chronic Hepatitis B

Authors: Chung‐Feng Huang, Po‐Cheng Liang, Michael Stec, Mark Anderson, Gavin Cloherty, Pei‐Chien Tsai, Tzu‐Chun Lin, Yu‐Min Ko, Yi‐Shan Tsai, Kuan‐Yu Chen
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Background and Aims A residual risk of hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) persists despite the use of nucleos(t)ide analogs (NA) in chronic hepatitis B (CHB) patients. We aimed to assess the utility of baseline serum laminin gamma 2 monomer (LG2m) by integrating PAGE‐B score to predict HCC. Methods NA‐treated CHB patients whose baseline serum LG2m, alpha‐fetoprotein (AFP) and PAGE‐B score were measured. We evaluated the predictive value of LG2m in subgroups stratified by cirrhosis status and PAGE‐B score and proposed a HCC surveillance algorithm. Results HCC developed in 56 of 1016 patients over a follow‐up of 3651 person‐years (annual incidence: 1.53%). None of the low PAGE‐B patients developed HCC over 5 years. LG2m did not significantly stratify risk in high PAGE‐B patients. Conversely, LG2m was significantly predictive of HCC in nonhigh PAGE‐B patients (5‐year incidence: 5.5% vs. low LG2m, p = 0.004). Integrating LG2m, AFP, and high PAGE‐B achieved a sensitivity of 98.2% for HCC detection. Among patients with a nonhigh PAGE‐B score, elevated LG2m was associated with a higher risk of HCC (adjusted HR, 4.40; 95% CI, 1.26–15.44), whereas AFP did not significantly stratify HCC risk in a separate analysis. Intermediate PAGE‐B patients with high LG2m should receive closer HCC surveillance (annual incidence: 1.4%) as with high PAGE‐B patients did (annual incidence: 1.9%). Conclusion Serum LG2m level was predictive of HCC specifically among patients with intermediate PAGE‐B score. Integration of LG2m into a PAGE‐B‐oriented algorithm provides a biomarker‐driven tool for individualized surveillance, which is vital for improving early‐stage HCC detection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess the clinical utility of adding laminin gamma 2 monomer (LG2m) to the PAGE‐B score for predicting hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) in chronic hepatitis B (CHB) patients. A residual risk of HCC persists despite the use of nucleos(t)ide analogs (NA) in CHB patients.
Design and population
The study involved NA-treated CHB patients whose baseline serum LG2m, alpha-fetoprotein (AFP), and PAGE-B score were measured. HCC developed in 56 of 1016 patients over a follow-up of 3651 person-years (annual incidence: 1.53%). None of the low PAGE-B patients developed HCC over 5 years.
Core methods
The methods included measuring baseline serum LG2m, AFP, and PAGE-B score in NA-treated CHB patients. The predictive value of LG2m was evaluated in subgroups stratified by cirrhosis status and PAGE-B score.
Main results
Among patients with a nonhigh PAGE-B score, elevated LG2m was associated with a higher risk of HCC (adjusted HR, 4.40; 95% CI, 1.26–15.44), whereas AFP did not significantly stratify HCC risk in a separate analysis.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that serum LG2m level was predictive of HCC specifically among patients with intermediate PAGE-B score. Integration of LG2m into a PAGE-B-oriented algorithm provides a biomarker-driven tool for individualized surveillance, which is vital for improving early-stage HCC detection.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that incorporating LG2m into HCC surveillance strategies could enhance early detection, particularly in patients with intermediate PAGE-B scores. This could inform more targeted and effective monitoring protocols for CHB patients.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on baseline measurements and does not account for long-term changes in LG2m levels. The evidence is moderate as it is based on an observational study without randomized design.

半定量超声评分揭示慢性乙型肝炎(慢性乙型肝炎)在接受替诺福韦艾拉酚胺治疗期间肝脏结构恢复和免疫调节

Semi-quantitative ultrasound scoring reveals hepatic structural recovery and immune modulation during tenofovir alafenamide therapy in chronic hepatitis B
作者： Wen-Juan Li, Li-Hui Yang, Juan He, Wenjuan Li, Lihui Yang
摘要中文翻译本研究旨在利用半定量超声(US)评分系统评估替诺福韦艾拉酚胺二甲基磺酸盐(TAF)在慢性乙型肝炎(慢性乙型肝炎)患者中的治疗反应,并探讨超声影像变化与免疫功能恢复之间的相关性。方法 入选了100例接受TAF单药治疗(25 mg/天)至少24个月的慢性乙型肝炎患者进行回顾性分析。在基线、12个月和24个月时计算了包含肝脏回声、包膜轮廓、边缘形态、血管清晰度、门静脉直径(PVD)、脾脏厚度(ST)和脾静脉血流速度(SVV)的七参数半定量超声评分。同时测量了血清乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)脱氧核糖核酸(DNA)、丙氨酸氨基转移酶(ALT)、天门冬氨酸氨基转移酶(AST)以及外周T细胞亚群,包括分化簇3阳性(CD3+)、CD4+、CD8+ T细胞、CD4+/CD8+比值和自然杀伤(NK)细胞活性。分析了超声评分与生化和免疫参数之间的相关性。结果 在24个月时,所有患者均达到HBV DNA <20 IU/mL,ALT和AST的正常化率分别为92%和95%。综合超声评分从基线的7.86 ± 1.73显著下降至24个月的3.37 ± 0.95(P < 0.001),其中包膜平滑度和实质均匀性的改善最为显著。CD4+ T细胞比例和CD4+/CD8+比值增加,而CD8+ T细胞比例下降;NK细胞活性从42.7 ± 7.3%上升至52.4 ± 6.2%(P < 0.05)。超声评分与ALT(r = 0.56)和AST(r = 0.47)呈正相关,与CD4+/CD8+比值(r = −0.46)和NK细胞活性(r = −0.38)呈负相关(所有P < 0.01)。结论 这些发现表明,TAF不仅抑制病毒复制并使肝酶正常化,还促进可测量的肝脏结构改善,这体现在半定量超声评分的下降。超声评分与生化和免疫参数之间的强相关性支持其作为非侵入性影像生物标志物用于监测治疗疗效和肝脏修复的潜力。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在利用半定量超声(US)评分系统评估替诺福韦艾拉酚胺二甲基磺酸盐(TAF)在慢性乙型肝炎(慢性乙型肝炎)患者中的治疗反应,并探讨超声影像变化与免疫功能恢复之间的相关性。
设计与对象
回顾性纳入 100 名接受 TAF 单药治疗(25 毫克/天)至少 24 个月的慢性乙型肝炎患者。
核心方法
在基线、12 个月和 24 个月时计算七参数半定量 US 评分,包括肝脏回声性、包膜轮廓、边缘形状、血管清晰度、门静脉直径 (PVD)、脾厚度 (ST) 和脾静脉速度 (SVV)。同时,测量血清 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 脱氧核糖核酸 (DNA)、丙氨酸转氨酶 (ALT)、天冬氨酸转氨酶 (AST)、外周 T 细胞亚群,包括分化簇 3 阳性 (CD3 + )、CD4 + 、CD8 + T 细胞、CD4 + / CD8 + 比率和自然杀伤 (NK) 细胞活性。
主要结果
在24个月时,所有患者均达到HBV DNA <20 IU/mL,ALT和AST的正常化率分别为92%和95%。综合超声评分从基线的7.86 ± 1.73显著下降至24个月的3.37 ± 0.95(P < 0.001),其中包膜平滑度和实质均匀性的改善最为显著。
解释与创新
分析了美国评分与生化/免疫参数之间的相关性。US评分与ALT(r = 0.56)和AST(r = 0.47)呈正相关,与CD4 + /CD8 + 比值(r = -0.46)和NK细胞活性(r = -0.38)呈负相关(均P < 0.01)。
科研与公卫意义
超声评分与生化和免疫参数之间的强相关性支持其作为非侵入性影像生物标志物用于监测治疗疗效和肝脏修复的潜力。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告局限性。

Semi-quantitative ultrasound scoring reveals hepatic structural recovery and immune modulation during tenofovir alafenamide therapy in chronic hepatitis B

Authors: Wen-Juan Li, Li-Hui Yang, Juan He, Wenjuan Li, Lihui Yang
Original abstract (English)
Objective This study aimed to evaluate the therapeutic response to tenofovir alafenamide fumarate (TAF) in chronic hepatitis B (CHB) using a semi-quantitative ultrasound (US) scoring system, and to investigate the correlation between sonographic changes and immune function restoration. Methods A total of 100 CHB patients who received TAF monotherapy (25 mg/day) for at least 24 months were retrospectively enrolled. A seven-parameter semi-quantitative US score, encompassing liver echogenicity, capsular contour, margin shape, vessel clarity, portal vein diameter (PVD), spleen thickness (ST), and splenic vein velocity (SVV), was calculated at baseline, 12 months, and 24 months. Concurrently, serum hepatitis B virus (HBV) deoxyribonucleic acid (DNA), alanine aminotransferase (ALT), aspartate aminotransferase (AST), peripheral T-cell subsets, including cluster of differentiation 3-positive (CD3 + ), CD4 + , CD8 + T cells, CD4+/CD8+ ratio, and natural killer (NK) cell activity were measured. Correlations between US scores and biochemical/immune parameters were analyzed. Results At 24 months, all patients achieved HBV DNA <20 IU/mL, with ALT and AST normalization rates of 92% and 95%, respectively. The comprehensive US score decreased significantly from 7.86 ± 1.73 at baseline to 3.37 ± 0.95 at 24 months ( P < 0.001), with the most pronounced improvements observed in capsular smoothness and parenchymal homogeneity. The CD4 + T-cell proportion and CD4 + /CD8 + ratio increased, whereas the CD8 + proportion decreased; NK cell activity rose from 42.7 ± 7.3% to 52.4 ± 6.2% ( P < 0.05). The US score was positively correlated with ALT (r = 0.56) and AST (r = 0.47), and negatively correlated with the CD4 + /CD8 + ratio (r = −0.46) and NK cell activity (r = −0.38) (all P < 0.01). Conclusion These findings indicate that TAF not only suppresses viral replication and normalises liver enzymes but also promotes measurable structural liver improvement, as reflected by declining semi-quantitative US scores. The strong correlations of US scores with biochemical and immune parameters support its utility as a non-invasive imaging biomarker for monitoring treatment efficacy and hepatic repair.

Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate the therapeutic response to tenofovir alafenamide fumarate (TAF) in chronic hepatitis B (CHB) using a semi-quantitative ultrasound (US) scoring system, and to investigate the correlation between sonographic changes and immune function restoration.

At 24 months, all patients achieved HBV DNA <20 IU/mL, with ALT and AST normalization rates of 92% and 95%, respectively. The comprehensive US score decreased significantly from 7.86 ± 1.73 at baseline to 3.37 ± 0.95 at 24 months (P < 0.001), with the most pronounced improvements observed in capsular smoothness and parenchymal homogeneity.

The strong correlations of US scores with biochemical and immune parameters support its utility as a non-invasive imaging biomarker for monitoring treatment efficacy and hepatic repair.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations.

HIV 感染者遵守欧洲艾滋病临床学会乙型肝炎管理指南:来自西班牙和德国的真实世界证据。

Adherence to European AIDS clinical society hepatitis B management guidelines in people with HIV: real-world evidence from Spain and Germany.
作者： Juan Berenguer, Pablo Ryan, Thomas Lutz, Luz Martín-Carbonero, Leire Pérez-Latorre, María J Vivancos, Jordi Navarro, Roser Navarro-Soler, Oscar L Ferrero, Rosario Palacios
摘要中文翻译目的:评估 HIV 感染者 (PWH) 和慢性 HBV 感染者对 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 管理建议的遵守情况。设计:一项多中心横断面研究。方法:我们于 2024 年 6 月至 2025 年 3 月期间在西班牙和德国的 42 个中心进行了一项横断面研究。慢性 HBV 感染的定义是至少两次相隔 6 个月以上的乙型肝炎表面抗原检测呈阳性。对 2023 年欧洲艾滋病临床协会关于抗 HBV 治疗、肝酶监测、HBV-DNA 检测、抗丁型肝炎病毒 (HDV) 筛查和 HDV-RNA 确认的建议的遵守情况进行了评估。肝细胞癌 监测 单独进行了评估,并已在其他地方报道过。结果:共纳入 1235 名慢性 HBV 感染者。中位年龄为 56 岁,84% 为男性,36% 出生于欧洲以外地区。总体而言,88% 的 HIV-RNA 低于 50 拷贝/ml,CD4+ 细胞计数中位数为 623 个细胞/μl。在可获得 HBV-DNA 结果的参与者中,87% 的 HBV-DNA 检测不到。88% 的个体记录了指南推荐的抗 HBV 治疗。在过去 12 个月中,94% 的人进行了肝酶监测,71% 的人进行了 HBV-DNA 检测。总体而言,81% 的人曾经接受过抗 HDV 检测,而在抗 HDV 阳性个体中,66% 的人接受过 HDV-RNA 检测。结论:尽管对 HBV 护理的多个组成部分的依从性很高,但在实施当前的感染者卫生保健建议方面仍存在差距,特别是在 HBV-DNA 监测和 HDV 评估方面。将这些措施更好地融入日常艾滋病毒护理中可能会改善肝脏相关并发症的长期预防。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估 HIV 感染者 (PWH) 和慢性 HBV 感染者对 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 管理建议的遵守情况,满足了解当前指南在临床实践中实施情况的需要。
设计与对象
2024年6月至2025年3月期间,在西班牙和德国的42个中心进行了一项多中心横断面研究,共纳入1235名慢性乙型肝炎病毒感染者。
核心方法
慢性乙型肝炎病毒感染被定义为至少两次乙型肝炎表面抗原检测呈阳性,且时间间隔超过 6 个月。对 2023 年欧洲艾滋病临床协会关于抗 HBV 治疗、肝酶监测、HBV-DNA 检测、抗丁型肝炎病毒 (HDV) 筛查和 HDV-RNA 确认的建议的遵守情况进行了评估。
主要结果
在可获得 HBV-DNA 结果的参与者中,87% 的 HBV-DNA 检测不到。总体而言,81% 的人曾经接受过抗 HDV 检测,而在抗 HDV 阳性个体中,66% 的人接受过 HDV-RNA 检测。
解释与创新
作者解释说,尽管对 HBV 护理的多个组成部分的遵守率很高,但在执行目前的感染者卫生保健建议方面仍存在差距,特别是在 HBV-DNA 监测和 HDV 评估方面。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,将这些措施更好地融入日常艾滋病毒护理中可能会改善肝脏相关并发症的长期预防。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到横截面设计的限制,不允许进行因果推断,并且数据可能无法完全推广到其他人群。

Adherence to European AIDS clinical society hepatitis B management guidelines in people with HIV: real-world evidence from Spain and Germany.

Authors: Juan Berenguer, Pablo Ryan, Thomas Lutz, Luz Martín-Carbonero, Leire Pérez-Latorre, María J Vivancos, Jordi Navarro, Roser Navarro-Soler, Oscar L Ferrero, Rosario Palacios
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVE: To evaluate adherence to hepatitis B virus (HBV) management recommendations among people with HIV (PWH) and chronic HBV infection. DESIGN: A multicenter cross-sectional study. METHODS: We conducted a cross-sectional study in 42 centers in Spain and Germany between June 2024 and March 2025. Chronic HBV infection was defined as at least two positive hepatitis B surface antigen tests obtained more than 6 months apart. Adherence to 2023 European AIDS Clinical Society recommendations regarding anti-HBV treatment, liver enzyme monitoring, HBV-DNA testing, antihepatitis D virus (HDV) screening, and HDV-RNA confirmation was assessed. Hepatocellular carcinoma surveillance was evaluated separately and has been reported elsewhere. RESULTS: A total of 1235 PWH with chronic HBV infection were included. The median age was 56 years, 84% were men, and 36% were born outside Europe. Overall, 88% had HIV-RNA less than 50 copies/ml, and the median CD4+ cell count was 623 cells/μl. Among participants with available HBV-DNA result, 87% had undetectable HBV-DNA. Guideline-recommended anti-HBV treatment was documented in 88% of individuals. During the previous 12 months, liver enzyme monitoring was performed in 94% and HBV-DNA testing in 71%. Overall, 81% had ever undergone anti-HDV testing and, among anti-HDV-positive individuals, 66% had undergone HDV-RNA testing. CONCLUSION: Although adherence to several components of HBV care was high, gaps remain in the implementation of current recommendations for PWH, particularly in HBV-DNA monitoring and HDV evaluation. Better integration of these measures into routine HIV care may improve long-term prevention of liver-related complications.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate adherence to hepatitis B virus (HBV) management recommendations among people with HIV (PWH) and chronic HBV infection, addressing the need to understand how well current guidelines are implemented in clinical practice.
Design and population
A multicenter cross-sectional study was conducted in 42 centers in Spain and Germany between June 2024 and March 2025, including a total of 1235 PWH with chronic HBV infection.
Core methods
Chronic HBV infection was defined as at least two positive hepatitis B surface antigen tests obtained more than 6 months apart. Adherence to 2023 European AIDS Clinical Society recommendations regarding anti-HBV treatment, liver enzyme monitoring, HBV-DNA testing, antihepatitis D virus (HDV) screening, and HDV-RNA confirmation was assessed.
Main results
Among participants with available HBV-DNA result, 87% had undetectable HBV-DNA. Overall, 81% had ever undergone anti-HDV testing and, among anti-HDV-positive individuals, 66% had undergone HDV-RNA testing.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that although adherence to several components of HBV care was high, gaps remain in the implementation of current recommendations for PWH, particularly in HBV-DNA monitoring and HDV evaluation.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that better integration of these measures into routine HIV care may improve long-term prevention of liver-related complications.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design, which does not allow for causal inference, and the data may not be fully generalizable to other populations.

保加利亚索非亚吸毒者的艾滋病毒、乙型肝炎、丙型肝炎和梅毒反应标记:一项横断面生物行为调查

Reactive markers of HIV, hepatitis B, hepatitis C and syphilis among people who use drugs in Sofia, Bulgaria: a cross-sectional bio-behavioural survey
作者： Vanya Rangelova, Kostadin Rangelov Kostadinov, Yuliya Georgieva, Kristina Zhelyazkova, Elmira Nesheva, Ani Kevorkyan, Аni Kevorkyan
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 在东欧和中亚,注射吸毒者中的 HIV 发病率 持续上升,但保加利亚的当代生物行为数据却很少。我们测量了索非亚吸毒者中艾滋病毒、乙型肝炎、丙型肝炎和梅毒的反应性筛查标记物 患病率,并检查了它们的相关性。方法 对 2024 年 6 月至 8 月通过流动外展招募的 480 名上月报告非法药物使用的成年人进行横断面调查。通过快速检测对毛细血管血进行现场筛查。由于结果很常见,因此关联性表示为来自具有鲁棒方差的修正泊松回归的调整后的 患病率 比率,协变量是从有向非循环图中选择的,而不是通过显着性筛选。结果 480 名参与者(78.8% 为男性,中位年龄 40 岁)中,320 名参与者(66.7%,95% 置信区间 62.3–70.9)至少有一种反应性标志物。305 例 (63.5%,59.1–67.9) 出现抗 HCV 反应,61 例 (12.7%,9.9–16.0) 出现抗 HIV 反应,15 例 (3.1%,1.8–5.1) 出现密螺旋体抗体反应,11 例 (2.3%,1.1–4.1) 出现 HBsAg 反应。反应性集中在曾经注射过的 304 名参与者中(抗 HCV 为 89.1%,未注射过的参与者为 19.3%;抗 HIV 为 17.1%,未注射过 5.1%),而密螺旋体抗体和 HBsAg 并没有因注射史而存在差异。抗 HCV 反应性从 20-29 岁时的 25.0% 上升至 40-49 岁时的 82.0%(趋势 p 检验)
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决保加利亚索非亚吸毒者中艾滋病毒、乙型肝炎、丙型肝炎和梅毒当代生物行为数据的缺乏问题,因为东欧和中亚的艾滋病毒感染人数不断上升。
设计与对象
2024 年 6 月至 8 月在保加利亚索非亚对 480 名上个月报告非法药物使用的成年人进行了一项横断面调查。
核心方法
使用快速测试对毛细血管血进行现场筛查,并使用从有向无环图中选择的协变量,将关联性表达为来自具有稳健方差的改良泊松回归的调整后的 患病率 比率。
主要结果
在 480 名参与者中(78.8% 为男性,中位年龄 40 岁),320 名参与者(66.7%,95% 置信区间 62.3-70.9)至少有一种反应性标记。305 例 (63.5%,59.1–67.9) 出现抗 HCV 反应,61 例 (12.7%,9.9–16.0) 出现抗 HIV 反应,15 例 (3.1%,1.8–5.1) 出现密螺旋体抗体反应,11 例 (2.3%,1.1–4.1) 出现 HBsAg 反应。
解释与创新
研究发现,抗HCV反应性集中在曾经注射过药物的人中,而密螺旋体抗体和HBsAg并没有因注射史而异。研究结果表明,需要对注射者采取有针对性的干预措施,以减轻丙肝病毒的负担。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可为吸毒者中的 监测 和 HCV 预防策略提供信息,特别是在 监测 较高的保加利亚索非亚。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到横断面设计的限制,无法确定因果关系,并且依赖自我报告的药物使用情况,这可能会引入偏差。证据强度中等。

Reactive markers of HIV, hepatitis B, hepatitis C and syphilis among people who use drugs in Sofia, Bulgaria: a cross-sectional bio-behavioural survey

Authors: Vanya Rangelova, Kostadin Rangelov Kostadinov, Yuliya Georgieva, Kristina Zhelyazkova, Elmira Nesheva, Ani Kevorkyan, Аni Kevorkyan
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background In eastern Europe and central Asia, HIV incidence among people who inject drugs has continued to rise, yet contemporary bio-behavioural data from Bulgaria are scarce. We measured the prevalence of reactive screening markers for HIV, hepatitis B, hepatitis C and syphilis among people who use drugs in Sofia and examined their correlates. Methods Cross-sectional survey of 480 adults reporting illicit drug use in the previous month, recruited through mobile outreach in June–August 2024. Capillary blood was screened on site with rapid tests. Because the outcomes are common, associations are expressed as adjusted prevalence ratios from modified Poisson regression with robust variance, with covariates chosen from a directed acyclic graph rather than by significance screening. Results Of 480 participants (78.8% men, median age 40 years), 320 (66.7%, 95% confidence interval 62.3–70.9) had at least one reactive marker. Anti-HCV was reactive in 305 (63.5%, 59.1–67.9), anti-HIV in 61 (12.7%, 9.9–16.0), treponemal antibody in 15 (3.1%, 1.8–5.1) and HBsAg in 11 (2.3%, 1.1–4.1). Reactivity was concentrated among the 304 participants who had ever injected (anti-HCV 89.1% vs 19.3% among those who had not; anti-HIV 17.1% vs 5.1%), whereas treponemal antibody and HBsAg did not differ by injecting history. Anti-HCV reactivity rose from 25.0% at 20–29 years to 82.0% at 40–49 years (test for trend p
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the scarcity of contemporary bio-behavioural data on HIV, hepatitis B, hepatitis C and syphilis among people who use drugs in Sofia, Bulgaria, given the rising incidence of HIV in eastern Europe and central Asia.
Design and population
A cross-sectional survey of 480 adults who reported illicit drug use in the previous month was conducted in Sofia, Bulgaria, from June to August 2024.
Core methods
Capillary blood was screened on site using rapid tests, and associations were expressed as adjusted prevalence ratios from modified Poisson regression with robust variance, using covariates chosen from a directed acyclic graph.
Main results
Of 480 participants (78.8% men, median age 40 years), 320 (66.7%, 95% confidence interval 62.3–70.9) had at least one reactive marker. Anti-HCV was reactive in 305 (63.5%, 59.1–67.9), anti-HIV in 61 (12.7%, 9.9–16.0), treponemal antibody in 15 (3.1%, 1.8–5.1) and HBsAg in 11 (2.3%, 1.1–4.1).
Interpretation and novelty
The study found that anti-HCV reactivity was concentrated among those who had ever injected drugs, while treponemal antibody and HBsAg did not differ by injecting history. The findings suggest a need for targeted interventions among injectors to reduce the burden of HCV.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform surveillance and prevention strategies for HCV among people who use drugs, particularly in Sofia, Bulgaria, where the prevalence is high.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design, which cannot establish causality, and the reliance on self-reported drug use, which may introduce bias. The evidence strength is moderate.

干扰素 λ 受体阳性单核细胞/巨噬细胞在 乙型肝炎病毒 相关肝纤维化中协调促纤维化免疫环境。

Interferon-λ receptor positive monocytes/macrophages orchestrate a pro-fibrotic immune environment in hepatitis B virus-related liver fibrosis.
作者： Siqi Wang, Shiqin Li, Jun Zhang, Zicheng Liu, Yucheng Zhu, Lisha Cheng, Fangyuan Chen, Yue Shen, Jian Wu, Wei Jiang
摘要中文翻译干扰素-λ 受体 (IFNLR) 是一种由 IFNLR1 和 IL-10Rβ 组成的异二聚体,介导 IFN-λ 驱动的纤维化作用。本研究调查了 IFNLR1+ 单核细胞/巨噬细胞在协调促纤维化免疫微环境中的参与情况。外周血流式细胞术分析显示,与健康对照(HC)相比,慢性乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)感染(CHI)患者和HBV相关肝硬化(LC)患者的IFNLR1+单核细胞升高。这些细胞还表现出 CCR2hiCCR5hiCX3CR1hiCD86hiHLA-DRhiCD163hiMERTKhi 表型和 IL-10hiTNF-αhi 细胞因子谱。使用四氯化碳和重组腺相关病毒 8-1.3HBV 在野生型和 Ifnlr1 敲除 (Ifnlr1-/-) 小鼠中诱导肝纤维化。Ifnlr1缺陷下调单核细胞/巨噬细胞上的CCR2和CCR5表达,减弱胶原蛋白沉积,但增强HBV-DNA复制。在体外,将骨髓源性巨噬细胞 (BMDM) 与肝星状细胞 (HSC) 或初始 CD4+ T 细胞共培养,以评估 IFNLR1+ 巨噬细胞的功能影响。同时,使用肝脏 X 受体 (LXR) 激动剂/拮抗剂检查了脂质代谢在 IFN-λ/IFNLR1 介导的巨噬细胞调节中的参与情况。Ifnlr1-/- BMDM 激活 HSC 和驱动调节性 T 细胞分化的能力减弱。从机制上讲,IFN-λ-IFNLR1信号传导抑制LXR依赖性脂质代谢,从而促进肿瘤坏死因子(TNF)-α和IL-1β的产生,并且LXR通路抑制有利于M1巨噬细胞极化。总的来说,IFNLR1 识别出促纤维化和免疫调节的单核细胞/巨噬细胞亚群。靶向 IFNLR1-LXR 轴可能是治疗 HBV 相关肝纤维化的有前景的抗纤维化策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨 IFNLR1+ 单核细胞/巨噬细胞在 乙型肝炎病毒 相关肝纤维化中协调促纤维化免疫微环境中的作用。
设计与对象
该研究涉及对 慢性乙型肝炎 病毒 (HBV) 感染和肝硬化患者的外周血进行流式细胞术分析,并与健康对照进行比较。此外,使用四氯化碳和重组腺相关病毒 8-1.3HBV 在野生型和 Ifnlr1 敲除小鼠中诱导肝纤维化。
核心方法
该研究使用流式细胞术分析单核细胞/巨噬细胞群体及其表型。体外实验涉及骨髓源性巨噬细胞 (BMDM) 与肝星状细胞 (HSC) 或初始 CD4+ T 细胞的共培养。使用肝脏 X 受体 (LXR) 激动剂和拮抗剂检查脂质代谢的参与情况。
主要结果
流式细胞术分析显示,与健康对照相比,慢性 HBV 感染和肝硬化患者的 IFNLR1+ 单核细胞升高。小鼠中的 Ifnlr1 缺陷会下调单核细胞/巨噬细胞上的 CCR2 和 CCR5 表达,减少胶原沉积,但增加 HBV-DNA 复制。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,IFNLR1+单核细胞/巨噬细胞通过其免疫调节功能在促进肝纤维化中发挥着关键作用,而靶向IFNLR1-LXR轴可能为HBV相关纤维化提供新的治疗策略。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可能为针对 HBV 相关肝纤维化患者的 IFNLR1 和 LXR 通路的抗纤维化治疗的开发提供信息,从而有可能改善疾病管理和结果。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于体外和动物模型,由于缺乏直接的人类临床结果以及可能存在物种特异性差异,因此证据强度中等。

Interferon-λ receptor positive monocytes/macrophages orchestrate a pro-fibrotic immune environment in hepatitis B virus-related liver fibrosis.

Authors: Siqi Wang, Shiqin Li, Jun Zhang, Zicheng Liu, Yucheng Zhu, Lisha Cheng, Fangyuan Chen, Yue Shen, Jian Wu, Wei Jiang
Original abstract (English)
Interferon-λ receptor (IFNLR), a heterodimer composed of IFNLR1 and IL-10Rβ, mediates IFN-λ-driven fibrotic effects. This study investigated the involvement of IFNLR1+ monocytes/macrophages in orchestrating pro-fibrotic immune microenvironment. Flow cytometric analysis of peripheral blood showed that IFNLR1+ monocytes were elevated in patients with chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection (CHI) and patients with HBV-related liver cirrhosis (LC), compared with healthy controls (HC). These cells also displayed a CCR2hiCCR5hiCX3CR1hiCD86hiHLA-DRhiCD163hiMERTKhi phenotype with an IL-10hiTNF-αhi cytokine profile. Hepatic fibrosis was induced in wild-type and Ifnlr1-knockout (Ifnlr1-/-) mice using carbon tetrachloride and recombinant adeno-associated virus 8-1.3HBV. Ifnlr1 deficiency down-regulated CCR2 and CCR5 expression on monocytes/macrophages, attenuated collagen deposition, but enhanced HBV-DNA replication. In vitro, bone marrow-derived macrophages (BMDMs) were co-cultured with hepatic stellate cells (HSCs) or naive CD4+ T cells to assess the functional impact of IFNLR1+ macrophages. Concurrently, the involvement of lipid metabolism in IFN-λ/IFNLR1-mediated macrophage regulation was examined using liver X receptor (LXR) agonists/antagonists. Ifnlr1-/- BMDMs exhibited a diminished capacity to activate HSCs and drive regulatory T cells differentiation. Mechanistically, IFN-λ-IFNLR1 signaling suppressed LXR-dependent lipid metabolism, thereby promoting tumor necrosis factor (TNF)-α and IL-1β production, and LXR pathway inhibition favored M1-macrophage polarization. Collectively, IFNLR1 identifies a pro-fibrotic and immunoregulatory monocyte/macrophage subset. Targeting IFNLR1-LXR axis may represent promising anti-fibrotic strategies for HBV-related liver fibrosis.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate the role of IFNLR1+ monocytes/macrophages in orchestrating a pro-fibrotic immune microenvironment in hepatitis B virus-related liver fibrosis.
Design and population
The study involved flow cytometric analysis of peripheral blood from patients with chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection and liver cirrhosis, compared with healthy controls. Additionally, hepatic fibrosis was induced in wild-type and Ifnlr1-knockout mice using carbon tetrachloride and recombinant adeno-associated virus 8-1.3HBV.
Core methods
The study used flow cytometry to analyze monocyte/macrophage populations and their phenotypes. In vitro experiments involved co-culturing bone marrow-derived macrophages (BMDMs) with hepatic stellate cells (HSCs) or naive CD4+ T cells. The involvement of lipid metabolism was examined using liver X receptor (LXR) agonists and antagonists.
Main results
Flow cytometric analysis showed elevated IFNLR1+ monocytes in patients with chronic HBV infection and liver cirrhosis compared to healthy controls. Ifnlr1 deficiency in mice down-regulated CCR2 and CCR5 expression on monocytes/macrophages, reduced collagen deposition, but increased HBV-DNA replication.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that IFNLR1+ monocytes/macrophages play a critical role in promoting liver fibrosis through their immunoregulatory functions, and that targeting the IFNLR1-LXR axis may offer new therapeutic strategies for HBV-related fibrosis.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results may inform the development of anti-fibrotic therapies targeting IFNLR1 and LXR pathways for patients with HBV-related liver fibrosis, potentially improving disease management and outcomes.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro and animal models, and the evidence strength is moderate due to the absence of direct human clinical outcomes and the potential for species-specific differences.
