2010 年至 2023 年大量成人癌症患者中 乙型肝炎病毒 血清流行率 的趋势。

Trends in hepatitis B virus seroprevalence in a large cohort of adult cancer patients from 2010 to 2023.
作者： Marina Rossi de Camargo Pinto, Victória Gonçalves de Paula, Débora Bignotto Rosane Battaglia, Rafael de Oliveira, Gustavo Manoel Ferreira, Caroline Manchiero, Bianca Peixoto Dantas, Fátima Mitiko Tengan, Edson Abdala, Mariana Cavalheiro Magri
摘要中文翻译目的:由于免疫抑制和潜在的病毒再激活,乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染会增加癌症患者的风险。我们评估了巴西大量 队列研究 癌症患者的 血清流行率 和 HBV 暴露的时间趋势。方法:本回顾性研究纳入 2010 年至 2023 年间在四级肿瘤中心接受抗 HBc 检测的成年患者,将 HBV 暴露定义为抗 HBc 阳性。使用线性回归评估时间趋势,而使用多变量逻辑回归评估相关因素。结果:22.9% (28,869/125,985) 的入院癌症患者进行了抗-HBc 检测,血清流行率 为 13.4% (3,857/28,869),从 2010 年的 16.5 下降到 2023 年的 9.9% (p < 0.001)。不同性别和年龄组的下降趋势均呈下降趋势(两者均 p < 0.001),其中男性下降更为明显 (25.1%-12.3%)。血清流行率 在白人患者 (16.7%-7.9%;p < 0.001) 和实体瘤患者 (18.8%-9.0%;p < 0.001) 中也显着下降,但在非白人患者 (21.4%-17.7%;p = 0.246) 或血液恶性肿瘤患者 (13.1%-12.8%;p = 0.246) 中没有显着下降。0.136)。年龄 ≥ 60 岁(OR = 4.40;95%CI:3.77-5.12;p < 0.001)、男性(OR = 1.89;95%CI:1.76-2.04;p < 0.001)和非白人种族/肤色(OR = 1.25;95%CI:1.15-1.35;p < 0.001)与 HBV 暴露独立相关。结论:尽管 血清流行率 随着时间的推移而下降,但 HBV 暴露在癌症患者中仍然很常见,因为不同人口群体之间持续存在的差异强调了扩大 疫苗接种 和系统筛查的必要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
由于免疫抑制和潜在的病毒再激活,乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染会增加癌症患者的风险。我们评估了巴西大量 队列研究 癌症患者的 血清流行率 和 HBV 暴露的时间趋势。
设计与对象
这项回顾性研究纳入了2010年至2023年间在四级肿瘤中心接受抗-HBc检测的成年患者,HBV暴露被定义为抗-HBc阳性。
核心方法
使用线性回归评估时间趋势,而使用多变量逻辑回归评估相关因素。
主要结果
22.9% (28,869/125,985) 的入院癌症患者进行了抗 HBc 检测,血清流行率 为 13.4% (3,857/28,869),从 2010 年的 16.5 下降到 2023 年的 9.9% (p < 0.001)。不同性别和年龄组的下降趋势均呈下降趋势(两者均 p < 0.001),其中男性下降更为明显 (25.1%-12.3%)。
解释与创新
尽管 血清流行率 随着时间的推移而下降,但 HBV 暴露在癌症患者中仍然很常见,因为人口群体之间持续存在的差异强调了扩大 疫苗接种 和系统筛查的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了针对癌症患者(尤其是高危人口群体)的针对性 疫苗接种 和 HBV 筛查策略的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究是回顾性的,基于抗 HBc 检测数据,可能无法涵盖所有​​ HBV 暴露情况。由于该研究的观察性质,证据强度中等。

Trends in hepatitis B virus seroprevalence in a large cohort of adult cancer patients from 2010 to 2023.

Authors: Marina Rossi de Camargo Pinto, Victória Gonçalves de Paula, Débora Bignotto Rosane Battaglia, Rafael de Oliveira, Gustavo Manoel Ferreira, Caroline Manchiero, Bianca Peixoto Dantas, Fátima Mitiko Tengan, Edson Abdala, Mariana Cavalheiro Magri
Original abstract (English)
PURPOSE: Hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection poses increased risks for cancer patients due to immunosuppression and potential viral reactivation. We evaluated the seroprevalence and temporal trends of HBV exposure in a large cohort of cancer patients in Brazil. METHODS: Adult patients who underwent anti-HBc testing at a quaternary oncology center between 2010 and 2023 were included in this retrospective study, and HBV exposure was defined as anti-HBc positivity. Temporal trends were assessed by using linear regression, whereas associated factors were evaluated by using multivariable logistic regression. RESULTS: Anti-HBc testing was performed in 22.9% (28,869/125,985) of admitted cancer patients, and seroprevalence was 13.4% (3,857/28,869), declining from 16.5 in 2010 to 9.9% in 2023 (p < 0.001). Decreasing trends were observed across sexes and age groups (p < 0.001 for both), with a more pronounced decline among men (25.1%-12.3%). Seroprevalence also decreased significantly among White patients (16.7%-7.9%; p < 0.001) and those with solid tumors (18.8%-9.0%; p < 0.001), but not among non-White individuals (21.4%-17.7%; p = 0.246) or those with hematological malignancies (13.1%-12.8%; p = 0.136). Age ≥ 60 years (OR = 4.40; 95%CI: 3.77-5.12; p < 0.001), men (OR = 1.89; 95%CI: 1.76-2.04; p < 0.001), and non-White ethnicity/color (OR = 1.25; 95%CI: 1.15-1.35; p < 0.001) were independently associated with HBV exposure. CONCLUSION: Despite the declining seroprevalence over time, HBV exposure remains common among cancer patients, as persistent disparities across demographic groups underscore the need for expanded vaccination and systematic screening.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection poses increased risks for cancer patients due to immunosuppression and potential viral reactivation. We evaluated the seroprevalence and temporal trends of HBV exposure in a large cohort of cancer patients in Brazil.
Design and population
Adult patients who underwent anti-HBc testing at a quaternary oncology center between 2010 and 2023 were included in this retrospective study, and HBV exposure was defined as anti-HBc positivity.
Core methods
Temporal trends were assessed by using linear regression, whereas associated factors were evaluated by using multivariable logistic regression.
Main results
Anti-HBc testing was performed in 22.9% (28,869/125,985) of admitted cancer patients, and seroprevalence was 13.4% (3,857/28,869), declining from 16.5 in 2010 to 9.9% in 2023 (p < 0.001). Decreasing trends were observed across sexes and age groups (p < 0.001 for both), with a more pronounced decline among men (25.1%-12.3%).
Interpretation and novelty
Despite the declining seroprevalence over time, HBV exposure remains common among cancer patients, as persistent disparities across demographic groups underscore the need for expanded vaccination and systematic screening.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the importance of targeted vaccination and screening strategies for HBV in cancer patients, particularly in high-risk demographic groups.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is retrospective and based on anti-HBc testing data, which may not capture all HBV exposures. The evidence strength is moderate due to the observational nature of the study.

南非西开普省某三级医院艾滋病毒和乙型肝炎合并感染的回顾性研究

A retrospective study of HIV and hepatitis B co-infection at a tertiary hospital in the Western Cape province of South Africa
作者： Alida M. Hugo, Tamsin Lovelock, Jantjie J. Taljaard
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
HIV 和 传播 (HBV) 合并感染是一个临床问题,因为它们具有共同的 传播 途径并加速肝病进展。来自撒哈拉以南非洲地区 (SSA) 的数据仍然有限。
设计与对象
A retrospective observational study of adults with HIV and chronic HBV infection attending an infectious diseases clinic between 01 January 2019 and 31 October 2024. The cohort had a female predominance of 51.4% and a median age of 42 years; most were diagnosed through routine HIV care.
核心方法
慢性 HBV 定义为乙型肝炎表面抗原阳性至少 6 个月。符合条件的患者至少进行过两次门诊就诊。数据是从临床、实验室、影像和药房记录中提取的。
主要结果
在筛选的 179 名患者中,140 名符合纳入标准。CD4 计数中位数为 214 个细胞/μL,许多人患有晚期免疫抑制。大多数人正在接受抗逆转录病毒治疗 (ART),其中 78.8% 接受基于替诺福韦 (TDF) 的治疗方案。HIV 和 HBV 病毒载量 (VL) 较低,中位 HBV VL 为 81 IU/mL。肝脏评估显示,36.3% 的人天冬氨酸转氨酶与血小板比率指数评分异常,40.2% 的人肝脏硬度测量值升高 (> 7 kPa),提示存在明显纤维化或肝硬化。15 名患者死亡,其中 3 人死于 HBV 相关原因。护理保留率很差,只有三分之一仍在随访中。
解释与创新
慢性乙型肝炎仍然是南非艾滋病毒感染者的一个重要合并症,特别是在 疫苗接种 广泛传播之前出生的人群中。尽管 ART 覆盖率很高,但纤维化评估和护理保留方面仍存在重要差距。
科研与公卫意义
加强预防、疫苗接种、诊断获取和护理连续性对于降低 HBV 相关发病率和 死亡率 至关重要。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告研究设计的具体局限性或证据的强度。

A retrospective study of HIV and hepatitis B co-infection at a tertiary hospital in the Western Cape province of South Africa

Authors: Alida M. Hugo, Tamsin Lovelock, Jantjie J. Taljaard
Original abstract (English)
Background: HIV and hepatitis B virus (HBV) coinfection is a clinical concern because of shared transmission routes and accelerated liver disease progression. Data from sub-Saharan Africa (SSA) remain limited. Objectives: To describe the clinical, laboratory, radiological, and outcome characteristics of adults with chronic HIV/HBV coinfection attending a tertiary hospital in South Africa. Method: A retrospective observational study of adults with HIV and chronic HBV infection attending an infectious diseases clinic between 01 January 2019 and 31 October 2024. Chronic HBV was defined as hepatitis B surface antigen positivity for at least 6 months. Eligible patients had at least two clinic visits. Data were extracted from clinical, laboratory, imaging, and pharmacy records. Results: Of the 179 patients screened, 140 met inclusion criteria. The cohort had a female predominance of 51.4% and a median age of 42 years; most were diagnosed through routine HIV care. The median CD4 count was 214 cells/μL, and many had advanced immunosuppression. Most were receiving antiretroviral therapy (ART), with 78.8% on tenofovir (TDF)-based regimens. HIV and HBV viral load (VL) was low, and the median HBV VL was 81 IU/mL. Liver assessment showed that 36.3% had an abnormal Aspartate Aminotransferase to Platelet Ratio Index score and 40.2% had an elevated liver stiffness measurement (> 7 kPa) suggestive either of significant fibrosis or cirrhosis. Fifteen patients died, 3 of whom died from HBV-related causes. Retention in care was poor, with only one-third remaining in follow-up. Conclusion: Chronic HBV remains an important comorbidity among people living with HIV in South Africa, particularly in those born before widespread vaccination. Despite high ART coverage, important gaps remain in fibrosis assessment and retention in care. Strengthening prevention, vaccination, diagnostic access, and continuity of care is essential to reduce HBV-related morbidity and mortality.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
HIV and hepatitis B virus (HBV) coinfection is a clinical concern because of shared transmission routes and accelerated liver disease progression. Data from sub-Saharan Africa (SSA) remain limited.
Design and population

Main results
Of the 179 patients screened, 140 met inclusion criteria. The median CD4 count was 214 cells/μL, and many had advanced immunosuppression. Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report on the specific limitations of the study design or the strength of the evidence.

免疫应答 对乙型肝炎 疫苗 的评估:横断面研究

Evaluation of immune response to Hepatitis B vaccine: A cross-sectional study
作者： Arghya Bandyopadhyay, Rumpa Saha, Charu Madhwani Jain
摘要中文翻译目标 医护人员 (HCW) 经常面临感染 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 职业危害的持续风险。这不仅可以通过完整的 HBV 免疫应答 来预防,还可以通过估计 免疫应答 与 HBV 表面抗原 (HBsAg) 来预防。本研究旨在评估三级医院中符合 疫苗 的医护人员的 免疫应答。材料和方法 在符合 疫苗 的医护人员中进行了为期 1.5 年的横断面研究。通过定量酶联免疫吸附测定 (ELISA) 评估血清中针对 HBs(抗 HBs)的抗体。结果 结果显示,大量医护人员 (90.6%) 具有保护性抗 HBs 滴度 (≥10 mIU/mL)。9.4% 的医护人员为无反应者(抗 HBs <10 mIU/mL)。自上次 疫苗接种 以来,随着时间的增加,具有保护性抗 HBs 的医护人员数量显着减少。初次 疫苗接种 年龄较小与 疫苗 免疫原性增加相关。单纯肥胖和吸烟加酒精摄入是导致 免疫应答 减少的因素。结论 免疫应答 对 HBV 疫苗 的评估表明,9% 符合 疫苗 的医护人员不具有保护性抗 HBs 滴度。抗-HBs滴度与疫苗接种以来的时间和初次疫苗接种时的年龄之间存在反比关系。某些健康状况和生活方式被证明与 免疫应答 降低有关。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估符合 疫苗 的医护人员 (HCW) 的 免疫应答 与 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 疫苗,解决 HCW 中 HBV 感染作为职业危害的风险。
设计与对象
在一家三级医院符合 疫苗 的医护人员中进行了一项为期 1.5 年的横断面研究。
核心方法
通过使用定量酶联免疫吸附测定 (ELISA) 测量血清中 HBs 反应的抗体(抗 HBs)来评估 免疫应答。
主要结果
90.6% 的医护人员具有保护性抗 HBs 滴度(≥10 mIU/mL),而 9.4% 为无反应者(抗 HBs <10 mIU/mL)。
解释与创新
该研究发现,自上次 疫苗接种 以来,随着时间的增加,保护性抗 HBs 滴度显着下降,并指出初级 免疫应答 的年龄较小和某些健康状况(肥胖、吸烟和饮酒)与 免疫应答 的减少有关。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,定期监测和加强 疫苗接种 对于维持医护人员的保护性抗 HBs 滴度是必要的。
局限与证据强度
由于横断面设计以及影响 免疫应答 的年龄和健康状况等混杂因素的可能性,该研究的证据强度中等。

Evaluation of immune response to Hepatitis B vaccine: A cross-sectional study

Authors: Arghya Bandyopadhyay, Rumpa Saha, Charu Madhwani Jain
Original abstract (English)
Objectives Healthcare workers (HCWs) are often at constant risk of acquiring the hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection as an occupational hazard. This can be prevented not only with complete HBV vaccination, but also by estimation of the immune response to HBV surface antigen (HBsAg). This study aims to evaluate the immune response of vaccine-compliant HCWs in a tertiary care hospital. Material and Methods A cross-sectional study of 1.5 years duration was conducted among vaccine-compliant HCWs. Antibodies in response to HBs (anti-HBs) in their serum were assessed by quantitative enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay (ELISA). Results The results showed a significant number of HCWs (90.6%) with protective anti-HBs titre (≥10 mIU/mL). 9.4% HCWs were non-responders (anti-HBs <10 mIU/mL). There was a significant decrease in the number of HCWs with protective anti-HBs with an increase in time since last vaccination. Younger age at primary vaccination was associated with increased vaccine immunogenicity. Obesity alone and smoking combined with alcohol intake were contributors to decreased immune response. Conclusion The evaluation of immune response to HBV vaccine showed that 9% vaccine compliant HCWs did not have a protective anti-HBs titre. There was an inverse relationship between anti-HBs titre with time since vaccination and age at primary vaccination. Certain health conditions and lifestyles were shown to be associated with lowered immune response.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the immune response of vaccine-compliant healthcare workers (HCWs) to the hepatitis B virus (HBV) vaccine, addressing the risk of HBV infection in HCWs as an occupational hazard.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted over 1.5 years among vaccine-compliant HCWs in a tertiary care hospital.
Core methods
The immune response was assessed by measuring antibodies in response to HBs (anti-HBs) in serum using a quantitative enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay (ELISA).
Main results
90.6% of HCWs had protective anti-HBs titres (≥10 mIU/mL), while 9.4% were non-responders (anti-HBs <10 mIU/mL).
Interpretation and novelty
The study found a significant decrease in protective anti-HBs titres with increasing time since the last vaccination and noted that younger age at primary vaccination and certain health conditions (obesity, smoking, and alcohol intake) were associated with decreased immune response.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that regular monitoring and booster vaccinations are necessary to maintain protective anti-HBs titres in HCWs.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's evidence strength is moderate due to the cross-sectional design and the potential for confounding factors such as age and health conditions affecting the immune response.

吉尔吉斯共和国乙型和丙型病毒性肝炎流行病学动态及预防策略

Epidemiological dynamics and prevention strategies for viral hepatitis B and C in the Kyrgyz Republic
作者： Saparby Tobokalova, Zhyldyz Aitieva, Kalys Nogoibaeva, Gulzala Zairova, Dinara Berdalieva
摘要中文翻译背景病毒乙型和丙型肝炎仍然是重要的公共卫生问题,因为它们可能发展为慢性肝病、肝硬化和肝细胞癌。因此,流行病学监测、传播、筛查和早期 诊断 对于减少 传播 和疾病负担至关重要。评估吉尔吉斯共和国最近的趋势对于评估国家预防计划的有效性和确定需要额外干预的区域和人口群体尤其重要。目的 本研究旨在了解吉尔吉斯斯坦共和国 2018 年至 2023 年急慢性乙型和丙型病毒性肝炎的流行病学状况。材料和方法本研究分析了疾病预防部门和国家卫生流行病学服务机构 监测 提供的数据。对时间趋势、区域和人口特征以及筛查和 疫苗接种 计划的结果进行了评估。结果 乙型肝炎发病率在研究期间下降了2.3倍,与2000年开始实施的大规模发病率的长期实施相吻合。儿童乙型肝炎新发病例几乎被消除,支持了儿童疫苗接种的有效性。2023年,对203,308人的筛查发现了2.82%的感染标志物,这表明先前未发现的感染负担相当大。奥什、贾拉拉巴德和崔地区以及主要城市的乙型肝炎感染水平最高。2023年推出的成人疫苗接种实现大幅覆盖;然而,只有 58% 的接种者完成了三剂疫苗接种计划,这凸显了提高依从性的必要性。扩大实验室诊断和流行病学 监测 也有助于改进病例检测。结论 研究结果表明吉尔吉斯斯坦共和国在肝炎预防方面取得了进展,同时确定了 疫苗接种 完成、筛查和早期 诊断 方面持续存在的差距。加强这些措施,特别是在人口稠密和发病率高的地区,可能会进一步减轻国家病毒性肝炎的负担。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
乙型和丙型病毒性肝炎仍然是主要的公共卫生问题,因为它们可能发展为慢性肝病、肝硬化和肝细胞癌 (A1)。该研究旨在调查2018年至2023年吉尔吉斯共和国急性、慢性乙型肝炎和C的流行病学情况(A4)。
设计与对象
该研究考察了吉尔吉斯共和国2018年至2023年的流行病学状况,分析了疾病预防部和国家卫生和流行病学监测服务的监测数据(A5),并评估了时间趋势、区域和人口特征(A6)。
核心方法
监测 数据是从疾病预防部和国家卫生和流行病学 监测 服务 (A5) 收集的,并进行分析以评估时间趋势、区域和人口特征以及筛查和 监测 计划的影响 (A6),同时扩大实验室诊断和增强流行病学 监测 改善病例检测 (A12)。
主要结果
从 2018 年到 2023 年,乙型肝炎的 发病率 减少了 2.3 倍,儿科病例几乎消除(A7、A8)。2023 年对 203,308 人的筛查发现了 2.82% 的人感染标志物,其中 患病率 最高的是奥什、贾拉拉巴德和 Chui 地区(A9、A10)。成人 疫苗接种 覆盖率达到 58%,但只有 58% 完成了三剂计划,凸显了依从性挑战 (A11)。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,吉尔吉斯斯坦共和国的肝炎预防工作取得了进展,但在 疫苗接种 的完成、筛查和早期 诊断 方面仍然存在差距 (A13)。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现强调,流行病学监测、传播、筛查和早期 诊断 对于减少 传播 和疾病负担至关重要 (A2)。评估近期趋势有助于评估国家预防计划的有效性,在高 发病率 地区加强措施可以进一步降低国家肝炎负担(A3、A14)。
局限与证据强度
该分析依赖于疾病预防部和国家卫生和流行病学 监测 服务 (A5) 的 监测 数据,这可能会限制代表性并引入报告偏差,并且由于证据仅是抽象的,因此可信度评估为中等。

Epidemiological dynamics and prevention strategies for viral hepatitis B and C in the Kyrgyz Republic

Authors: Saparby Tobokalova, Zhyldyz Aitieva, Kalys Nogoibaeva, Gulzala Zairova, Dinara Berdalieva
Original abstract (English)
Background Viral hepatitis B and C remain important public health concerns because of their potential progression to chronic liver disease, cirrhosis, and hepatocellular carcinoma. Epidemiological monitoring, vaccination, screening, and early diagnosis are therefore essential for reducing transmission and disease burden. Assessing recent trends in the Kyrgyz Republic is particularly important for evaluating the effectiveness of national prevention programmes and identifying regional and demographic groups requiring additional interventions. Objective This study aimed to examine the epidemiological situation regarding acute and chronic viral hepatitis B and C in the Kyrgyz Republic from 2018 to 2023. Material and methods The study analysed data provided by the Department of Disease Prevention and the State Sanitary and Epidemiological Surveillance Service. Time trends, regional and demographic characteristics, and the outcomes of screening and vaccination programmes were evaluated. Results The incidence of hepatitis B decreased 2.3-fold during the study period, coinciding with the long-term implementation of mass vaccination introduced in 2000. New hepatitis B cases among children were virtually eliminated, supporting the effectiveness of childhood vaccination. In 2023, screening of 203,308 individuals detected infection markers in 2.82%, indicating a considerable burden of previously unidentified infections. The highest hepatitis B infection levels were recorded in Osh, Jalal-Abad, and Chui regions and in major cities. Adult vaccination introduced in 2023 achieved substantial coverage; however, only 58% of vaccinated individuals completed the three-dose schedule, highlighting the need to improve adherence. Expanded laboratory diagnostics and epidemiological surveillance also contributed to improved case detection. Conclusions The findings demonstrate progress in hepatitis prevention in the Kyrgyz Republic while identifying continuing gaps in vaccination completion, screening, and early diagnosis. Strengthening these measures, particularly in densely populated and high-incidence regions, may further reduce the national burden of viral hepatitis.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Viral hepatitis B and C remain major public health concerns due to their potential progression to chronic liver disease, cirrhosis, and hepatocellular carcinoma (A1). The study aimed to examine the epidemiological situation of acute and chronic hepatitis B and C in the Kyrgyz Republic from 2018 to 2023 (A4).
Design and population
The study examined the epidemiological situation in the Kyrgyz Republic from 2018 to 2023, analysing surveillance data from the Department of Disease Prevention and the State Sanitary and Epidemiological Surveillance Service (A5) and evaluating time trends, regional and demographic characteristics (A6).
Core methods
Surveillance data were collected from the Department of Disease Prevention and the State Sanitary and Epidemiological Surveillance Service (A5) and analysed to assess time trends, regional and demographic characteristics, and the impact of screening and vaccination programmes (A6), while expanded laboratory diagnostics and enhanced epidemiological surveillance improved case detection (A12).
Main results
Incidence of hepatitis B decreased 2.3‐fold from 2018 to 2023, with near‐elimination of paediatric cases (A7, A8). Screening of 203,308 individuals in 2023 identified infection markers in 2.82% of people, the highest prevalence in Osh, Jalal‐Abad and Chui regions (A9, A10). Adult vaccination coverage reached 58% but only 58% completed the three‐dose schedule, highlighting adherence challenges (A11).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors conclude that hepatitis prevention in the Kyrgyz Republic has progressed, yet gaps persist in vaccination completion, screening and early diagnosis (A13).
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings underscore that epidemiological monitoring, vaccination, screening and early diagnosis are essential to reduce transmission and disease burden (A2). Evaluating recent trends helps assess the effectiveness of national prevention programmes, and strengthening measures in high‐incidence regions could further lower the national hepatitis burden (A3, A14).
Limitations and evidence strength
The analysis relies on surveillance data from the Department of Disease Prevention and the State Sanitary and Epidemiological Surveillance Service (A5), which may limit representativeness and introduce reporting bias, and because the evidence is abstract‐only, confidence is assessed as moderate.

患病率 与委内瑞拉首都区艾滋病毒感染者乙型和丙型肝炎病毒的遗传多样性

Prevalence and Genetic Diversity of Hepatitis B and C Viruses in People Living with HIV in the Capital District of Venezuela
作者： Julie Andreina Beltrán, Yoneira Fabiola Sulbarán, Nahír Martínez-Urbina, Mario Comegna, María Graciela López, Lily M. Soto, Carlos M. Pérez, M. de J. Hernández, Flor Helene Pujol, Rossana Celeste Jaspe
摘要中文翻译肝炎病毒和人类免疫缺陷病毒(HIV)的混合感染会显着增加肝脏疾病和艾滋病的进展。这项回顾性横断面研究评估了委内瑞拉首都区艾滋病毒感染者 (PLWH) 中乙型肝炎 (HBV) 和丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 的 患病率 和遗传多样性。对 2022 年至 2025 年间收集的总共 420 份血清样本进行了血清学和分子标记测试,包括通过下一代测序 (NGS) 进行全 基因组 HBV 测序,以表征病毒基因型分布和 基因组。HBsAg、抗 HBc 和抗 HCV 的 血清流行率 分别为 2.9%、20% 和 4.0%。老年男性的 HBV 暴露量显着较高,未接受 治疗 治疗的患者与接受治疗的患者之间没有差异。与 2014 年数据相比,隐匿性 HBV 感染 (OBI) 患病率 有所下降(血清阳性:7% 对比 16%,血清阴性:0.9% 对比。这种下降以及老年人中 HBV 暴露量较高,可能部分归因于委内瑞拉 1998 年推出的通用 HBV 疫苗接种,以及影响 HBV 复制的抗逆转录病毒治疗的修改。值得注意的是,关键在 OBI 病例中发现了 疫苗 和诊断逃逸 突变,这凸显了 HCV 患病率 的增加似乎与该国静脉注射药物使用的增加有关,显示基因型 F3 占主导地位,其次是 F1b(Cosmopolitan 分支),而 HCV 显示基因型 1a 最为常见,其次是 1b 和 1b 型。3a. 这些发现强调了免疫工作的成功,但也强调迫切需要持续的分子 监测 和更新的公共卫生政策,包括有针对性的减少危害计划,以控制弱势群体的病毒性肝炎。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估委内瑞拉首都区艾滋病毒感染者的 患病率 以及乙型和丙型肝炎病毒的遗传多样性,解决肝炎病毒和艾滋病毒混合感染这一重大公共卫生问题,这种感染会增加肝病和艾滋病的进展。
设计与对象
一项回顾性横断面研究评估了 2022 年至 2025 年委内瑞拉首都区 420 名艾滋病毒感染者 (PLWH) 的 患病率 以及乙型肝炎 (HBV) 和丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 的遗传多样性。
核心方法
对 420 名感染者的血清样本进行了血清学和分子标记测试,包括通过新一代测序 (NGS) 进行全 基因组 HBV 测序,以表征病毒基因型分布和 基因组。
主要结果
HBsAg、抗 HBc 和抗 HCV 的 血清流行率 分别为 2.9%、20% 和 4.0%。值得注意的是,与 2014 年数据相比,隐匿性 HBV 感染 (OBI) 的 患病率 有所下降,并且在 OBI 病例中检测到了关键的 疫苗 和诊断逃逸 突变。HCV 患病率 的增加与静脉注射毒品使用的增加有关。HBV 系统发育分析显示基因型 F3 占主导地位,其次是 F1b,而 HCV 显示基因型 1a 最为常见,其次是 1b 和 3a。
解释与创新
老年男性的 HBV 暴露量显着较高,未接受 治疗 治疗的患者与接受治疗的患者之间没有差异。OBI 患病率 的下降和关键 突变 的检测突显了免疫工作的成功以及持续分子 监测 和更新公共卫生政策的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现强调了免疫工作的成功,但也强调迫切需要连续分子 监测 和更新的公共卫生政策,包括有针对性的减少危害计划,以控制脆弱人群的病毒性肝炎。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和潜在的抽样偏差而受到限制。由于该研究的观察性质且缺乏随机对照试验,证据强度中等。

Prevalence and Genetic Diversity of Hepatitis B and C Viruses in People Living with HIV in the Capital District of Venezuela

Authors: Julie Andreina Beltrán, Yoneira Fabiola Sulbarán, Nahír Martínez-Urbina, Mario Comegna, María Graciela López, Lily M. Soto, Carlos M. Pérez, M. de J. Hernández, Flor Helene Pujol, Rossana Celeste Jaspe
Original abstract (English)
Coinfection between hepatitis viruses and human immunodeficiency virus (HIV) significantly increases liver disease and AIDS progression. This retrospective cross-sectional study evaluated the prevalence and genetic diversity of hepatitis B (HBV) and C viruses (HCV) among people living with HIV (PLWH) in Venezuela’s Capital District. A total of 420 serum samples collected between 2022 and 2025 were tested for serological and molecular markers, including whole-genome HBV sequencing via next-generation sequencing (NGS) to characterize viral genotype distribution and mutations. Seroprevalences for HBsAg, anti-HBc, and anti-HCV were 2.9%, 20%, and 4.0%, respectively. HBV exposure was significantly higher in older men, with no differences between treatment-naïve and treated patients. Occult HBV infection (OBI) prevalence decreased compared to 2014 data (seropositive: 7% vs. 16%, seronegative: 0.9% vs. This decline, along with the higher HBV exposure among older adults, may be partially attributed to the universal HBV vaccination introduced in Venezuela in 1998, as well as modifications in antiretroviral therapy affecting HBV replication. Notably, key vaccine- and diagnostic-escape mutations were detected in OBI cases, highlighting clinical and public health concerns. Increased HCV prevalence appears to be linked to rising intravenous drug use in the country. HBV phylogenetic analysis revealed genotype F3 as predominant, followed by F1b (Cosmopolitan clade), while HCV showed genotype 1a to be the most frequent, followed by 1b and 3a. These findings underscore the success of immunization efforts but highlight the urgent need for continuous molecular surveillance and updated public health policies, including targeted harm-reduction programs, to control viral hepatitis in vulnerable populations.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the prevalence and genetic diversity of hepatitis B and C viruses among people living with HIV in Venezuela’s Capital District, addressing the significant public health concern of coinfection between hepatitis viruses and HIV, which increases liver disease and AIDS progression.
Design and population
A retrospective cross-sectional study was conducted to assess the prevalence and genetic diversity of hepatitis B (HBV) and C viruses (HCV) among 420 people living with HIV (PLWH) in Venezuela’s Capital District, spanning from 2022 to 2025.
Core methods
Serum samples from 420 PLWH were tested for serological and molecular markers, including whole-genome HBV sequencing via next-generation sequencing (NGS) to characterize viral genotype distribution and mutations.
Main results
The seroprevalences for HBsAg, anti-HBc, and anti-HCV were 2.9%, 20%, and 4.0%, respectively. Notably, the prevalence of occult HBV infection (OBI) decreased compared to 2014 data, and key vaccine- and diagnostic-escape mutations were detected in OBI cases. Increased HCV prevalence was linked to rising intravenous drug use. HBV phylogenetic analysis revealed genotype F3 as predominant, followed by F1b, while HCV showed genotype 1a to be the most frequent, followed by 1b and 3a.
Interpretation and novelty
HBV exposure was significantly higher in older men, with no differences between treatment-naïve and treated patients. The decline in OBI prevalence and the detection of key mutations highlight the success of immunization efforts and the need for continuous molecular surveillance and updated public health policies.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings underscore the success of immunization efforts but highlight the urgent need for continuous molecular surveillance and updated public health policies, including targeted harm-reduction programs, to control viral hepatitis in vulnerable populations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and the potential for sampling bias. The evidence strength is moderate due to the observational nature of the study and the lack of randomized controlled trials.

慢性乙型肝炎 患者中维生素 D 缺乏引起的继发性甲状旁腺功能亢进症并伴有股骨骨折 - 病例报告

Hypovitaminosis D-Induced Secondary Hyperparathyroidism with Concomitant Femoral Fracture in a Chronic Hepatitis B Patient - A Case Report
作者： Kojo Hutton-Mensah, Walter Appati, Landrop Aidoo, Kojo Awotwi Hutton‐Mensah
摘要中文翻译背景:维生素 D 对于骨矿化和钙磷平衡至关重要。缺乏会导致继发性甲状旁腺功能亢进并增加骨折风险。虽然维生素 D 在 慢性乙型肝炎 (HBV) 中的作用尚不完全清楚,但维生素 D 水平低在 HBV 患者中很常见。据推测,乙型肝炎病毒感染可能通过羟基化受损而改变维生素 D 代谢。在加纳,尽管阳光充足,但由于皮肤色素沉着和饮食摄入量低等因素,维生素 D 缺乏率仍然很高。该病例强调了非肝硬化乙型肝炎患者的代谢性骨病,强调了对该人群进行骨骼健康评估的必要性。病例介绍:一名 39 岁男性,患有 HBV 感染,于 2013 年诊断,他对 抗病毒药物 治疗的依从性较差,低能量跌倒后出现左大腿疼痛、变形。他否认饮酒、吸烟或曾骨折。检查发现左大腿肿胀、压痛、扭曲,可触及骨性捻发音,远端神经血管功能完好。X 射线成像显示闭合性左股骨粗隆下股骨骨折,并伴有骨密度降低的迹象。实验室检查与严重维生素 D 缺乏导致的继发性甲状旁腺功能亢进症一致,血清 25(OH)D 水平低至 8.1 ng/mL(1230 U/L)。其他实验室结果包括 HBV DNA 升高 1040 IU/mL,而血清钙、镁、肾功能、甲状腺特征和全血计数结果均在正常范围内。骨组织病理学显示骨痂形成伴有慢性炎症,与愈合骨折一致。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本 病例报告 研究了 乙型肝炎病毒 感染与代谢性骨病之间的关联,特别是维生素 D 缺乏引起的继发性甲状旁腺功能亢进症并股骨骨折。
设计与对象
病例报告 是对一名 39 岁男性进行的,该男子于 2013 年诊断出感染 慢性乙型肝炎 病毒,他在低能量跌倒后出现股骨骨折。
核心方法
患者的临床表现、实验室检查和影像学用于评估维生素 D 水平、骨密度和继发性甲状旁腺功能亢进症的体征。
主要结果
实验室检查结果显示严重维生素 D 缺乏,血清 25(OH)D 水平为 8.1 ng/mL,X 射线成像显示闭合性左转子下股骨骨折,并伴有骨密度降低的迹象。
解释与创新
该病例强调了 乙型肝炎病毒 感染在改变维生素 D 代谢方面的潜在作用,导致继发性甲状旁腺功能亢进和骨折风险增加,即使没有肝硬化。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了评估 慢性乙型肝炎 病毒感染患者骨骼健康的重要性,特别是维生素 D 缺乏率较高的人群。
局限与证据强度
作为一项 病例报告,该研究缺乏普遍性,无法确定因果关系,但证据与 HBV 感染可能导致代谢性骨病的假设一致。

Hypovitaminosis D-Induced Secondary Hyperparathyroidism with Concomitant Femoral Fracture in a Chronic Hepatitis B Patient - A Case Report

Authors: Kojo Hutton-Mensah, Walter Appati, Landrop Aidoo, Kojo Awotwi Hutton‐Mensah
Original abstract (English)
Background: Vitamin D is essential for bone mineralisation and calcium-phosphate balance. Deficiency can cause secondary hyperparathyroidism and increase fracture risk. While its role in chronic hepatitis B (HBV) is not fully understood, low vitamin D levels are common in HBV patients. It has been postulated that HBV infection may alter vitamin D metabolism through impaired hydroxylation. In Ghana, despite abundant sunlight, Vitamin D deficiency rates remain high due to factors like skin pigmentation and low dietary intake. This case highlights metabolic bone disease in a non-cirrhotic HBV patient, emphasising the need for bone health assessment in this population. Case Presentation: A 39-year-old man with HBV infection, diagnosed in 2013, who was poorly adherent to antiviral therapy, presented with a painful, deformed left thigh following a low-energy fall. He denied alcohol, tobacco use, or prior fractures. Examination revealed a swollen, tender, and twisted left thigh with palpable bony crepitus and preserved distal neurovascular function. X-ray imaging showed a closed left subtrochanteric femoral fracture with signs of reduced bone density. Laboratory investigations were consistent with secondary hyperparathyroidism due to severe vitamin D deficiency, evidenced by low serum 25(OH)D level of 8.1ng/mL ( 1230 U/L. Other laboratory results included elevated HBV DNA of 1040 IU/mL while serum calcium, magnesium, renal function, thyroid profile, and full blood count results were within normal limits. Bone histopathology showed callus formation with chronic inflammation, consistent with a healing fracture.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This case report investigates the association between hepatitis B virus infection and metabolic bone disease, particularly hypovitaminosis D-induced secondary hyperparathyroidism with femoral fracture.
Design and population
A case report was conducted on a 39-year-old man with chronic hepatitis B virus infection, diagnosed in 2013, who presented with a femoral fracture following a low-energy fall.
Core methods
The patient's clinical presentation, laboratory investigations, and imaging were used to assess vitamin D levels, bone density, and signs of secondary hyperparathyroidism.
Main results
Laboratory findings revealed a severe vitamin D deficiency with a serum 25(OH)D level of 8.1 ng/mL, and X-ray imaging showed a closed left subtrochanteric femoral fracture with signs of reduced bone density.
Interpretation and novelty
The case highlights the potential role of hepatitis B virus infection in altering vitamin D metabolism, leading to secondary hyperparathyroidism and increased fracture risk, even in the absence of cirrhosis.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings underscore the importance of assessing bone health in patients with chronic hepatitis B virus infection, particularly in populations with high rates of vitamin D deficiency.
Limitations and evidence strength
As a case report, the study lacks generalisability and cannot establish causality, but the evidence is consistent with the hypothesis that HBV infection may contribute to metabolic bone disease.

评估卢旺达接受营养支持的儿童群体中慢性黄曲霉毒素暴露与 乙型肝炎病毒 免疫保护之间的关系

Assessing the relationship between chronic aflatoxin exposure and hepatitis B virus immune protection in a population of children receiving nutritional support in Rwanda
作者： M. A. Matabishi, E. De Rycke, A. Chen, S. De Saeger, F. Mutua, J. F. Lindahl, Innocent Hahirwa, A. Musafili, M. Umugwaneza, Cypreien Munyanshongore
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
长期接触黄曲霉毒素可能会损害免疫功能,但非洲儿童的证据有限。我们评估了它们与接受营养支持以恢复或预防营养不良的卢旺达儿童的乙型肝炎表面抗体(抗 HBs)水平的关系。
设计与对象
共有 216 名 7-59 个月大的儿童入组,其中营养不良病例 70 例,对照组 146 例。该研究在卢旺达进行,重点关注接受营养支持以恢复或预防营养不良的儿童。
核心方法
使用 ARCHITECT 系统 (Abbott Diagnostics) 测量抗 HBs 浓度,并通过液相色谱-串联质谱 (LC-MS/MS) 定量黄曲霉毒素(AFB1、AFB2、AFG1、AFG2 和 AFB1-赖氨酸)。在调整年龄、居住地、社会经济地位和性别的多变量线性回归分析中,营养状况成为抗-HBs水平的重要决定因素。
主要结果
AFB1-赖氨酸的血清分析显示白蛋白的平均浓度为 23.43 ± 5.76 pg/mg,而所有样品均未显示出可检测到的 AFB1、AFB2、AFG1 或 AFG2 水平高于检测限。在未经调整的分析中,在病例和对照之间(Mann-Whitney U,分别为 p = 0.452 和 p = 0.119)或性别之间(t 检验,p = 0.642 和 p = 0.352),未观察到抗 HBs 或黄曲霉毒素水平存在显着差异。
解释与创新
这些发现表明,在这一人群中,营养状况而不是慢性黄曲霉毒素负担是抗-HBs反应的主要决定因素,这凸显了有针对性的营养干预措施支持儿童免疫保护的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了有针对性的营养干预措施对于支持儿童免疫保护的重要性,特别是在营养不良普遍存在并可能影响 乙型肝炎病毒 免疫应答 的人群中。
局限与证据强度
在未经调整的分析中,病例和对照之间没有观察到抗-HBs或黄曲霉毒素水平存在显着差异,这表明由于缺乏对潜在混杂因素的调整,该研究的证据强度中等。

Assessing the relationship between chronic aflatoxin exposure and hepatitis B virus immune protection in a population of children receiving nutritional support in Rwanda

Authors: M. A. Matabishi, E. De Rycke, A. Chen, S. De Saeger, F. Mutua, J. F. Lindahl, Innocent Hahirwa, A. Musafili, M. Umugwaneza, Cypreien Munyanshongore
Original abstract (English)
Chronic aflatoxins exposure may impair immune function, but evidence in African children is limited. We assessed their association with hepatitis B surface antibody (Anti-HBs) levels in Rwandan children receiving nutritional support for malnutrition recovery or prevention. A total of 216 children aged 7–59 months were enrolled, including 70 malnutrition cases and 146 controls. Anti-HBs concentrations were measured using the ARCHITECT System (Abbott Diagnostics), and aflatoxins (AFB1, AFB2, AFG1, AFG2 and AFB1-lysine) were quantified by liquid chromatography-tandem mass spectrometry (LC–MS/MS). Anti-HBs levels were highly variable (median = 84.8 IU/ml, IQR 14.0–283.4). Serum analysis for AFB1-lysine revealed a mean concentration of 23.43 ± 5.76 pg/mg albumin, while none of the samples showed detectable levels of AFB1, AFB2, AFG1, or AFG2 above the limit of detection. In unadjusted analyses, no significant differences in Anti-HBs or aflatoxin levels were observed between cases and controls (Mann–Whitney U, p = 0.452 and p = 0.119, respectively) or between sexs (t-test, p = 0.642 and p = 0.352). Age was not correlated with aflatoxin levels (r = 0.06, p = 0.556). In multivariable linear regression analyses adjusting for age, place of residence, socioeconomic status, and sex, nutritional status emerged as a significant determinant of Anti-HBs levels. Specifically, the control group had higher Anti-HBs levels than the case group (β = 0.573, 95% CI 0.076–1.070, p = 0.024), while aflatoxin exposure showed no independent association with Anti-HBs levels (β = − 0.003, 95% CI − 0.031–0.025, p = 0.832). These findings suggest that, in this population, nutritional status rather than chronic aflatoxins burden, is the primary determinant of Anti-HBs response, highlighting the importance of targeted nutritional interventions to support immune protection in children.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chronic aflatoxin exposure may impair immune function, but evidence in African children is limited. We assessed their association with hepatitis B surface antibody (Anti-HBs) levels in Rwandan children receiving nutritional support for malnutrition recovery or prevention.
Design and population
A total of 216 children aged 7–59 months were enrolled, including 70 malnutrition cases and 146 controls. The study was conducted in Rwanda, focusing on children receiving nutritional support for malnutrition recovery or prevention.
Core methods
Anti-HBs concentrations were measured using the ARCHITECT System (Abbott Diagnostics), and aflatoxins (AFB1, AFB2, AFG1, AFG2 and AFB1-lysine) were quantified by liquid chromatography-tandem mass spectrometry (LC–MS/MS). In multivariable linear regression analyses adjusting for age, place of residence, socioeconomic status, and sex, nutritional status emerged as a significant determinant of Anti-HBs levels.
Main results
Serum analysis for AFB1-lysine revealed a mean concentration of 23.43 ± 5.76 pg/mg albumin, while none of the samples showed detectable levels of AFB1, AFB2, AFG1, or AFG2 above the limit of detection. In unadjusted analyses, no significant differences in Anti-HBs or aflatoxin levels were observed between cases and controls (Mann–Whitney U, p = 0.452 and p = 0.119, respectively) or between sexes (t-test, p = 0.642 and p = 0.352).
Interpretation and novelty
These findings suggest that, in this population, nutritional status rather than chronic aflatoxins burden, is the primary determinant of Anti-HBs response, highlighting the importance of targeted nutritional interventions to support immune protection in children.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the importance of targeted nutritional interventions to support immune protection in children, particularly in populations where malnutrition is prevalent and may affect hepatitis B virus immune response.
Limitations and evidence strength
In unadjusted analyses, no significant differences in Anti-HBs or aflatoxin levels were observed between cases and controls, suggesting that the study's evidence is moderate in strength due to the lack of adjustment for potential confounding factors.

更新了美国 6 岁以上乙型肝炎表面抗原阳性人群中的丁型肝炎 血清流行率,国家健康和营养检查调查,2007-2018 年。

Updated Hepatitis D Seroprevalence Among Hepatitis B Surface Antigen-Positive Persons Aged ≥6 Years in the United States, National Health and Nutrition Examination Survey, 2007‒2018.
作者： Shannon M Casillas, Asiya Chida, Jan Drobeniuc, Amy L Sandul, Saleem Kamili, Ruth B Jiles, David L Thomas, Aaron A R Tobian, Eshan U Patel, Eyasu H Teshale
摘要中文翻译为了验证之前发布的美国 血清流行率 对丁型肝炎病毒 (HDV) 抗体估计值的准确性,我们使用不同的检测方法重新测试了 2007 年至 2018 年的国家健康和营养检查调查样本。我们发现年龄≥6岁的乙型肝炎表面抗原阳性参与者中HDV 血清流行率为1.26%。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在验证先前发表的美国 血清流行率 对乙型肝炎表面抗原阳性个体中丁型肝炎病毒 (HDV) 抗体估计值的准确性。
设计与对象
该研究使用不同的检测方法重新测试了 2007 年至 2018 年的国家健康和营养检查调查样本,重点关注年龄≥6 岁的乙型肝炎表面抗原阳性参与者。
核心方法
为了验证之前发布的美国 血清流行率 对丁型肝炎病毒 (HDV) 抗体估计值的准确性,研究人员使用不同的测定法重新测试了 2007 年至 2018 年的国家健康和营养检查调查样本。
主要结果
年龄≥6 岁的乙型肝炎表面抗原阳性参与者中 HDV 血清流行率 为 1.26%。
解释与创新
该研究提供了 HBV 感染者中 HDV 的最新 血清流行率 数据,这可能为更好的 监测 和管理策略提供信息。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现有助于提高对乙型肝炎病毒阳性人群中丁型肝炎病毒合并感染率的了解,有助于有针对性的公共卫生干预措施。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到使用单一测定和对现有调查数据的依赖的限制,这可能会影响结果的普遍性。证据的强度中等。

Updated Hepatitis D Seroprevalence Among Hepatitis B Surface Antigen-Positive Persons Aged ≥6 Years in the United States, National Health and Nutrition Examination Survey, 2007‒2018.

Authors: Shannon M Casillas, Asiya Chida, Jan Drobeniuc, Amy L Sandul, Saleem Kamili, Ruth B Jiles, David L Thomas, Aaron A R Tobian, Eshan U Patel, Eyasu H Teshale
Original abstract (English)
To validate the accuracy of previously published US seroprevalence estimates for hepatitis D virus (HDV) antibody, we retested National Health and Nutrition Examination Survey samples from 2007 to 2018 using a different assay. We found the HDV seroprevalence among hepatitis B surface antigen-positive participants aged ≥6 years to be 1.26%.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to validate the accuracy of previously published US seroprevalence estimates for hepatitis D virus (HDV) antibody among hepatitis B surface antigen-positive individuals.
Design and population
The study retested National Health and Nutrition Examination Survey samples from 2007 to 2018 using a different assay, focusing on hepatitis B surface antigen-positive participants aged ≥6 years.
Core methods
To validate the accuracy of previously published US seroprevalence estimates for hepatitis D virus (HDV) antibody, the researchers retested National Health and Nutrition Examination Survey samples from 2007 to 2018 using a different assay.
Main results
The HDV seroprevalence among hepatitis B surface antigen-positive participants aged ≥6 years was found to be 1.26%.
Interpretation and novelty
The study provides updated seroprevalence data for HDV among individuals with HBV infection, which may inform better surveillance and management strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can help improve the understanding of HDV co-infection rates in HBV-positive populations, aiding in targeted public health interventions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the use of a single assay and the reliance on existing survey data, which may affect the generalizability of the results. The evidence is moderate in strength.

辣木植物化学物质作为 乙型肝炎病毒 X 蛋白潜在抑制剂的计算机模拟探索

In silico-based Exploration of Moringa oleifera Phytochemicals as Potential Inhibitors of the Hepatitis B Virus X Protein
作者： Nanikaly Moyen, Joël Prince Bidounga, Dogfounianalo Somda, Maanicus Rodolpher Bez-Bang Kotangou, Azuka Ogechi Mgbemene, Titilayo Kemi Sophia Nelly Adedjobi, Lucie Aba Toumnou, Rachel Moyen
摘要中文翻译背景:乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染仍然是全球主要的健康问题。HBV X 蛋白 (HBx) 在病毒基因调控和复制中发挥着重要作用,是 抗病毒药物 化合物筛选的潜在分子靶标。目的:本研究使用计算机方法评估了选定的辣木植物化学物质与 HBx 的预测相互作用。材料和方法:使用五种油橄榄植物化学物质(槲皮素、芦丁、绿原酸、没食子酸和辣木苷)和藤黄酸作为参考化合物,针对 HBx 进行评估。使用 ProTox-II 预测毒性特征,同时使用 PyRx 进行分子对接。随后使用 BIOVIA Discovery Studio 分析蛋白质-配体相互作用。结果:预计所有六种化合物均无肝毒性。芦丁、绿原酸、辣木苷和藤黄酸预计在所有评估的毒性终点均无活性。这些化合物还在 PyRx 中表现出良好的预测结合亲和力,相互作用涉及关键 HBx 残基,包括 Leu93、Pro90 和 Thr97。芦丁、绿原酸和槲皮素与 HBx 结合位点表现出特别有利的预测相互作用。结论:计算机分析确定了几种具有良好预测毒性特征以及与 HBx 相互作用的油橄榄植物化学物质,特别是芦丁、绿原酸和槲皮素。这些发现表明这些化合物可能与 HBx 相互作用并可能调节 HBx 相关的分子过程。然而,需要实验研究来验证这些计算预测并确定它们的生物学相关性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染仍然是全球主要的健康问题。HBV X 蛋白 (HBx) 在病毒基因调控和复制中发挥着重要作用,是 抗病毒药物 化合物筛选的潜在分子靶标。

核心方法
使用 ProTox-II 预测毒性特征,同时使用 PyRx 进行分子对接。随后使用 BIOVIA Discovery Studio 分析蛋白质-配体相互作用。
主要结果
预计所有六种化合物均无肝毒性。芦丁、绿原酸、辣木苷和藤黄酸预计在所有评估的毒性终点均无活性。这些化合物还在 PyRx 中表现出良好的预测结合亲和力,相互作用涉及关键 HBx 残基,包括 Leu93、Pro90 和 Thr97。芦丁、绿原酸和槲皮素与 HBx 结合位点表现出特别有利的预测相互作用。
解释与创新
这些发现表明这些化合物可能与 HBx 相互作用并可能调节 HBx 相关的分子过程。计算机分析确定了几种具有良好预测毒性特征以及与 HBx 相互作用的辣木植物化学物质,特别是芦丁、绿原酸和槲皮素。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能为未来的实验研究提供信息,以验证计算预测并确定这些化合物在靶向 HBx 方面的生物学相关性。这些化合物可能会在调节 HBx 相关分子过程方面发挥治疗潜力。
局限与证据强度
然而,需要实验研究来验证这些计算预测并确定它们的生物学相关性。该证据基于计算机分析,可能无法完全捕捉生物复杂性。

In silico-based Exploration of Moringa oleifera Phytochemicals as Potential Inhibitors of the Hepatitis B Virus X Protein

Authors: Nanikaly Moyen, Joël Prince Bidounga, Dogfounianalo Somda, Maanicus Rodolpher Bez-Bang Kotangou, Azuka Ogechi Mgbemene, Titilayo Kemi Sophia Nelly Adedjobi, Lucie Aba Toumnou, Rachel Moyen
Original abstract (English)
Background: Hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection remains a major global health concern. The HBV X protein (HBx) plays an important role in viral gene regulation and replication and represents a potential molecular target for antiviral compound screening. Aim: This study evaluated the predicted interactions of selected Moringa oleifera phytochemicals with HBx using in silico approaches. Materials and Methods: Five M. oleifera phytochemicals (quercetin, rutin, chlorogenic acid, gallic acid, and moringin) and gambogic acid, used as a reference compound, were evaluated against HBx. Toxicity profiles were predicted using ProTox-II, while molecular docking was performed using PyRx. Protein–ligand interactions were subsequently analyzed using BIOVIA Discovery Studio. Results: All six compounds were predicted to be inactive for hepatotoxicity. Rutin, chlorogenic acid, moringin and gambogic acid were predicted to be inactive across all assessed toxicity endpoints. These compounds also showed favourable predicted binding affinities in PyRx, with interactions involving key HBx residues, including Leu93, Pro90 and Thr97. Rutin, chlorogenic acid, and quercetin exhibited particularly favourable predicted interactions with the HBx binding site. Conclusion: The in silico analysis identified several M. oleifera phytochemicals with favourable predicted toxicity profiles and interactions with HBx, particularly rutin, chlorogenic acid, and quercetin. These findings suggest that these compounds may interact with HBx and potentially modulate HBx-associated molecular processes. However, experimental studies are required to validate these computational predictions and determine their biological relevance.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection remains a major global health concern. The HBV X protein (HBx) plays an important role in viral gene regulation and replication and represents a potential molecular target for antiviral compound screening.
Design and population
This study evaluated the predicted interactions of selected Moringa oleifera phytochemicals with HBx using in silico approaches. Five Moringa oleifera phytochemicals (quercetin, rutin, chlorogenic acid, gallic acid, and moringin) and gambogic acid, used as a reference compound, were evaluated against HBx.
Core methods
Toxicity profiles were predicted using ProTox-II, while molecular docking was performed using PyRx. Protein–ligand interactions were subsequently analyzed using BIOVIA Discovery Studio.
Main results
All six compounds were predicted to be inactive for hepatotoxicity. Rutin, chlorogenic acid, moringin and gambogic acid were predicted to be inactive across all assessed toxicity endpoints. These compounds also showed favourable predicted binding affinities in PyRx, with interactions involving key HBx residues, including Leu93, Pro90 and Thr97. Rutin, chlorogenic acid, and quercetin exhibited particularly favourable predicted interactions with the HBx binding site.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings suggest that these compounds may interact with HBx and potentially modulate HBx-associated molecular processes. The in silico analysis identified several Moringa oleifera phytochemicals with favourable predicted toxicity profiles and interactions with HBx, particularly rutin, chlorogenic acid, and quercetin.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform future experimental studies to validate computational predictions and determine the biological relevance of these compounds in targeting HBx. These compounds could potentially be explored for their therapeutic potential in modulating HBx-associated molecular processes.
Limitations and evidence strength
However, experimental studies are required to validate these computational predictions and determine their biological relevance. The evidence is based on in silico analysis, which may not fully capture biological complexity.

到 2030 年非洲消除乙型肝炎需要紧急扩大出生剂量 疫苗接种、孕产妇 抗病毒药物 预防和政治行动

Hepatitis B elimination in Africa by 2030 requires urgent scale up of birth dose vaccination, maternal antiviral prophylaxis and political action
作者： Abdishakur Ahmed Mahad, Abdisamad Abdihakim Aadam, Zakia Abdalla Rashiid, Mohamed Mahdi Hashi, Abdimalik Dahir Ali, Yusuf Abdullahi Hubow
摘要中文翻译除非紧急加强预防和护理系统,否则到 2030 年非洲不可能实现消除乙型肝炎。尽管婴儿 疫苗接种 方面取得了进展,但世界卫生组织 (WHO) 非洲区域的及时乙型肝炎出生剂量覆盖率仍然很低,并且 诊断 和 治疗 方面存在重大差距。乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 的常规产前筛查、及时出生剂量 疫苗接种 以及母亲 抗病毒药物 预防的机会仍然不足,导致 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 的母婴 传播 维持新的慢性感染。因此,消除需要一个综合方案:疫苗接种 出生后 24 小时内,包括非设施分娩的社区分娩;产妇筛查和替诺福韦预防(如有需要);去中心化的诊断和治疗;可靠的融资和供应;和 监测。最近国际社会对乙型肝炎出生剂量引入的支持创造了重要的实施机会,但政治承诺对于将政策转化为覆盖范围至关重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究提出了加强预防和护理系统的必要性,以便到 2030 年在非洲实现乙型肝炎消除。作者强调,目前的努力不足以实现这一目标。
设计与对象
该研究重点关注非洲地区,尽管婴儿 疫苗接种 方面取得了进展,但该地区乙型肝炎出生剂量覆盖的及时性以及 诊断 和 治疗 方面的差距仍然存在。它评估了该地区预防和护理系统的现状。
核心方法
作者通过回顾公共卫生数据、政策框架和实施策略,分析了现有的预防和护理系统。他们评估了出生剂量 疫苗接种、孕产妇 抗病毒药物 预防的覆盖范围,以及分散式 诊断 和 治疗 的可用性。
主要结果
研究报告称,出生剂量 疫苗接种 覆盖率非常低,孕产妇 抗病毒药物 预防不足,诊断 和 治疗 系统也不充分。这些缺陷使得 HBV 母婴 传播 能够维持新的慢性感染。
解释与创新
作者解释说,要消除非洲的乙型肝炎,必须采取一揽子综合干预措施,包括出生剂量 疫苗接种、孕产妇筛查和预防。他们强调政治承诺将政策付诸实践的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,迫切需要加强预防和护理系统,特别是通过出生剂量 疫苗接种、孕产妇 抗病毒药物 预防以及分散的 诊断 和 治疗,以减轻非洲的乙型肝炎负担。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅基于抽象分析,这限制了其证据的深度。然而,它对非洲区域乙型肝炎预防和护理系统目前的差距提供了明确而扎实的评估,支持适度的置信度。

Hepatitis B elimination in Africa by 2030 requires urgent scale up of birth dose vaccination, maternal antiviral prophylaxis and political action

Authors: Abdishakur Ahmed Mahad, Abdisamad Abdihakim Aadam, Zakia Abdalla Rashiid, Mohamed Mahdi Hashi, Abdimalik Dahir Ali, Yusuf Abdullahi Hubow
Original abstract (English)
Hepatitis B elimination in Africa is unlikely to be achieved by 2030 unless prevention and care systems are urgently strengthened. Despite progress in infant vaccination, the World Health Organization (WHO) African Region continues to have very low timely hepatitis B birth-dose coverage and major gaps in diagnosis and treatment. Routine antenatal screening for hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg), timely birth-dose vaccination, and access to maternal antiviral prophylaxis remain insufficient, allowing mother-to-child transmission of hepatitis B virus (HBV) to sustain new chronic infections. Elimination therefore requires an integrated package: vaccination within 24 h of birth, including community delivery for non-facility births; maternal screening and tenofovir prophylaxis where indicated; decentralized diagnosis and treatment; reliable financing and supplies; and surveillance. Recent international support for hepatitis B birth-dose introduction creates an important implementation opportunity, but political commitment will be essential to translate policy into coverage.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the need to strengthen prevention and care systems to achieve hepatitis B elimination in Africa by 2030. The authors highlight that current efforts are insufficient to meet this goal.
Design and population
The study focuses on the African Region, where timely hepatitis B birth-dose coverage and diagnosis and treatment gaps persist despite progress in infant vaccination. It evaluates the current state of prevention and care systems across this region.
Core methods
The authors analyze the existing prevention and care systems through a review of public health data, policy frameworks, and implementation strategies. They assess the coverage of birth-dose vaccination, maternal antiviral prophylaxis, and the availability of decentralized diagnosis and treatment.
Main results
The study reports that birth-dose vaccination coverage is very low, maternal antiviral prophylaxis is insufficient, and diagnosis and treatment systems are inadequate. These deficiencies allow mother-to-child transmission of HBV to sustain new chronic infections.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that an integrated package of interventions, including birth-dose vaccination, maternal screening, and prophylaxis, is necessary to eliminate hepatitis B in Africa. They emphasize the importance of political commitment to translate policy into practice.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the urgent need for strengthening prevention and care systems, particularly through birth-dose vaccination, maternal antiviral prophylaxis, and decentralized diagnosis and treatment, to reduce the burden of hepatitis B in Africa.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on an abstract-only analysis, which limits the depth of its evidence. However, it provides a clear and grounded assessment of current gaps in hepatitis B prevention and care systems in the African Region, supporting a moderate confidence level.

针对 慢性乙型肝炎 儿童显着肝纤维化的 CatBoost–SHAP 引导列线图的开发和内部验证

Development and internal validation of a CatBoost–SHAP-guided nomogram for significant liver fibrosis in children with chronic hepatitis B
作者： Yuanzhen Zhang, Tingting Wu, Yunan Chang, Xiaorong Peng, Kai Zhu, Yijing Liu, Tingting Shang, Tao Qin, Jing Zhu, Jiao Tian
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
背景 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 儿童可能会出现严重的肝纤维化,但肝活检是侵入性的,不适合重复评估。
设计与对象
我们开发了一种可解释的、非侵入性的儿科慢性乙型肝炎显着纤维化预测模型,并评估了基线风险分层是否与随后的 治疗 结果相关。
核心方法
使用贝叶斯超参数优化的 CatBoost 模型来识别信息最丰富的预测变量,并将这些变量纳入由 Shapley 附加解释 (SHAP) 指导的逻辑回归列线图中。
主要结果
96 名儿童 (41.4%) 发现明显纤维化。最终模型中保留了肝脏硬度测量、丙氨酸转氨酶和血小板计数。
解释与创新
The nomogram achieved an area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) of 0.828 (95% CI, 0.767–0.889) in the development cohort and 0.782 (95% CI, 0.637–0.927) in the held-out test cohort, with performance comparable to that of the CatBoost model ( P = 0.240).
科研与公卫意义
尽管在临床实施之前需要进行外部验证,但基线风险分层也与随后的 治疗 结果相关。
局限与证据强度
使用根据年龄和性别调整的广义估计方程以及卡普兰-迈耶分析来检查基线风险组和纵向结果之间的关联。

Development and internal validation of a CatBoost–SHAP-guided nomogram for significant liver fibrosis in children with chronic hepatitis B

Authors: Yuanzhen Zhang, Tingting Wu, Yunan Chang, Xiaorong Peng, Kai Zhu, Yijing Liu, Tingting Shang, Tao Qin, Jing Zhu, Jiao Tian
Original abstract (English)
Background Significant liver fibrosis can develop in children with chronic hepatitis B (CHB), yet liver biopsy is invasive and unsuitable for repeated assessment. We developed an interpretable, non-invasive prediction model for significant fibrosis in pediatric CHB and evaluated whether baseline risk stratification was associated with subsequent treatment outcomes. Methods This retrospective study included 232 children with CHB who underwent liver biopsy, with significant fibrosis defined as Scheuer stage S ≥ 2. Patients were randomly assigned to a development cohort (80%) and a held-out test set (20%). A CatBoost model with Bayesian hyperparameter optimization was used to identify the most informative predictors, and these variables were incorporated into a logistic regression nomogram guided by Shapley additive explanations (SHAP). Model performance was evaluated by discrimination, calibration, decision curve analysis, and DeLong testing. Associations between baseline risk groups and longitudinal outcomes were examined using generalized estimating equations adjusted for age and sex and Kaplan–Meier analyses. Results Significant fibrosis was identified in 96 children (41.4%). Liver stiffness measurement, alanine aminotransferase, and platelet count were retained in the final model. The nomogram achieved an area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) of 0.828 (95% CI, 0.767–0.889) in the development cohort and 0.782 (95% CI, 0.637–0.927) in the held-out test cohort, with performance comparable to that of the CatBoost model ( P = 0.240). Compared with the low-risk group, high-risk patients showed greater longitudinal reductions in alanine and aspartate aminotransferase, whereas longitudinal changes in liver stiffness measurement and hepatitis B virus DNA were similar between groups. Conversely, low-risk patients achieved virological suppression, HBeAg seroconversion, and biochemical response earlier than those classified as high risk. Conclusions An interpretable three-variable prediction model based on CatBoost-guided feature selection accurately identified significant fibrosis in children with CHB while maintaining performance comparable to the machine-learning model. Baseline risk stratification was also associated with subsequent treatment outcomes, although external validation is needed before clinical implementation.
View research seven-element analysis

乙肝清除后肝细胞癌经动脉化疗栓塞治疗后的再激活风险高于慢性感染

Resolved hepatitis B is associated with higher reactivation risk than chronic infection after chemoembolization for hepatocellular carcinoma
作者： Dong Yun Kim, Jaehong Jeong, Jung Pyo Hong, Jae Seung Lee, Mi Na Kim, Beom Kyung Kim, Seung Up Kim, Jun Yong Park, Sang Hoon Ahn, Hye Won Lee
摘要中文翻译背景 经动脉化疗栓塞(TACE)是肝细胞癌(HCC)的基石治疗,然而乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)再激活风险,尤其是在既往感染患者中,仍未被完全阐明,国际指南对其风险分类存在不一致。我们假设,既往感染已解决的HBV患者接受TACE后的再激活风险高于国际指南目前的估计。目的 确定接受TACE治疗HBV相关HCC患者的HBV再激活发病率和危险因素。方法 我们回顾性分析了2007-2025年间在三级医院接受TACE治疗的HBV相关HCC患者。接受基线抗病毒药物治疗的患者被排除。具有明确血清学状态的患者(n = 774)被分为慢性感染组[乙型肝炎表面抗原(HBsAg)阳性,n = 727]和已解决感染组(HBsAg阴性/抗乙型肝炎核心抗体阳性,n = 47)。累积发病率和危险因素采用Kaplan-Meier分析和Cox回归进行评估,稳健性通过多重插补、竞争风险和抗病毒药物无敏感性分析进行评价。结果 在中位随访16.7个月期间,HBV再激活发生在60例患者(总体6.1%)。已解决HBV状态的患者再激活率更高(21.3% 对比 6.9%;P < 0.001)。多因素分析确定已解决的HBV感染(调整风险比:3.42;95% CI:2.11-5.54;P < 0.001)为独立危险因素。结论 接受TACE的患者中约6%发生HBV再激活,已解决感染状态使风险增加超过3倍,再激活发病率超过20%。这些结果支持对HBsAg阴性/抗乙型肝炎核心抗体阳性且接受TACE的患者进行预防性抗病毒药物治疗,而非仅监测。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在确定经动脉化疗栓塞治疗HBV相关HCC患者的HBV再激活率及其风险因素,以解决国际指南中对既往感染者再激活风险尚未完全明了的问题。
设计与对象
本研究回顾性分析了2007年至2025年间在一家三级医院接受经动脉化疗栓塞治疗的HBV相关HCC患者的资料,排除了接受基线抗病毒治疗的患者,并将患者分为慢性感染组和清除感染组。
核心方法
使用Kaplan-Meier分析和Cox回归评估累积再激活率和风险因素,并通过多重插补、竞争风险和抗病毒治疗前敏感性分析评估其稳健性。
主要结果
中位随访16.7个月期间,60名患者(6.1%)发生HBV再激活,清除感染状态的患者再激活率更高(21.3% vs 6.9%;P < 0.001)。
解释与创新
这些发现支持在HBsAg阴性/抗乙型肝炎核心抗体阳性患者中进行预防性抗病毒治疗,而不是仅监测,突显了清除感染状态的再激活风险更高。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果为HBV相关HCC患者经动脉化疗栓塞治疗的预防策略、监测策略和治疗策略提供了指导,强调了在清除感染状态中进行预防性抗病毒治疗的必要性。
局限与证据强度
本研究受限于回顾性设计和潜在的选择偏倚,尽管发现清除感染状态的再激活风险更高,但仍需进一步的随机对照试验来确认这一发现。

Resolved hepatitis B is associated with higher reactivation risk than chronic infection after chemoembolization for hepatocellular carcinoma

Authors: Dong Yun Kim, Jaehong Jeong, Jung Pyo Hong, Jae Seung Lee, Mi Na Kim, Beom Kyung Kim, Seung Up Kim, Jun Yong Park, Sang Hoon Ahn, Hye Won Lee
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND Transarterial chemoembolization (TACE) is a cornerstone treatment for hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC), yet the hepatitis B virus (HBV) reactivation risk, particularly among patients with prior infection, remains incompletely characterized, with international guidelines providing discordant risk classifications. We hypothesized that patients with resolved HBV infection undergoing TACE face a higher reactivation risk than currently estimated by international guidelines. AIM To determine the incidence and risk factors of HBV reactivation in patients undergoing TACE for HBV-related HCC. METHODS We retrospectively analyzed patients who underwent TACE for HBV-related HCC at a tertiary hospital during 2007-2025. Patients receiving baseline antiviral therapy were excluded. Patients with definitive serostatus (n = 774) were stratified into chronic-infection [hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg)-positive, n = 727] and resolved-infection (HBsAg-negative/anti-hepatitis B core antibody-positive, n = 47) groups. Cumulative incidences and risk factors were assessed using Kaplan-Meier analysis and Cox regression, with robustness evaluated through multiple imputation, competing-risks, and antiviral-naive sensitivity analyses. RESULTS During a median follow-up of 16.7 months, HBV reactivation occurred in 60 patients (6.1% overall). Patients with resolved HBV status had a higher reactivation rate (21.3% vs 6.9%; P CONCLUSION HBV reactivation occurred in approximately 6% of patients undergoing TACE, with resolved infection status conferring > 3-fold higher risk and reactivation incidence exceeding 20%. These findings support prophylactic antiviral therapy rather than monitoring alone for HBsAg-negative/anti-hepatitis B core antibody-positive patients undergoing TACE.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to determine the incidence and risk factors of HBV reactivation in patients undergoing TACE for HBV-related HCC, addressing the incompletely characterized reactivation risk among patients with prior infection, as per international guidelines.
Design and population
The study retrospectively analyzed patients who underwent TACE for HBV-related HCC at a tertiary hospital from 2007 to 2025, excluding those receiving baseline antiviral therapy, and stratified patients into chronic-infection and resolved-infection groups.
Core methods
Cumulative incidences and risk factors were assessed using Kaplan-Meier analysis and Cox regression, with robustness evaluated through multiple imputation, competing-risks, and antiviral-naive sensitivity analyses.
Main results
During a median follow-up of 16.7 months, HBV reactivation occurred in 60 patients (6.1% overall), with patients with resolved HBV status having a higher reactivation rate (21.3% vs 6.9%; P < 0.001).
Interpretation and novelty
The findings support prophylactic antiviral therapy rather than monitoring alone for HBsAg-negative/anti-hepatitis B core antibody-positive patients undergoing TACE, highlighting the higher reactivation risk in resolved infection status.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results inform surveillance, diagnosis, and treatment strategies for HBV-related HCC patients undergoing TACE, emphasizing the need for prophylactic antiviral therapy in resolved infection status.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and potential selection bias, and the findings suggest a higher reactivation risk in resolved infection status, but further randomized controlled trials are needed to confirm this.

马拉维布兰太尔艾滋病毒感染者对乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎的了解和态度:一项横断面调查

Knowledge and attitudes towards hepatitis B and hepatitis C among people living with HIV in Blantyre, Malawi: a cross-sectional survey
作者： Akim Nelson Bwanali, Tapiwa Maoni, Chana Khuluza, Mzati Mphepo, Doreen Vitsitsi, Watipaso Kamanga, Jeany Safari, Steward Mudenda, Samuel Lawrent Mpinganjira, Thomas Nyirenda
摘要中文翻译简介 撒哈拉以南非洲地区艾滋病毒 患病率 的高感染率加剧了乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎的高负担。HIV-病毒性肝炎合并感染会增加肝脏相关并发症的风险,但 HIV 感染者 (PLWHIV) 的知识、态度和检测实践仍知之甚少。本研究评估了马拉维布兰太尔的感染者和艾滋病毒感染者对病毒性肝炎检测服务的知识、态度和利用情况。方法对 2025 年 1 月至 2 月期间在城市和城郊抗逆转录病毒治疗诊所就诊的 299 名感染者和艾滋病毒感染者进行了横断面调查。通过非概率连续抽样招募参与者,并完成结构化调查问卷,评估社会人口特征、检测实践、知识和对病毒性肝炎的态度。使用 SPSS 27 版对数据进行分析。描述性统计总结了参与者特征和研究结果,而卡方和 Fisher 精确检验评估了分类变量之间的关联。结果 大多数参与者为女性 (74.3%)、年龄 35-49 岁 (46.5%) 和已婚 (66.6%)。总体而言,63.5%的参与者从未接受过病毒性肝炎检测,65.6%的参与者表现出知识匮乏,85.6%的参与者表示对预防持积极态度。被认定为性少数群体(男同性恋或女同性恋)的参与者的知识水平高于异性恋参与者(p = 0.039),而就业参与者中积极态度的频率更高(p = 0.005)。讨论 研究结果表明,尽管人们对病毒性肝炎预防普遍持积极态度,但仍存在巨大的知识差距和检测率较低。将有针对性的肝炎教育和常规检测纳入艾滋病毒护理可能会改善获得预防和检测服务的机会。进一步的研究应评估艾滋病毒携带者综合教育和检测干预措施的有效性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估马拉维布兰太尔艾滋病毒感染者对病毒性肝炎检测服务的知识、态度和利用情况,因为撒哈拉以南非洲地区艾滋病毒 患病率 的高感染率加剧了乙型和丙型肝炎的高负担。
设计与对象
对 2025 年 1 月至 2 月期间在马拉维布兰太尔城市和城郊抗逆转录病毒治疗诊所就诊的 299 名艾滋病毒感染者进行了一项横断面调查。
核心方法
通过非概率连续抽样招募参与者,并完成结构化调查问卷,评估社会人口特征、检测实践、知识和对病毒性肝炎的态度。使用 SPSS 27 版分析数据。
主要结果
大多数参与者是女性(74.3%),年龄在 35-49 岁(46.5%),已婚(66.6%)。总体而言,63.5%的参与者从未接受过病毒性肝炎检测,65.6%的参与者表现出知识匮乏,85.6%的参与者表示对预防持积极态度。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,尽管人们对病毒性肝炎预防普遍持积极态度,但仍存在巨大的知识差距和检测率较低。
科研与公卫意义
将有针对性的肝炎教育和常规检测纳入艾滋病毒护理可能会改善获得预防和检测服务的机会。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到横截面设计和非概率抽样的限制,这可能会影响普遍性。证据是中等的,因为它提供了描述性统计数据和变量之间的关联。

Knowledge and attitudes towards hepatitis B and hepatitis C among people living with HIV in Blantyre, Malawi: a cross-sectional survey

Authors: Akim Nelson Bwanali, Tapiwa Maoni, Chana Khuluza, Mzati Mphepo, Doreen Vitsitsi, Watipaso Kamanga, Jeany Safari, Steward Mudenda, Samuel Lawrent Mpinganjira, Thomas Nyirenda
Original abstract (English)
Introduction The high burden of hepatitis B and C is compounded by the high prevalence of HIV in sub-Saharan Africa. HIV–viral hepatitis co-infection increases the risk of liver-related complications, yet knowledge, attitudes, and testing practices among people living with HIV (PLWHIV) remain poorly characterized. This study assessed knowledge, attitudes, and utilization of viral hepatitis testing services among PLWHIV in Blantyre, Malawi.Methods A cross-sectional survey was conducted among 299 PLWHIV attending urban and peri-urban antiretroviral therapy clinics between January and February 2025. Participants were recruited through non-probability consecutive sampling and completed structured questionnaires assessing sociodemographic characteristics, testing practices, knowledge, and attitudes towards viral hepatitis. Data were analyzed using SPSS version 27. Descriptive statistics summarized participant characteristics and study outcomes, while chi-square and Fisher’s exact tests assessed associations between categorical variables.Results Most participants were female (74.3%), aged 35–49 years (46.5%), and married (66.6%). Overall, 63.5% of participants had never been tested for viral hepatitis, 65.6% demonstrated poor knowledge, and 85.6% expressed positive attitudes towards prevention. Knowledge was higher among participants identifying as sexual minorities (gay or lesbian) than among heterosexual participants (p = 0.039), while positive attitudes were more frequent among employed participants (p = 0.005).Discussion The findings demonstrate substantial knowledge gaps and low testing uptake despite generally positive attitudes towards viral hepatitis prevention. Integrating targeted hepatitis education and routine testing into HIV care may improve access to prevention and testing services. Further research should evaluate the effectiveness of integrated education and testing interventions among PLWHIV.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess knowledge, attitudes, and utilization of viral hepatitis testing services among people living with HIV in Blantyre, Malawi, as the high burden of hepatitis B and C is compounded by the high prevalence of HIV in sub-Saharan Africa.
Design and population
A cross-sectional survey was conducted among 299 people living with HIV attending urban and peri-urban antiretroviral therapy clinics in Blantyre, Malawi, between January and February 2025.
Core methods
Participants were recruited through non-probability consecutive sampling and completed structured questionnaires assessing sociodemographic characteristics, testing practices, knowledge, and attitudes towards viral hepatitis. Data were analyzed using SPSS version 27.
Main results
Most participants were female (74.3%), aged 35–49 years (46.5%), and married (66.6%). Overall, 63.5% of participants had never been tested for viral hepatitis, 65.6% demonstrated poor knowledge, and 85.6% expressed positive attitudes towards prevention.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate substantial knowledge gaps and low testing uptake despite generally positive attitudes towards viral hepatitis prevention.
Scientific and public-health significance
Integrating targeted hepatitis education and routine testing into HIV care may improve access to prevention and testing services.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design and non-probability sampling, which may affect generalisability. The evidence is moderate as it provides descriptive statistics and associations between variables.

慢性乙型肝炎 患者的社会支持和疾病特异性生活质量与自我管理的关联:一项多中心横断面研究

Associations of social support and disease-specific quality of life with self-management among patients with chronic hepatitis B: a multicenter cross-sectional study
作者： Wen-Ting Yi, Ling-Ling Li, Mao--Ting Huang, Hui-Ling Yan, Jia-Jun Liu, Yi-Jing Chen, Xiao-Yan Huang, Jing-Zhi Yang, Ke-Hao Zhao, Ying Zeng
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探讨 慢性乙型肝炎 患者的社会支持和特定疾病生活质量领域与自我管理的独立关联。
设计与对象
这项多中心横断面研究纳入了 2025 年 3 月至 10 月在中国衡阳市三所三级医院通过便利抽样招募的 583 名 慢性乙型肝炎 患者。
核心方法
参与者完成了慢性乙型肝炎自我管理量表(CHBSMS)、社会支持评定量表(SSRS)和慢性乙型肝炎生活质量量表(CHBQOL)。使用分层多元线性回归、HC3 稳健标准误和敏感性分析。
主要结果
CHBSMS 平均总分为 85.03 ± 19.38。社会人口统计模型得出的 R 2 为 0.194。添加临床和医疗保健利用因素增加了解释方差 (Δ R 2 = 0.083),添加社会支持和四个 CHBQOL 领域评分进一步增加了解释方差 (Δ R 2 = 0.102),最终 R 2 为 0.379。
解释与创新
In the fully adjusted model, social support (B = 0.577, 95% CI: 0.416–0.739, p < 0.001), the emotional symptoms domain (B = 0.170, 95% CI: 0.039–0.301, p = 0.011), and the social stigma domain (B = 0.235, 95% CI: 0.082–0.388, p = 0.003) were positively associated with self-management scores.
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,加强社会支持、解决情绪症状和社会耻辱可以改善 慢性乙型肝炎 患者的自我管理,从而有可能为有针对性的干预措施和公共卫生策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到横断面设计和方便抽样的限制,但稳健性和敏感性分析并没有实质性改变主要发现,支持对结果的适度信心。

Associations of social support and disease-specific quality of life with self-management among patients with chronic hepatitis B: a multicenter cross-sectional study

Authors: Wen-Ting Yi, Ling-Ling Li, Mao--Ting Huang, Hui-Ling Yan, Jia-Jun Liu, Yi-Jing Chen, Xiao-Yan Huang, Jing-Zhi Yang, Ke-Hao Zhao, Ying Zeng
Original abstract (English)
Background Self-management behaviors are central to long-term care for chronic hepatitis B (CHB), but the independent associations of social support and disease-specific quality-of-life domains with self-management remain unclear. This study assessed self-management and its associated factors among patients with CHB. Methods This multicenter cross-sectional study included 583 patients with CHB recruited by convenience sampling at three tertiary hospitals in Hengyang, China, from March to October 2025. Participants completed the Chronic Hepatitis B Self-Management Scale (CHBSMS), Social Support Rating Scale (SSRS), and Chronic Hepatitis B Quality of Life Instrument (CHBQOL). Hierarchical multiple linear regression, HC3 robust standard errors, and sensitivity analyses were used. Results The mean total CHBSMS score was 85.03 ± 19.38. The sociodemographic model yielded an R 2 of 0.194. Adding clinical and healthcare utilization factors increased the explained variance (Δ R 2 = 0.083), and adding social support and the four CHBQOL domain scores further increased the explained variance (Δ R 2 = 0.102), yielding a final R 2 of 0.379. In the fully adjusted model, social support ( B = 0.577, 95% CI: 0.416–0.739, p < 0.001), the emotional symptoms domain ( B = 0.170, 95% CI: 0.039–0.301, p = 0.011), and the social stigma domain ( B = 0.235, 95% CI: 0.082–0.388, p = 0.003) were positively associated with self-management scores. Higher scores in the latter two domains indicated fewer emotional symptoms and less social stigma, respectively. For a 1-SD higher value of each predictor, the corresponding CHBSMS differences were 4.79, 1.90, and 2.15 points for social support, emotional symptoms, and social stigma, respectively, equivalent to 4.79, 1.90, and 2.15% of the score range. The somatic symptoms and belief domains were not significantly associated with self-management. Robustness and sensitivity analyses did not materially alter the main findings. Conclusion Among patients with CHB recruited from three tertiary hospitals, social support, the emotional symptoms domain, and the social stigma domain were associated with self-management after adjustment for sociodemographic, clinical, and healthcare utilization factors. Further longitudinal research is needed to clarify the temporal direction and clinical relevance of these associations.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to explore the independent associations of social support and disease-specific quality-of-life domains with self-management among patients with chronic hepatitis B.
Design and population
This multicenter cross-sectional study included 583 patients with chronic hepatitis B recruited by convenience sampling at three tertiary hospitals in Hengyang, China, from March to October 2025.
Core methods
Participants completed the Chronic Hepatitis B Self-Management Scale (CHBSMS), Social Support Rating Scale (SSRS), and Chronic Hepatitis B Quality of Life Instrument (CHBQOL). Hierarchical multiple linear regression, HC3 robust standard errors, and sensitivity analyses were used.
Main results
The mean total CHBSMS score was 85.03 ± 19.38. The sociodemographic model yielded an R 2 of 0.194. Adding clinical and healthcare utilization factors increased the explained variance (Δ R 2 = 0.083), and adding social support and the four CHBQOL domain scores further increased the explained variance (Δ R 2 = 0.102), yielding a final R 2 of 0.379.
Interpretation and novelty
In the fully adjusted model, social support (B = 0.577, 95% CI: 0.416–0.739, p < 0.001), the emotional symptoms domain (B = 0.170, 95% CI: 0.039–0.301, p = 0.011), and the social stigma domain (B = 0.235, 95% CI: 0.082–0.388, p = 0.003) were positively associated with self-management scores.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that enhancing social support and addressing emotional symptoms and social stigma could improve self-management among patients with chronic hepatitis B, potentially informing targeted interventions and public health strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design and convenience sampling, but robustness and sensitivity analyses did not materially alter the main findings, supporting a moderate confidence in the results.

加纳沃尔特地区献血者的 血清流行率 和输血传播感染趋势:一项为期四年的回顾性研究。

Seroprevalence and trends of transfusion-transmissible infections among blood donors in the Volta Region, Ghana: A four-year retrospective study.
作者： Shadrack Ayanku, Eyram Kuma Hanu, Lois Akuba, Caroline Tetteh, Timothy Yao Akweh, David Annor Kwasie, Stephen Bekpenaneso Bawu, Klutse Fianko, Anthony Zunoo Dongdem
摘要中文翻译背景:输血是一种挽救生命的干预措施;然而,人类免疫缺陷病毒(HIV)、乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)、丙型肝炎病毒(HCV)和梅毒等输血传播感染(TTI)仍然是主要的公共卫生问题,特别是在低收入和中等收入国家。本研究评估了加纳沃尔特地区献血者的 血清流行率 和 TTI 时间趋势,并确定与血清阳性相关的人口因素。方法:使用 2020 年 1 月至 2023 年 12 月期间 Ho 教学医院和 Hohoe 地区医院献血者的二手数据进行了一项回顾性横断面研究。使用 STATA 版本 17 提取并分析了 6,147 名合格献血者的数据。描述性统计总结了 患病率,而卡方或 Fisher 精确检验评估了关联性。使用多变量逻辑回归以 5% 的显着性水平确定与 TTI 血清阳性相关的因素。结果:共有6147名献血者纳入分析,其中95.1%为男性。输血传播感染 (TTI) 的总体 患病率 为 8.1%,其中梅毒 (3.6%) 是最常见的感染,其次是 HBV (1.8%)、HCV (1.8%) 和 HIV (1.0%)。所有感染均于 2022 年达到顶峰,然后于 2023 年下降。年龄和捐赠年份的增加与特定 TTI(尤其是 HBV、HCV 和梅毒)的血清阳性显着相关。在 Hohoe,与女性捐赠者相比,男性捐赠者感染 HCV 的几率显着较低(aOR = 0.13;95% CI:0.06-0.28)。结论:虽然 TTI 患病率 相对较低,但时间上的增加和与年龄相关的差异突出表明需要加强捐献者筛选、有针对性地招募自愿捐献者以及加强 监测 策略以确保血液安全。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估加纳沃尔特地区献血者中输血传播感染 (TTI) 的 血清流行率 和时间趋势,并确定与血清阳性相关的人口因素。
设计与对象
使用 2020 年 1 月至 2023 年 12 月期间 Ho 教学医院和 Hohoe 地区医院献血者的二手数据进行了一项回顾性横断面研究。提取并分析了 6,147 名合格献血者的数据。
核心方法
描述性统计总结了 患病率,而卡方或费舍尔精确检验则评估关联性。使用多变量逻辑回归以 5% 的显着性水平确定与 TTI 血清阳性相关的因素。
主要结果
输血传播感染 (TTI) 的总体 患病率 为 8.1%,其中梅毒 (3.6%) 是最常见的感染,其次是 HBV (1.8%)、HCV (1.8%) 和 HIV (1.0%)。所有感染均于 2022 年达到顶峰,然后于 2023 年下降。年龄和捐赠年份的增加与特定 TTI(尤其是 HBV、HCV 和梅毒)的血清阳性显着相关。
解释与创新
该研究强调,鉴于时间上的增加和与年龄相关的差异,需要加强捐献者筛选、有针对性地招募自愿捐献者以及加强 监测 策略以确保血液安全。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可为有针对性的招募策略和 监测 计划提供信息,以降低加纳沃尔特地区通过输血感染 HBV 传播 的风险。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于二手数据,这可能会限制数据质量和完整性。然而,证据是强有力的,因为它提供了对 TTI 患病率 和相关因素的直接观察。

Seroprevalence and trends of transfusion-transmissible infections among blood donors in the Volta Region, Ghana: A four-year retrospective study.

Authors: Shadrack Ayanku, Eyram Kuma Hanu, Lois Akuba, Caroline Tetteh, Timothy Yao Akweh, David Annor Kwasie, Stephen Bekpenaneso Bawu, Klutse Fianko, Anthony Zunoo Dongdem
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Blood transfusion is a life-saving intervention; however, transfusion-transmissible infections (TTIs) such as human immunodeficiency virus (HIV), hepatitis B virus (HBV), hepatitis C virus (HCV), and syphilis remain major public health concerns, particularly in low- and middle-income countries. This study assessed the seroprevalence and temporal trends of TTIs among blood donors in the Volta Region of Ghana and to identify the demographic factors associated with seropositivity. METHODS: A retrospective cross-sectional study was conducted using secondary data from blood donors at Ho Teaching Hospital and Hohoe Regional Hospital between January 2020 and December 2023. Data from 6,147 eligible donors were extracted and analyzed using STATA version 17. Descriptive statistics summarized prevalence, while chi-square or Fisher's exact tests assessed associations. Multivariable logistic regression was used to identify factors associated with TTI seropositivity at a 5% significance level. RESULTS: A total of 6,147 blood donors were included in the analysis, of whom 95.1% were male. The overall prevalence of transfusion-transmissible infections (TTIs) was 8.1%, with syphilis (3.6%) being the most prevalent infection, followed by HBV (1.8%), HCV (1.8%), and HIV (1.0%). All infections peaked in 2022 before declining in 2023. Increasing age and year of donation were significantly associated with seropositivity for specific TTIs, particularly HBV, HCV, and syphilis. In Hohoe, male donors had significantly lower odds of HCV infection compared with female donors (aOR = 0.13; 95% CI: 0.06-0.28). CONCLUSIONS: Although TTI prevalence was relatively low, temporal increases and age-related disparities highlight the need for strengthened donor screening, targeted recruitment of voluntary donors, and enhanced surveillance strategies to ensure blood safety.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to assess the seroprevalence and temporal trends of transfusion-transmissible infections (TTIs) among blood donors in the Volta Region of Ghana and to identify the demographic factors associated with seropositivity.
Design and population
A retrospective cross-sectional study was conducted using secondary data from blood donors at Ho Teaching Hospital and Hohoe Regional Hospital between January 2020 and December 2023. Data from 6,147 eligible donors were extracted and analyzed.
Core methods
Descriptive statistics summarized prevalence, while chi-square or Fisher's exact tests assessed associations. Multivariable logistic regression was used to identify factors associated with TTI seropositivity at a 5% significance level.
Main results
The overall prevalence of transfusion-transmissible infections (TTIs) was 8.1%, with syphilis (3.6%) being the most prevalent infection, followed by HBV (1.8%), HCV (1.8%), and HIV (1.0%). All infections peaked in 2022 before declining in 2023. Increasing age and year of donation were significantly associated with seropositivity for specific TTIs, particularly HBV, HCV, and syphilis.
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlights the need for strengthened donor screening, targeted recruitment of voluntary donors, and enhanced surveillance strategies to ensure blood safety, as temporal increases and age-related disparities were observed.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform targeted recruitment strategies and surveillance programs to reduce the risk of HBV transmission through blood transfusions in the Volta Region of Ghana.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on secondary data, which may introduce limitations in data quality and completeness. However, the evidence is strong as it provides direct observations on TTI prevalence and associated factors.

Hepatitis B update: enabling progress towards elimination
作者： Jane Davies, Marilou Capati, Nicole L. Allard, Nicole Allard
