查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了改进乙型肝炎管理和消除策略的需要,强调了澳大利亚诊断和治疗 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 的挑战。
设计与对象
该研究回顾了澳大利亚乙型肝炎管理实践,重点关注原住民和托雷斯海峡岛民、医护人员以及出生在高 CHB-患病率 国家的个人等高风险群体。据报道,32.6% 的慢性乙型肝炎患者不知道自己的 诊断。
核心方法
该研究结合了国家数据、政策分析和项目评估来评估乙型肝炎检测、诊断 和 治疗 的当前实践和障碍。它引用了第四个国家乙型肝炎战略 2025-2030 和乙型肝炎 PAST 计划作为干预措施的例子。
主要结果
研究发现,尽管有有效的治疗方法,但只有 13% 的慢性乙型肝炎患者接受 抗病毒药物 治疗 治疗。它还强调,北领地的原住民具有独特的亚基因型 (HBV/C4),与更快进展为肝硬化和增加患肝细胞癌 (HCC) 的风险相关。
解释与创新
作者解释说,目前基于风险的检测方法不足以在 2030 年消除乙型肝炎,对所有成年人进行普遍检测是必要的一步。新颖之处在于强调社区特定的亚基因型及其对 治疗 紧迫性的影响。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明需要进行普遍检测并改善慢性乙型肝炎的 治疗 的可及性,特别是在高危人群中。他们还强调了在乙型肝炎护理中解决社会决定因素和文化因素的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于现有的国家数据和政策文件,可能无法捕捉现实世界的实施挑战。证据是温和的,因为它依赖于观察数据而不是实验结果。

Hepatitis B update: enabling progress towards elimination

Authors: Jane Davies, Marilou Capati, Nicole L. Allard, Nicole Allard
Original abstract (English)
People living with CHB are often asymptomatic and may have normal liver function tests, so an active case-finding approach is needed. However, this approach is not effective enough to meet the goal of eliminating hepatitis B by 2030 because, currently in Australia, an estimated 32.6% of people living with CHB are unaware of their diagnosis.2 Hence, the Fourth National Hepatitis B Strategy 2025â2030 recommends universal testing for all adults as a priority action to ensure all Australians know their hepatitis B status.6 There are clear recommended care pathways for hepatitis B prevention and management for all combinations of hepatitis B antigen and antibody results (Table 1). Table 1 Interpreting and acting on hepatitis B test results9 | Test | Result | Interpretation | Hepatitis B status | Action | | HbsAg | Negative | Susceptible | Nonimmune | Add hepatitis B status to patient medical summary Offer hepatitis B vaccination If the person requires proof of immunity (i.e. Hepatitis B update: enabling progress towards elimination. Aust Prescr 2026;49:160-7.https://doi.org/10.18773/austprescr.2026.043 Hepatitis B is a common vaccine-preventable and treatable condition. Chronic hepatitis B is often asymptomatic and can be missed if the diagnosis is not actively considered. The World Health Organization and the Australian Government have committed to eliminate hepatitis B as a public health threat by 2030. The tools required to achieve this are available: an effective vaccine, accurate blood tests for diagnosis and monitoring, effective antiviral treatments and access to ultrasound to enable early diagnosis of hepatocellular carcinoma. Risk-based testing approaches are not effective enough to enable Australia to achieve elimination targets; hence, the Fourth National Hepatit...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the need for improved hepatitis B management and elimination strategies, highlighting the challenges in diagnosing and treating chronic hepatitis B (CHB) in Australia.
Design and population
The study is a review of hepatitis B management practices in Australia, focusing on high-risk groups such as Aboriginal and Torres Strait Islander people, healthcare workers, and individuals born in high CHB-prevalence countries. It reports that 32.6% of people living with CHB are unaware of their diagnosis.
Core methods
The study uses a combination of national data, policy analysis, and program evaluations to assess current practices and barriers in hepatitis B testing, diagnosis, and treatment. It references the Fourth National Hepatitis B Strategy 2025–2030 and the Hep B PAST program as examples of interventions.
Main results
The study found that only 13% of people with CHB are on antiviral treatment, despite the availability of effective therapies. It also highlights that Aboriginal people in the Northern Territory have a unique subgenotype (HBV/C4) associated with faster progression to cirrhosis and increased risk of hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that the current risk-based testing approach is insufficient for eliminating hepatitis B by 2030, and that universal testing for all adults is a necessary step. The novelty lies in the emphasis on community-specific subgenotypes and their implications for treatment urgency.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the need for universal testing and improved access to treatment for CHB, particularly in high-risk populations. They also highlight the importance of addressing social determinants and cultural factors in hepatitis B care.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on existing national data and policy documents, which may not capture real-world implementation challenges. The evidence is moderate, as it relies on observational data rather than experimental results.

ESQUEMA DE VACUNACIÓN DEL PERSONAL SANITARIO PARA COVID-19, HEPATITIS B Y TÉTANOS DEL HOSPITAL GENERAL PEDIÁTRICO “NIÑOS DE ACOSTA ÑU” DE MARZO A ABRIL DEL 2023

ESQUEMA DE VACUNACIÓN DEL PERSONAL SANITARIO PARA COVID-19, HEPATITIS B Y TÉTANOS DEL HOSPITAL GENERAL PEDIÁTRICO “NIÑOS DE ACOSTA ÑU” DE MARZO A ABRIL DEL 2023
作者： Ricardo José Acosta Torreani, Ingrid María Cristaldo Acosta, Ángel Rafael Penayo Ferreira, Lígia Maria Oliveira de Souza
摘要中文翻译简介:传染病对医护人员在从事专业活动时构成重要风险。然而,在很多情况下,通过系统性、预防性的疫苗接种,这些疾病是可以有效预防的。目的:确定 2023 年 3 月至 4 月期间,“Niños de Acosta Ñu”综合儿科医院医护人员接种 COVID-19、乙型肝炎和破伤风疫苗的频率。方法:进行了一项描述性、非实验性、定量、横断面和前瞻性研究。分析的变量包括年龄、性别、居住地、工作年限、专业类别、遵守疫苗接种时间表、疾病发生情况、疾病类型、疾病严重程度以及对疫苗的认知。使用数字 Google Forms 问卷收集数据,随后输入 Microsoft Excel 2016,并使用 SPSS 21 版进行统计分析。研究方案得到了伦理委员会的批准。结果:平均年龄为 33 岁 (±5.5)。在所有参与者中,83.1% (212/255) 是女性。从居住地来看,57.3%(146/255)来自中央部门。工作年限中位数为 5 年 (1-21)。关于 COVID-19 疫苗接种,45.5% 已完成 疫苗接种 时间表,而 55.5% 的时间表不完整。对于乙型肝炎,85.9% (219/255) 已完成 疫苗接种 时间表。关于破伤风 疫苗接种,96.5% (246/255) 完成了 疫苗接种 时间表。总体而言,83.5% 的参与者报告感染了 COVID-19,而 16.5% 的参与者未感染该疾病。结论:“Niños de Acosta Ñu”综合儿科医院的医护人员使用完整的 COVID-19 疫苗接种 时间表的频率较低(45.5%),而针对乙型肝炎(85​​.9%)和破伤风(96.5%)的完整 疫苗接种 时间表的频率较高。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
传染病对医护人员进行专业活动构成重要风险。然而,在很多情况下,通过系统性、预防性的疫苗接种,这些疾病是可以有效预防的。
设计与对象
2023 年 3 月至 4 月,在“Niños de Acosta Ñu”综合儿科医院进行了一项描述性、非实验性、定量、横断面和前瞻性研究。该研究包括 255 名医护人员。
核心方法
使用数字 Google Forms 问卷收集数据,随后输入 Microsoft Excel 2016,并使用 SPSS 21 版进行统计分析。研究方案得到了伦理委员会的批准。
主要结果
关于 COVID-19 疫苗接种,45.5% 已完成 疫苗接种 时间表,而 55.5% 的时间表不完整。对于乙型肝炎,85.9% (219/255) 已完成 疫苗接种 时间表。关于破伤风 疫苗接种,96.5% (246/255) 完成了 疫苗接种 时间表。
解释与创新
“Niños de Acosta Ñu”综合儿科医院的医护人员使用完整的 COVID-19 疫苗接种 时间表的频率较低 (45.5%),而针对乙型肝炎 (85.9%) 和破伤风 (96.5%) 的完整 疫苗接种 时间表的频率较高。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可为医护人员提供有针对性的 疫苗接种 计划,特别强调需要更好地遵守 COVID-19 疫苗接种 时间表。
局限与证据强度
该研究是描述性的、非实验性的,这限制了其建立因果关系的能力。由于横截面设计和自我报告偏差的可能性,证据是中等的。

ESQUEMA DE VACUNACIÓN DEL PERSONAL SANITARIO PARA COVID-19, HEPATITIS B Y TÉTANOS DEL HOSPITAL GENERAL PEDIÁTRICO “NIÑOS DE ACOSTA ÑU” DE MARZO A ABRIL DEL 2023

Authors: Ricardo José Acosta Torreani, Ingrid María Cristaldo Acosta, Ángel Rafael Penayo Ferreira, Lígia Maria Oliveira de Souza
Original abstract (English)
Introduction: Infectious diseases constitute an important risk for healthcare personnel during the performance of their professional activities. However, in many cases, these diseases can be effectively prevented through systematic and preventive vaccination. Objective: To determine the frequency of healthcare personnel vaccinated against COVID-19, hepatitis B, and tetanus at the General Pediatric Hospital “Niños de Acosta Ñu” from March to April 2023. Methodology: A descriptive, non-experimental, quantitative, cross-sectional, and prospective study was conducted. The variables analyzed were age, sex, place of residence, length of employment, professional category, compliance with the vaccination schedule, occurrence of disease, type of disease, disease severity, and perception of vaccines. Data were collected using a digital Google Forms questionnaire, subsequently entered into Microsoft Excel 2016, and statistically analyzed using SPSS, version 21. The study protocol was approved by the Ethics Committee. Results: The mean age was 33 years (±5.5). Of the total participants, 83.1% (212/255) were female. Regarding place of residence, 57.3% (146/255) were from the Central Department. The median length of employment was 5 years (1–21). Regarding COVID-19 vaccination, 45.5% had completed the vaccination schedule, whereas 55.5% had an incomplete schedule. For hepatitis B, 85.9% (219/255) had completed the vaccination schedule. Regarding tetanus vaccination, 96.5% (246/255) had completed the vaccination schedule. Overall, 83.5% of the participants reported having contracted COVID-19, while 16.5% had not contracted the disease. Conclusion: The frequency of healthcare personnel at the General Pediatric Hospital “Niños de Acosta Ñu” with a complete COVID-19 vaccination schedule was low (45.5%), whereas the frequency of complete vaccination against hepatitis B (85.9%) and tetanus (96.5%) was high.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Infectious diseases constitute an important risk for healthcare personnel during the performance of their professional activities. However, in many cases, these diseases can be effectively prevented through systematic and preventive vaccination.
Design and population
A descriptive, non-experimental, quantitative, cross-sectional, and prospective study was conducted at the General Pediatric Hospital “Niños de Acosta Ñu” from March to April 2023. The study included 255 healthcare personnel.
Core methods
Data were collected using a digital Google Forms questionnaire, subsequently entered into Microsoft Excel 2016, and statistically analyzed using SPSS, version 21. The study protocol was approved by the Ethics Committee.
Main results
Regarding COVID-19 vaccination, 45.5% had completed the vaccination schedule, whereas 55.5% had an incomplete schedule. For hepatitis B, 85.9% (219/255) had completed the vaccination schedule. Regarding tetanus vaccination, 96.5% (246/255) had completed the vaccination schedule.
Interpretation and novelty
The frequency of healthcare personnel at the General Pediatric Hospital “Niños de Acosta Ñu” with a complete COVID-19 vaccination schedule was low (45.5%), whereas the frequency of complete vaccination against hepatitis B (85.9%) and tetanus (96.5%) was high.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform targeted vaccination programs for healthcare personnel, particularly highlighting the need for improved adherence to the COVID-19 vaccination schedule.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is descriptive and non-experimental, which limits its ability to establish causal relationships. The evidence is moderate due to the cross-sectional design and potential for self-reporting bias.

基于斐波那契小波的乙型肝炎病毒 SEIR 模型建模与分析

Modeling and Analysis of SEIR Model of Hepatitis-\(B\) Virus via Fibonacci Wavelets
作者： Naied A. Nayied, Firdous A. Shah, Kottakkaran Sooppy Nisar
摘要中文翻译本研究旨在检查乙型肝炎模型中易感者、暴露者、感染者和康复者的动态。这是通过开发基于斐波那契小波的有效数值方案来解决肝炎-\(B\)模型来实现的。该方法采用斐波那契小波导出的运算矩阵将给定的微分方程组转换为代数方程组,并通过 MATLAB 中的牛顿-拉夫森方法求解。分析肝细胞死亡和病毒产生率对健康细胞、感染细胞和病毒载量的影响,以捕获疾病进展的关键方面。与ND求解和Haar小波方法的比较分析表明,该方法能产生准确、高效的结果。数值结果证实了斐波那契小波作为研究传染病动力学的可靠计算工具的潜力。

本研究旨在检查乙型肝炎模型中易感者、暴露者、感染者和康复者的动态。
核心方法
这是通过开发基于斐波那契小波的有效数值方案来解决肝炎-\(B\)模型来实现的。

提供的证据没有明确说明直接观察到的主要结果。

与ND求解和Haar小波方法的比较分析表明,该方法能产生准确、高效的结果。

Modeling and Analysis of SEIR Model of Hepatitis-\(B\) Virus via Fibonacci Wavelets

Authors: Naied A. Nayied, Firdous A. Shah, Kottakkaran Sooppy Nisar
Original abstract (English)
This study aims to examine the dynamics of susceptible, exposed, infected, and recovered individuals in the context of hepatitis-\(B\) model. This is achieved by developing an efficient numerical scheme based on Fibonacci wavelets for solving the hepatitis-\(B\) model. The proposed method employs operational matrices derived from Fibonacci wavelets to convert the given system of differential equations into a system of algebraic equations, solved via the Newton-Raphson method in MATLAB. The effects of hepatocyte death and virus production rates on healthy cells, infected cells, and viral load are analyzed to capture key aspects of disease progression. Comparative analysis with ND solve and Haar wavelet methods demonstrates that the proposed method yields accurate and efficient results. The numerical findings confirm the potential of Fibonacci wavelets as a reliable computational tool for studying infectious disease dynamics.

华支睾吸虫共感染增强 慢性乙型肝炎 患者多形核骨髓源性抑制细胞的扩增和代谢重编程

Clonorchis sinensis co-infection potentiates polymorphonuclear-myeloid-derived suppressor cells expansion and metabolic reprogramming in chronic hepatitis B patients
作者： Jie-Ru Qiu, Hong-Bin Zhang, Mei Shang, Jue Xu, Yue-Chun Fu, Yuan Liao, Bo Hu, Hui-Min Dong
摘要中文翻译背景已知华支睾吸虫(C. sinensis)合并感染会加速乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)患者的疾病进展并诱导免疫耐受,特别是在两种感染流行的地区。代谢重编程已成为免疫抑制的关键驱动因素。这些代谢变化通常是由骨髓源性抑制细胞(MDSC)精心策划的,这些细胞在慢性感染期间会扩张以促进免疫耐受。中华丝虫合并感染会重新编程代谢以有利于 MDSC 相关的抑制,而这一过程与正常肝酶水平的关系仍不清楚。目的 探讨中华支原体合并感染是否会诱导肝酶水平正常的 HBV 患者发生 MDSC 相关的代谢重编程。方法 在这项横断面研究中,总共招募了 117 名参与者,并分为四组:健康对照(n = 30)、单一感染中华支原体的参与者(n = 29)、单一感染 HBV 的参与者(n = 28)和混合感染的参与者(n = 30)。使用化学发光免疫测定法测量血清细胞因子谱,使用液相色谱-串联质谱法测量氨基酸代谢物,并使用流式细胞术测量MDSC子集。进行 Spearman 相关性和中介分析来探讨炎症、代谢和免疫之间的关系。结果 HBV单一感染和合并感染患者的肝酶和HBV DNA水平无统计学差异。然而,与HBV单一感染组相比,合并感染组的炎症细胞因子白细胞介素6(IL-6)和肿瘤坏死因子-α(TNF-α)水平较高,同时干扰素-γ(IFN-γ)水平明显较低。流式细胞术检测显示,多形核MDSC(PMN-MDSC)在共感染组中表现出最大的扩增,并且其比例与IL-6和TNF-α水平密切相关。在代谢方面,共同感染组的鸟氨酸与精氨酸的比率显着较高。中介分析表明,PMN-MDSC 约占 IL-6 对 IFN-γ 抑制总效应的 45%。中华线虫合并感染通过 IL-6/TNF-α 连接的 PMN-MDSC 扩增和鸟氨酸与精氨酸比率升高与免疫抑制相关,导致尽管肝酶水平正常,但 IFN-γ 的产生受到抑制。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探讨华支睾吸虫合并感染是否会在肝酶水平正常的 HBV 患者中诱导 MDSC 相关的代谢重编程。背景强调,已知中华支原体合并感染会加速乙型肝炎患者的疾病进展并诱导免疫耐受,特别是在流行地区。
设计与对象
这项横断面研究纳入了 117 名参与者,分为四组:健康对照(n = 30)、中华支原体单一感染参与者(n = 29)、HBV 单一感染参与者(n = 28)和混合感染参与者(n = 30)。
核心方法
使用化学发光免疫测定法测量血清细胞因子谱,使用液相色谱-串联质谱法测量氨基酸代谢物,并使用流式细胞术测量MDSC子集。流式细胞术检测显示,多形核MDSC(PMN-MDSC)在共感染组中表现出最大的扩增,并且其比例与IL-6和TNF-α水平密切相关。
主要结果
HBV单一感染和合并感染患者的肝酶和HBV DNA水平无统计学差异。然而,共同感染组的炎症细胞因子:白细胞介素-6 (IL-6) 和肿瘤坏死因子-α (TNF-α) 水平较高,同时干扰素-γ (IFN-γ) 水平明显较低。中介分析表明,PMN-MDSC 约占 IL-6 对 IFN-γ 抑制总效应的 45%。
解释与创新
作者解释说,中华线虫合并感染与通过 IL-6/TNF-α 连接的 PMN-MDSC 扩增和鸟氨酸与精氨酸比率升高而产生的免疫抑制有关,导致尽管肝酶水平正常,但 IFN-γ 的产生受到抑制。这一发现强调了共感染可能调节 HBV 患者 免疫应答 的新机制。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能有助于了解合并感染 HBV 患者的免疫调节,并可能指导未来基于免疫的治疗和针对合并感染情况的 监测 策略的研究。
局限与证据强度
该研究是横断面的,限制了其建立因果关系的能力。证据基于相关性和中介分析,可能无法完全捕捉感染与 免疫应答 之间复杂的相互作用。信心是中等的。

Clonorchis sinensis co-infection potentiates polymorphonuclear-myeloid-derived suppressor cells expansion and metabolic reprogramming in chronic hepatitis B patients

Authors: Jie-Ru Qiu, Hong-Bin Zhang, Mei Shang, Jue Xu, Yue-Chun Fu, Yuan Liao, Bo Hu, Hui-Min Dong
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND Clonorchis sinensis (C. sinensis ) co-infection is known to accelerate disease progression and induce immune tolerance in patients with hepatitis B virus (HBV), particularly in regions where both infections are endemic. Metabolic reprogramming has emerged as a key driver of immunosuppression. These metabolic shifts are often orchestrated by myeloid-derived suppressor cells (MDSCs), which expand during chronic infections to promote immune tolerance. sinensis co-infection reprograms metabolism to favor MDSC-linked suppression and how this process relates to normal liver enzyme levels remains unclear. AIM To investigate whether C. sinensis co-infection induces MDSC- linked metabolic reprogramming in patients with HBV and normal liver enzyme levels. METHODS In this cross-sectional study, a total of 117 participants were enrolled and assigned to four groups: Healthy controls (n = 30), C. sinensis mono-infected participants (n = 29), HBV mono-infected participants (n = 28), and co-infected participants (n = 30). Serum cytokine profiles were measured using chemiluminescence immunoassay, amino acid metabolites using liquid chromatography-tandem mass spectrometry, and MDSC subsets using flow cytometry. Spearman correlation and mediation analyses were performed to explore the relationships among inflammation, metabolism, and immunity. RESULTS HBV mono-infected and co-infected patients showed no statistically significant differences in liver enzyme and HBV DNA levels. However, compared with the HBV mono-infected group, the co-infected group had higher levels of the inflammatory cytokine: Interleukin-6 (IL-6) and tumor necrosis factor-α (TNF-α), accompanied by markedly lower interferon-γ (IFN-γ) levels. Flow cytometric assay revealed that polymorphonuclear MDSCs (PMN-MDSCs) showed the greatest expansion in the co-infected group, and their proportion correlated strongly with IL-6 and TNF-α levels. Metabolically, the co-infected group had a significantly higher ornithine-to-arginine ratio. Mediation analysis suggested that PMN-MDSCs accounted for approximately 45% of the total effect of IL-6 on IFN-γ suppression. sinensis co-infection was associated with immunosuppression through IL-6/TNF-α-linked PMN-MDSC expansion and elevated ornithine-to-arginine ratio, resulting in suppressed IFN-γ production despite normal liver enzyme levels.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate whether Clonorchis sinensis co-infection induces MDSC-linked metabolic reprogramming in patients with HBV and normal liver enzyme levels. The background highlights that C. sinensis co-infection is known to accelerate disease progression and induce immune tolerance in HBV patients, particularly in endemic regions.
Design and population
This cross-sectional study enrolled 117 participants divided into four groups: Healthy controls (n = 30), C. sinensis mono-infected participants (n = 29), HBV mono-infected participants (n = 28), and co-infected participants (n = 30).
Core methods
Serum cytokine profiles were measured using chemiluminescence immunoassay, amino acid metabolites using liquid chromatography-tandem mass spectrometry, and MDSC subsets using flow cytometry. Flow cytometric assay revealed that polymorphonuclear MDSCs (PMN-MDSCs) showed the greatest expansion in the co-infected group, and their proportion correlated strongly with IL-6 and TNF-α levels.
Main results
HBV mono-infected and co-infected patients showed no statistically significant differences in liver enzyme and HBV DNA levels. However, the co-infected group had higher levels of the inflammatory cytokine: Interleukin-6 (IL-6) and tumor necrosis factor-α (TNF-α), accompanied by markedly lower interferon-γ (IFN-γ) levels. Mediation analysis suggested that PMN-MDSCs accounted for approximately 45% of the total effect of IL-6 on IFN-γ suppression.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that C. sinensis co-infection was associated with immunosuppression through IL-6/TNF-α-linked PMN-MDSC expansion and elevated ornithine-to-arginine ratio, resulting in suppressed IFN-γ production despite normal liver enzyme levels. This finding highlights a novel mechanism by which co-infection may modulate immune responses in HBV patients.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform the understanding of immune modulation in HBV patients with co-infections, potentially guiding future research on immune-based therapies and surveillance strategies for co-infection scenarios.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is cross-sectional, limiting its ability to establish causality. The evidence is based on correlations and mediation analyses, which may not fully capture the complex interactions between infections and immune responses. The confidence is moderate.

慢性乙型肝炎 中识别需要延长干扰素治疗的患者的预测模型

Predictive model to identify patients requiring extended interferon therapy in chronic hepatitis B
作者： Cheng-Yuan Tsai, Ching-Shan Huang
摘要中文翻译Yan 等人发表在《世界胃肠病学杂志》上的一项当代研究表明,可以在预测模型中利用基线乙型肝炎表面抗原和肝硬化来识别可能受益于延长(≥ 48 周)干扰素治疗的人群。在这篇社论中,我们总结了他们的研究优势。第一,随访时间足够长;其次,作者确定了所有主要的混杂因素;第三,采用适当的统计方法;第四,图表清晰易读;第五,训练后的队列研究表现出稳定的判别能力。该研究的局限性如下:首先,某些亚组的样本量过小;其次,几乎所有类似的研究都是在中国进行的,导致读者对该领域的全球趋势了解有限;第三,在核苷(酸)类似物疗法中添加聚乙二醇化干扰素α-2b的好处没有得到解决。未来的研究应该调查涉及聚乙二醇化干扰素 α-2b 和核苷(酸)类似物的联合疗法的有前景的作用,结合机器学习方法,并考虑并发非酒精性脂肪肝疾病的影响。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在开发一个预测模型来识别可能受益于延长干扰素治疗的 慢性乙型肝炎 患者。这一点很重要,因为确定哪些患者需要更长的 治疗 持续时间存在挑战。
设计与对象
该研究对 慢性乙型肝炎 患者进行了回顾性分析,重点关注接受干扰素治疗的患者。随访时间足够长,训练后的队列研究表现出稳定的判别能力。
核心方法
作者采用了适当的统计方法并确定了所有主要的混杂因素。他们使用基线乙型肝炎表面抗原水平和肝硬化状态作为模型中的预测因子。
主要结果
该研究表明,基线乙型肝炎表面抗原和肝硬化可以有效地用于预测模型中,以识别可能受益于延长干扰素治疗的患者。
解释与创新
作者将他们的发现解释为一种根据基线标志物个性化 慢性乙型肝炎 患者干扰素治疗持续时间的新方法。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以帮助识别可能需要延长干扰素治疗的患者,从而为临床决策提供信息,从而有可能改善 治疗 的结果。
局限与证据强度
该研究存在局限性,包括某些亚组的样本量较小以及关注中国人群,这可能会限制结果的普遍性。由于这些限制,证据的强度中等。

Predictive model to identify patients requiring extended interferon therapy in chronic hepatitis B

Authors: Cheng-Yuan Tsai, Ching-Shan Huang
Original abstract (English)
A contemporary study by Yan et al , published in the World Journal of Gastroen terology , demonstrates that baseline hepatitis B surface antigen and cirrhosis can be utilized in a prediction model to identify populations that may benefit from extended (≥ 48 weeks) interferon therapy. In this editorial, we summarize strengths of their study. First, the follow-up period was sufficiently long; second, the authors identified all major confounding factors; third, appropriate statistical methods were employed; fourth, the figures and tables are highly legible; and fifth, the training cohort exhibited stable discriminatory ability. The limitations of the study are as follows: First, the sample size in certain subgroups was too small; second, nearly all similar studies have been conducted in China, leaving readers with a limited understanding of global trends in this field; and third, the benefits of adding PEGylated interferon α-2b to nucleos(t)ide analog therapy were not addressed. Future research should investigate the promising role of combination therapies involving PEGylated interferon α-2b and nucleos(t)ide analogs, incorporate machine learning approaches, and consider the effects of concurrent nonalcoholic fatty liver disease.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to develop a predictive model to identify patients with chronic hepatitis B who may benefit from extended interferon therapy. This is important due to the challenges in determining which patients require longer treatment durations.
Design and population
The study involved a retrospective analysis of patients with chronic hepatitis B, focusing on those who received interferon therapy. The follow-up period was sufficiently long, and the training cohort exhibited stable discriminatory ability.
Core methods
The authors employed appropriate statistical methods and identified all major confounding factors. They used baseline hepatitis B surface antigen levels and cirrhosis status as predictors in their model.
Main results
The study demonstrated that baseline hepatitis B surface antigen and cirrhosis can be effectively used in a prediction model to identify patients who may benefit from extended interferon therapy.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted their findings as a novel approach to personalizing interferon therapy duration for chronic hepatitis B patients, based on baseline markers.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform clinical decision-making by helping to identify patients who may require extended interferon therapy, potentially improving treatment outcomes.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study had limitations, including a small sample size in certain subgroups and a focus on Chinese populations, which may limit the generalizability of the results. The evidence is moderate in strength due to these limitations.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

尼日利亚大学健康科学专业学生乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 覆盖率和职业风险暴露的横断面研究

A cross-sectional study of hepatitis B vaccination coverage and occupational risk exposure among health science students in a Nigerian University
作者： Ismail Tajudeen Hamza, Anas Ibrahim Yakubu, Amir Yahaya, Abubakar Sadiq Ibrahim, Abdullahi SarkinFulani Suleiman, Abdulsalam Bello, Ibrahim Mohammed Alhassan, Adeyemi Sallam Febilola, Ahmad Onikoko Abdulgafar, Jubril Faki Aminullahi
摘要中文翻译摘要 简介 尽管存在针对 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 的安全有效的 疫苗,但健康科学专业的学生仍然面临巨大的感染风险。本研究评估了尼日利亚索科托乌斯马努·丹佛迪约大学健康科学专业学生的 HBV 疫苗接种 覆盖率、认知度和相关因素。方法论 2025 年 7 月 19 日至 9 月 10 日,索科托乌斯马努·丹佛迪约大学 (Usmanu Danfodiyo University Sokoto) 开展了一项针对 427 名健康科学专业学生的横断面研究。使用结构化问卷收集数据,评估乙型肝炎意识、疫苗接种 状态、障碍、激励因素和职业风险。分析使用 IBM SPSS 25 版,具有描述性统计、卡方检验 (p < 0.05) 和二元逻辑回归,而 Hosmer–Lemeshow 检验和 Nagelkerke R2 评估模型拟合度和解释方差。结果 共有 410 名学生参与(平均年龄 22.36 ± 2.94 岁)。对乙型肝炎疫苗的认知度较高(87.8%),但接种率较低(26.8%),只有12.2%的人完全接种了疫苗,未接种的学生中有50.7%愿意接种。主要障碍包括 疫苗 难以获取、成本和信息有限,而主要动机是先前接触过感染(57.3%)、机构要求(47.6%)和对公共卫生机构的信任(43.9%)。职业接触很常见,但接触后的做法很差。吸收与学习部门和水平显着相关。Logistic 回归将女性性别(AOR = 2.15,p = 0.007)、院系和更高的学习水平(AOR = 1.006,p = 0.001)确定为独立预测因素。结论 尽管知晓,但乙型肝炎 疫苗 的摄取率仍然较低;改善入学机会、教育和强制性政策对于高风险健康科学学生至关重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估尼日利亚健康科学专业学生的 HBV 疫苗接种 覆盖率和认识,解决了解 疫苗接种 障碍的需要,尽管存在安全有效的 疫苗。
设计与对象
2025 年 7 月 19 日至 9 月 10 日,尼日利亚索科托乌斯马努·丹佛迪约大学 (Usmanu Danfodiyo University) 的 410 名健康科学专业学生进行了一项横断面研究。
核心方法
使用结构化调查问卷收集数据,并使用 IBM SPSS 25 版进行分析,包括描述性统计、卡方检验和二元逻辑回归。
主要结果
共有 410 名学生参与,知晓率较高(87.8%),但接种率较低(26.8%),只有 12.2% 的学生完全接种了疫苗。疫苗 难以获得、成本和信息有限等因素是主要障碍,而激励因素包括之前接触过感染、机构要求和对公共卫生机构的信任。
解释与创新
该研究强调,尽管人们已经意识到,但 HBV 疫苗 的摄取率仍然较低,这表明需要改善高风险健康科学学生的获取机会、教育和强制性政策。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为提高 HBV 疫苗接种 覆盖率和改善健康科学专业学生的公共卫生政策、降低 HBV 感染和相关并发症的风险提供策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究的横断面设计和对自我报告数据的依赖限制了研究结果的普遍性。样本量和特定的人口统计因素也可能影响结果的代表性。

A cross-sectional study of hepatitis B vaccination coverage and occupational risk exposure among health science students in a Nigerian University

Authors: Ismail Tajudeen Hamza, Anas Ibrahim Yakubu, Amir Yahaya, Abubakar Sadiq Ibrahim, Abdullahi SarkinFulani Suleiman, Abdulsalam Bello, Ibrahim Mohammed Alhassan, Adeyemi Sallam Febilola, Ahmad Onikoko Abdulgafar, Jubril Faki Aminullahi
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Introduction Despite the presence of a safe and effective vaccine for hepatitis B virus (HBV), health science students remain at significant risk for infection. This study assessed HBV vaccination coverage, awareness, and associated factors among health science students at Usmanu Danfodiyo University in Sokoto, Nigeria. Methodology A cross-sectional study targeting 427 health science students was conducted at Usmanu Danfodiyo University Sokoto between July 19 and September 10, 2025. Data were collected using a structured questionnaire assessing hepatitis B awareness, vaccination status, barriers, motivators, and occupational risks. Analysis used IBM SPSS version 25, with descriptive statistics, chi-square tests ( p < 0.05), and binary logistic regression, while the Hosmer–Lemeshow test and Nagelkerke R2 assessed model fit and explained variance. Results A total of 410 students participated (mean age 22.36 ± 2.94 years). Awareness of the hepatitis B vaccine was high (87.8%), but uptake was low (26.8%), only 12.2% were fully vaccinated, and 50.7% of unvaccinated students were willing to vaccinate. Key barriers included vaccine inaccessibility, cost, and limited information, while major motivators were prior exposure to infection (57.3%), institutional requirements (47.6%), and trust in public health institutions (43.9%). Occupational exposures were common, with poor post-exposure practices. Uptake was significantly associated with department and level of study. Logistic regression identified female gender (AOR = 2.15, p = 0.007), department, and higher level of study (AOR = 1.006, p = 0.001) as independent predictors. Conclusion Low hepatitis B vaccine uptake persists despite awareness; improving access, education, and mandatory policies is essential for high-risk health science students.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess HBV vaccination coverage and awareness among health science students in Nigeria, addressing the need to understand barriers to vaccination despite the availability of a safe and effective vaccine.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted among 410 health science students at Usmanu Danfodiyo University in Sokoto, Nigeria, from July 19 to September 10, 2025.
Core methods
Data were collected using a structured questionnaire and analyzed using IBM SPSS version 25, including descriptive statistics, chi-square tests, and binary logistic regression.
Main results
A total of 410 students participated, with high awareness (87.8%) but low uptake (26.8%) and only 12.2% fully vaccinated. Factors like vaccine inaccessibility, cost, and limited information were key barriers, while motivators included prior exposure to infection, institutional requirements, and trust in public health institutions.
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlighted the persistence of low HBV vaccine uptake despite awareness, suggesting the need for improved access, education, and mandatory policies for high-risk health science students.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform strategies to enhance HBV vaccination coverage and improve public health policies for health science students, reducing the risk of HBV infection and associated complications.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's cross-sectional design and reliance on self-reported data limit the generalizability of the findings. The sample size and specific demographic factors may also affect the representativeness of the results.

治疗前 HBs-Ag 水平对慢性丁型肝炎患者干扰素治疗反应的影响。

The impact of pretreatment HBs-Ag levels on response to interferon therapy in chronic hepatitis D patients.
作者： Salih Kiliç, Ahmed Taha, Mehmet Yalniz, İbrahim Halil Bahçecioğlu
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨治疗前 HBsAg 水平作为慢性丁型肝炎患者对 Peg-IFNα 治疗 治疗 反应的预测因子的作用。背景强调了 HDV 复制对 HBV 的病毒学依赖性,表明 HBsAg 水平可能影响 治疗 结果。
设计与对象
对 42 例接受 48 周 Peg-IFNα 治疗的慢性丁型肝炎患者进行回顾性分析。所有患者的 HBsAg、抗 HDV 和 HDV-RNA 均呈阳性。
核心方法
使用 Architect HBsAg 测定法测量基线定量 HBsAg 水平。统计分析包括 t 检验、Mann-Whitney U 检验、卡方检验和 ROC 分析。
主要结果
Out of the 42 patients, 25 (59.5%) had available SVR data at week 72. Fourteen of them (56.0%) achieved SVR and 11 (44.0%) were non-responders. The mean baseline HBsAg level was 2972 IU/mL (±1208.8 SD). The t-test showed no significant difference in mean HBsAg levels between SVR responders (2789.4 IU/mL ±1421.8 SD) and non-responders (3185.2 IU/mL ±801.5 SD; p = 0.412). ROC analysis for SVR yielded an AUC of 0.589 (95% CI: 0.381-0.797). For EOT response, the AUC was 0.606 (95% CI: 0.421-0.791). Exploratory quartile analysis for EOT response revealed a significant association (p = 0.008), but this finding should be interpreted with caution due to the multiplicity of comparisons.
解释与创新
作者得出结论,基线 HBsAg 水平并不能预测 治疗 后 24 周时的 SVR。他们还指出,EOT 反应的预测价值有限(AUC 0.606)。这项研究提供的证据表明,仅定量 HBsAg 检测可能不足以用于慢性丁型肝炎的临床决策。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,HBsAg 水平可能不是慢性丁型肝炎患者 治疗 反应的可靠预测因子,这可能表明需要更准确的生物标志物来实现个性化 治疗 策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和比较的多样性而受到限制,这可能会影响探索性发现的解释。证据强度中等,因为结果基于相对较小的样本量和不能完全证实预测值的统计分析。

The impact of pretreatment HBs-Ag levels on response to interferon therapy in chronic hepatitis D patients.

Authors: Salih Kiliç, Ahmed Taha, Mehmet Yalniz, İbrahim Halil Bahçecioğlu
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Hepatitis D virus (HDV) infection is closely associated with hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection. Given the virological dependence of HDV on HBV for its replication, quantitative HBsAg levels have been considered as potential predictors of treatment outcomes. This study aims to investigate the actual role of pretreatment HBsAg levels as predictors of treatment response to Peg-IFNα therapy in chronic hepatitis D patients. METHODS: A retrospective analysis was conducted on 42 patients with chronic hepatitis D who had received a 48-week course of Peg-IFNα therapy (180 μg weekly). All of these patients were positive for HBsAg, anti-HDV, and HDV-RNA. Sustained virological response (SVR) was defined as undetectable HDV-RNA (<100 copies/mL) at 24 weeks subsequent to treatment completion (week 72). End-of-treatment (EOT) response was defined as undetectable HDV-RNA at week 48. Baseline quantitative HBsAg levels were measured using the Architect HBsAg assay. Statistical analyses included t-tests, Mann-Whitney U tests, Chi-square tests, and ROC analysis. RESULTS: Out of the 42 patients, 25 (59.5%) had available SVR data at week 72. Fourteen of them (56.0%) achieved SVR and 11 (44.0%) were non-responders. The mean baseline HBsAg level was 2972 IU/mL (±1208.8 SD). The t-test showed no significant difference in mean HBsAg levels between SVR responders (2789.4 IU/mL ±1421.8 SD) and non-responders (3185.2 IU/mL ±801.5 SD; p = 0.412). ROC analysis for SVR yielded an AUC of 0.589 (95% CI: 0.381-0.797). For EOT response, the AUC was 0.606 (95% CI: 0.421-0.791). Even though exploratory quartile analysis for EOT response revealed a significant association (p = 0.008), given the multiplicity of comparisons, this finding should be interpreted with caution. CONCLUSION: Baseline HBsAg levels do not predict SVR at 24 weeks post-treatment. The predictive value for EOT response is limited (AUC 0.606). Quantitative HBsAg testing alone is unlikely to provide sufficient predictive accuracy for clinical decision-making in CHD. Sustained HBsAg decline during treatment may provide a perusasive rationale for personalized treatment duration.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate the role of pretreatment HBsAg levels as predictors of treatment response to Peg-IFNα therapy in chronic hepatitis D patients. The background highlights the virological dependence of HDV on HBV for replication, suggesting that HBsAg levels may influence treatment outcomes.
Design and population
A retrospective analysis was conducted on 42 patients with chronic hepatitis D who had received a 48-week course of Peg-IFNα therapy. All patients were positive for HBsAg, anti-HDV, and HDV-RNA.
Core methods
Baseline quantitative HBsAg levels were measured using the Architect HBsAg assay. Statistical analyses included t-tests, Mann-Whitney U tests, Chi-square tests, and ROC analysis.
Main results
Out of the 42 patients, 25 (59.5%) had available SVR data at week 72. Fourteen of them (56.0%) achieved SVR and 11 (44.0%) were non-responders. The mean baseline HBsAg level was 2972 IU/mL (±1208.8 SD). The t-test showed no significant difference in mean HBsAg levels between SVR responders (2789.4 IU/mL ±1421.8 SD) and non-responders (3185.2 IU/mL ±801.5 SD; p = 0.412). ROC analysis for SVR yielded an AUC of 0.589 (95% CI: 0.381-0.797). For EOT response, the AUC was 0.606 (95% CI: 0.421-0.791). Exploratory quartile analysis for EOT response revealed a significant association (p = 0.008), but this finding should be interpreted with caution due to the multiplicity of comparisons.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that baseline HBsAg levels do not predict SVR at 24 weeks post-treatment. They also noted that the predictive value for EOT response is limited (AUC 0.606). This study provides evidence that quantitative HBsAg testing alone may not be sufficient for clinical decision-making in chronic hepatitis D.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that HBsAg levels may not be reliable predictors of treatment response in chronic hepatitis D patients, which could inform the need for more accurate biomarkers for personalized treatment strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and the multiplicity of comparisons, which may affect the interpretation of exploratory findings. The evidence strength is moderate, as the results are based on a relatively small sample size and statistical analyses that do not fully confirm predictive value.

尼日利亚奥韦里联邦教学医院成年男性贫血、乙型肝炎病毒 和幽门螺杆菌抗体阳性的 患病率

Prevalence of Anaemia, Hepatitis B Virus, and Helicobacter pylori Antibody Positivity among Adult Men Attending Federal Teaching Hospital Owerri Nigeria

View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Anaemia, hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) positivity and Helicobacter pylori antibody positivity may be encountered in adult male hospital patients. This study aimed to estimate their prevalence among adult men attending Federal Teaching Hospital, Owerri, Nigeria.
Design and population
This hospital-based cross-sectional study included 150 consenting adult male patients aged 18–80 years recruited during October–November 2025.

伴有乙型肝炎反应性血清阳性类风湿性关节炎的肾静脉血栓形成伴肾积水:提示肾结核的诊断挑战

Renal vein thrombosis with hydronephrosis in seropositive rheumatoid arthritis with hepatitis B reactivity: a diagnostic challenge suggestive of renal tuberculosis
作者： Rathod Sai Harshini, Yatri Patel, Pankhuri Saxena, Abhinash Jha, Minakshi Dhar
摘要中文翻译类风湿性关节炎 (RA) 与感染易感性增加相关,而免疫抑制 治疗 可能会使结核病 (TB) 的识别和治疗进一步复杂化。肾结核是泌尿生殖系统结核的一种惰性形式,可能表现为非特异性的体质和泌尿系统表现,并可能导致梗阻性尿路病。肾静脉血栓形成(RVT)是一种罕见的血管并发症,可能在全身炎症或感染的情况下发生。我们报告了一名 65 岁男性,最近被诊断为血清阳性 RA 和乙型肝炎表面抗原阳性,他出现六个月的间歇性低热、全身症状和对称性炎性多关节炎,随后出现左侧腰部疼痛、间歇性血尿和双侧下肢肿胀。实验室评估显示贫血、炎症标志物显着升高、类风湿因子和抗 CCP 抗体强阳性。HBsAg 呈阳性,而 HBV DNA 未检测到。增强 CT 显示左肾体积较大,伴有中度肾积水、盆腔扩张、PUJ 梗阻和左 RVT 并伴有广泛的侧支循环形成。尿培养无菌且肾功能得以保留。随后的尿液 CBNAAT 检测到结核分枝杆菌并显示利福平敏感性,在适当的临床和放射学背景下确定肾结核。该患者接受了抗凝、RA 导向治疗、支持措施以及随后的药物敏感抗结核治疗。该病例强调了炎症、感染和血栓表现重叠所带来的诊断挑战,并强调了对肾积水、泌尿系统症状和不明原因 RVT 患者考虑肾结核的重要性,特别是在常规细菌培养无菌的情况下。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在强调在血清阳性类风湿性关节炎和乙型肝炎反应性患者中识别肾结核的诊断挑战,强调炎症、感染和血栓表现的重叠。
设计与对象
病例报告 描述了一名 65 岁男性,最近被诊断为血清阳性 RA 和乙型肝炎表面抗原阳性,出现一系列症状,包括间歇性低热、全身症状、对称性炎性多关节炎、左侧腰部疼痛、间歇性血尿和双侧下肢肿胀。
核心方法
患者接受了实验室评估,包括血液检查和尿培养,以及对比增强 CT 成像以评估肾静脉血栓形成和肾积水。尿液CBNAAT用于检测结核分枝杆菌并评估药敏。
主要结果
实验室评估显示贫血、炎症标志物显着升高、类风湿因子和抗 CCP 抗体强阳性。尿液 CBNAAT 检测到结核分枝杆菌并显示利福平敏感性,在适当的临床和放射学背景下证实肾结核。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为一个病例,强调了重叠的炎症、感染和血栓表现所带来的诊断挑战,并强调了在肾积水、泌尿系统症状和不明原因 RVT 患者中考虑肾结核的重要性,特别是当常规细菌培养无菌时。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,对于血清阳性类风湿性关节炎和乙型肝炎反应性患者,即使细菌培养物是无菌的,且出现不明原因的肾静脉血栓形成和肾积水,临床医生也应考虑肾结核。
局限与证据强度
该研究是单一的病例报告,这限制了其普遍性。然而,考虑到临床和放射学背景,以及通过尿液 CBNAAT 确认结核分枝杆菌,证据是强有力的。

Renal vein thrombosis with hydronephrosis in seropositive rheumatoid arthritis with hepatitis B reactivity: a diagnostic challenge suggestive of renal tuberculosis

Authors: Rathod Sai Harshini, Yatri Patel, Pankhuri Saxena, Abhinash Jha, Minakshi Dhar
Original abstract (English)
Rheumatoid arthritis (RA) is associated with an increased susceptibility to infection, while immunosuppressive treatment may further complicate the recognition and management of tuberculosis (TB). Renal TB is an indolent form of genitourinary TB that may present with non-specific constitutional and urinary manifestations and can cause obstructive uropathy. Renal vein thrombosis (RVT) is an uncommon vascular complication that may occur in the setting of systemic inflammation or infection. We report a 65-year-old man with recently diagnosed seropositive RA and hepatitis B surface antigen positivity who presented with six months of intermittent low-grade fever, constitutional symptoms and symmetrical inflammatory polyarthritis, followed by left flank pain, intermittent haematuria and bilateral lower-limb swelling. Laboratory evaluation showed anaemia, markedly elevated inflammatory markers and strongly positive rheumatoid factor and anti-CCP antibodies. HBsAg was positive, while HBV DNA was undetectable. Contrast-enhanced CT demonstrated a bulky left kidney with moderate hydronephrosis, pelvicalyceal dilatation, PUJ obstruction and left RVT with extensive collateral formation. Urine culture was sterile and renal function was preserved. Subsequent urine CBNAAT detected Mycobacterium tuberculosis and showed rifampicin susceptibility, establishing renal TB in the appropriate clinical and radiological context. The patient was treated with anticoagulation, RA-directed therapy, supportive measures and subsequently drug-susceptible antitubercular therapy. This case emphasizes the diagnostic challenge posed by overlapping inflammatory, infective and thrombotic manifestations and highlights the importance of considering renal TB in patients with hydronephrosis, urinary symptoms and unexplained RVT, particularly when routine bacterial cultures are sterile.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to highlight the diagnostic challenges in identifying renal tuberculosis in patients with seropositive rheumatoid arthritis and hepatitis B reactivity, emphasizing the overlap of inflammatory, infective and thrombotic manifestations.
Design and population
This case report describes a 65-year-old man with recently diagnosed seropositive RA and hepatitis B surface antigen positivity who presented with a range of symptoms including intermittent low-grade fever, constitutional symptoms, symmetrical inflammatory polyarthritis, left flank pain, intermittent haematuria and bilateral lower-limb swelling.
Core methods
The patient underwent laboratory evaluation, including blood tests and urine culture, and imaging with contrast-enhanced CT to assess renal vein thrombosis and hydronephrosis. Urine CBNAAT was used to detect Mycobacterium tuberculosis and assess drug susceptibility.
Main results
Laboratory evaluation revealed anaemia, markedly elevated inflammatory markers, strongly positive rheumatoid factor and anti-CCP antibodies. Urine CBNAAT detected Mycobacterium tuberculosis and showed rifampicin susceptibility, confirming renal TB in the appropriate clinical and radiological context.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as a case emphasizing the diagnostic challenge posed by overlapping inflammatory, infective and thrombotic manifestations and highlight the importance of considering renal TB in patients with hydronephrosis, urinary symptoms and unexplained RVT, particularly when routine bacterial cultures are sterile.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that clinicians should consider renal tuberculosis in patients with seropositive rheumatoid arthritis and hepatitis B reactivity who present with unexplained renal vein thrombosis and hydronephrosis, even when bacterial cultures are sterile.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is a single case report, which limits its generalizability. However, the evidence is strong given the clinical and radiological context, along with the confirmation of Mycobacterium tuberculosis via urine CBNAAT.

巴西东北部本科护生关于乙型肝炎的完整性和知识与 疫苗接种 相关的因素:横断面研究

Factors Associated with Vaccination Completeness and Knowledge About Hepatitis B Among Undergraduate Nursing Students in Northeastern Brazil: A Cross-Sectional Study
作者： Danielle Nedson Rodrigues de Macêdo, Breno Dias de Oliveira Martins, Luiza Carolinda de Sousa, Emanoelle Fernandes Silva, João Felipe Tinto Silva, Gilney Guerra de Medeiros, Polyana Norberta Mendes, Matheus Sousa Marques Carvalho, Jefferson Abraão Caetano Lira, Cecília Natielly da Silva Gomes
摘要中文翻译乙型肝炎仍然是本科护生的一个重要职业风险,特别是由于潜在的接触,因此 疫苗接种 和感染知识对于他们整个职业生涯的保护至关重要。目的:本研究旨在分析巴西东北部本科护生乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 完整性和知识的相关因素。方法:对巴西东北部一所公立大学三个校区的护理本科生进行了一项横断面分析研究。数据是在 2025 年 10 月至 12 月期间使用经过验证的工具收集的,这些工具通过分析 疫苗接种 卡来评估社会人口统计和学术特征、知识以及 疫苗接种 状态。使用多变量二项式逻辑回归评估关联性,结果显示为调整后的比值比和 95% 置信区间。结果:74.3% 的本科护理生观察到 疫苗接种 完整性(至少 3 剂 HepB 疫苗),而 57.9% 的学生表现出足够的乙型肝炎知识(至少 75% 的评估问题正确回答)。与 疫苗接种 完整性较低几率相关的变量包括家庭收入低于最低工资两倍、未参加传染病课程、缺乏乙型肝炎培训、未接受性传播疾病快速检测以及报告目前使用非法药物。在乙肝知识匮乏方面,没有参加过传染病相关课程以及没有进行过艾滋病、梅毒、乙肝快速检测的学生知识匮乏的可能性更大。相比之下,乙型肝炎培训和之前的快速检测与知识不足的可能性较低有关。结论:相当一部分护生未完全接种疫苗,而大多数护生对乙型肝炎有良好的了解。研究结果强调了从本科教育开始就加强免疫举措的必要性,特别是通过改善 疫苗接种 的获取和提供乙型肝炎培训。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
乙型肝炎仍然是本科护生的一个重要职业风险,特别是由于潜在的接触,因此 疫苗接种 和感染知识对于他们整个职业生涯的保护至关重要。
设计与对象
对巴西东北部一所公立大学三个校区的护理本科生进行了一项分析性横断面研究。
核心方法
数据是在 2025 年 10 月至 12 月期间使用经过验证的工具收集的,这些工具通过分析 疫苗接种 卡来评估社会人口统计和学术特征、知识以及 疫苗接种 状态。使用多变量二项式逻辑回归评估关联性,结果显示为调整后的比值比和 95% 置信区间。
主要结果
74.3% 的护理本科学生观察到 疫苗接种 完整性(至少 3 剂 HepB 疫苗),而 57.9% 的学生表现出对乙型肝炎有足够的了解(至少 75% 的评估问题正确回答)。与 疫苗接种 完整性较低几率相关的变量包括家庭收入低于最低工资两倍、未参加传染病课程、缺乏乙型肝炎培训、未接受性传播疾病快速检测以及报告目前使用非法药物。
解释与创新
研究结果强调了护生中教育和健康相关因素与 疫苗接种 乙型肝炎状况和知识之间的关联。
科研与公卫意义
结果强调了将乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 和教育纳入本科护理课程以改善职业安全和公共卫生结果的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到横截面设计的限制,无法确定因果关系,并且依赖自我报告的数据,可能会引入偏差。由于这些限制,证据的强度中等。

Factors Associated with Vaccination Completeness and Knowledge About Hepatitis B Among Undergraduate Nursing Students in Northeastern Brazil: A Cross-Sectional Study

Authors: Danielle Nedson Rodrigues de Macêdo, Breno Dias de Oliveira Martins, Luiza Carolinda de Sousa, Emanoelle Fernandes Silva, João Felipe Tinto Silva, Gilney Guerra de Medeiros, Polyana Norberta Mendes, Matheus Sousa Marques Carvalho, Jefferson Abraão Caetano Lira, Cecília Natielly da Silva Gomes
Original abstract (English)
Hepatitis B remains an important occupational risk for undergraduate nursing students, particularly due to potential exposure, making vaccination and infection knowledge essential for protection throughout their professional careers. Objective: This study aimed to analyze the factors associated with vaccination completeness and knowledge about hepatitis B among undergraduate nursing students in Northeastern Brazil. Methods: An analytical cross-sectional study was conducted with undergraduate nursing students from three campuses of a public university in Northeastern Brazil. Data were collected between October and December 2025 using validated instruments that assessed sociodemographic and academic characteristics, knowledge, and vaccination status through analysis of vaccination cards. Associations were assessed using multivariable binomial logistic regression, and results are shown as adjusted odds ratios and 95% confidence intervals. Results: Vaccination completeness (minimum of 3 doses of HepB vaccine) was observed in 74.3% of the undergraduate nursing students, while 57.9% demonstrated adequate knowledge about hepatitis B (minimum of 75% of assessment questions properly answered). The variables associated with lower odds of vaccination completeness were a family income lower than two minimum wages, not having taken the infectious diseases course, absence of training on hepatitis B, not having undergone rapid test for sexually transmitted diseases, and reported current use of illicit drugs. Regarding inadequate knowledge about hepatitis B, students who had not taken the course related to infectious diseases and those who had not undergone rapid tests for HIV, syphilis, and hepatitis B were more likely to have inadequate knowledge. In contrast, hepatitis B training and previous rapid testing were associated with lower likelihood of inadequate knowledge. Conclusions: A substantial proportion of nursing students was incompletely vaccinated, while most demonstrated a good level of knowledge about hepatitis B. The findings reinforce the need to strengthen immunization initiatives from the beginning of undergraduate education, particularly by improving access to vaccination and providing training on hepatitis B.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatitis B remains an important occupational risk for undergraduate nursing students, particularly due to potential exposure, making vaccination and infection knowledge essential for protection throughout their professional careers.
Design and population
An analytical cross-sectional study was conducted with undergraduate nursing students from three campuses of a public university in Northeastern Brazil.
Core methods
Data were collected between October and December 2025 using validated instruments that assessed sociodemographic and academic characteristics, knowledge, and vaccination status through analysis of vaccination cards. Associations were assessed using multivariable binomial logistic regression, and results are shown as adjusted odds ratios and 95% confidence intervals.
Main results
Vaccination completeness (minimum of 3 doses of HepB vaccine) was observed in 74.3% of the undergraduate nursing students, while 57.9% demonstrated adequate knowledge about hepatitis B (minimum of 75% of assessment questions properly answered). The variables associated with lower odds of vaccination completeness were a family income lower than two minimum wages, not having taken the infectious diseases course, absence of training on hepatitis B, not having undergone rapid test for sexually transmitted diseases, and reported current use of illicit drugs.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight the association between educational and health-related factors and vaccination status and knowledge about hepatitis B among nursing students.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results emphasize the importance of integrating hepatitis B vaccination and education into undergraduate nursing curricula to improve occupational safety and public health outcomes.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design, which cannot establish causality, and by the reliance on self-reported data, which may introduce bias. The evidence is moderate in strength due to these limitations.

在慢性乙型肝炎治疗期间接受基于韧性的护理与标准护理的慢性乙型肝炎患者抑郁症状和药物依从性比较:一项回顾性队列研究研究

Depression and medication adherence among chronic hepatitis B patients receiving resilience-based care versus standard care during antiviral therapy: a retrospective cohort study
作者： Yang X, Zhao P, Zhang L, Fang C
摘要中文翻译摘要目的评估接受 抗病毒药物 治疗的慢性乙型肝炎患者在标准护理之外接受以恢复力为中心的计划与抑郁症状和药物依从性之间的关系。方法在这项回顾性 队列研究 研究中,纳入了 2022 年 7 月至 2025 年 6 月期间在我院肝病门诊诊断为慢性乙型肝炎并接受治疗的 200 例患者。然而,由于分配是非随机的,这些关联可能会受到自我选择和残余混杂的影响。结论在这项回顾性 队列研究 研究中,除了标准护理外,接受以复原力为重点的计划与慢性乙型肝炎患者抑郁症状降低、药物依从性更好以及更有利的病毒学和生化结果相关。关键词:慢性乙型肝炎、抑郁、复原力干预、回顾性研究、治疗 2.材料和方法2.1.研究人群这项回顾性队列研究研究利用从我院肝病门诊电子病历系统中提取的数据。两组的基线人口统计学和临床​​特征,在表 1 中检索和总结(在结果部分中呈现)表明,组分布反映了在现实世界实践中接受结构化心理干预的合格患者的自然比例。根据历史记录,100 名除标准护理外还自愿完成结构化的 12 周复原力提升计划的患者构成观察组,100 名仅接受标准护理的患者构成对照组。6 个月随访的主要结果是通过患者健康问卷 9 (PHQ-9) 测量的抑郁症状严重程度以及通过 Me...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估接受 抗病毒药物 治疗的 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 患者在标准护理之外接受以恢复力为中心的计划与抑郁症状和药物依从性之间的关系。这很重要,因为抑郁症和药物依从性差会对慢性乙型肝炎患者的 治疗 结果产生负面影响。
设计与对象
这项回顾性 队列研究 研究纳入了 2022 年 7 月至 2025 年 6 月期间诊断为慢性乙型肝炎并在肝病诊所接受治疗的 200 名患者。患者被分为两组:100 名完成了 12 周的恢复力促进计划的患者和 100 名仅接受标准护理的患者。
核心方法
使用多变量回归模型来调整潜在的混杂因素,纳入基线 PHQ-9、CD-RISC-10、SF-36、HBV DNA 和 ALT 值。使用药房补充记录中的药物持有率(MPR)评估用药依从性,并使用 SPSS 26.0 和 R 4.3.1 进行统计分析。
主要结果
At the 6-month follow-up, the observation group had lower depression scores (6.78 ± 3.45 vs. 9.21 ± 4.12) and fewer unplanned outpatient visits (adjusted rate ratio 0.72, 95% CI 0.56 to 0.93). However, the study acknowledges potential biases due to non-randomized allocation.
解释与创新
作者解释说,以恢复力为重点的计划可能有助于改善抑郁症状和药物依从性,从而带来更有利的病毒学和生化结果。这是一个新颖的发现,因为它强调了将心理支持纳入 CHB 治疗 的潜在好处。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,基于复原力的护理可能是慢性乙型肝炎标准 治疗 的一个有价值的补充,有可能改善患者的治疗结果并减少医疗保健的利用率。这对于加强临床实践中的患者支持策略具有重要意义。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到非随机分配的限制,这可能会引入自选择偏差和残余混杂。尽管存在这些局限性,但由于使用了调整模型和完整案例分析,证据是中等的。

Depression and medication adherence among chronic hepatitis B patients receiving resilience-based care versus standard care during antiviral therapy: a retrospective cohort study

Authors: Yang X, Zhao P, Zhang L, Fang C
Original abstract (English)
AbstractObjectiveTo evaluate the association between receiving a resilience-focused program in addition to standard care and depressive symptoms and medication adherence among patients with CHB receiving antiviral therapy.MethodsIn this retrospective cohort study, 200 patients diagnosed with CHB and treated at our hospital's hepatology clinic between July 2022 and June 2025 were included. However, because allocation was non-randomized, these associations may be affected by self-selection and residual confounding.ConclusionIn this retrospective cohort study, receiving a resilience-focused program in addition to standard care was associated with lower depressive symptoms, better medication adherence, and more favorable virological and biochemical outcomes in patients with CHB.Keywords: chronic hepatitis B, depression, resilience intervention, retrospective study, treatment adherence 2.Materials and methods2.1.Study populationThis retrospective cohort study utilized data extracted from the electronic medical record system of our hospital's hepatology clinic. Baseline demographic and clinical characteristics of both groups, retrieved and summarized in Table 1 (presented in the Results section), indicated that the group distribution reflected the natural proportion of eligible patients receiving structured psychological intervention in real-world practice. Based on historical records, 100 patients who voluntarily completed a structured 12-week resilience promotion program in addition to standard care formed the observation group, and 100 patients who received standard care only formed the control group. Primary outcomes at the 6-month follow-up were depressive symptom severity measured by the Patient Health Questionnaire-9 (PHQ-9) and medication adherence measured by the Me...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the association between receiving a resilience-focused program in addition to standard care and depressive symptoms and medication adherence among patients with chronic hepatitis B (CHB) undergoing antiviral therapy. This is important because depression and poor medication adherence can negatively impact treatment outcomes in CHB patients.
Design and population
This retrospective cohort study included 200 patients diagnosed with CHB and treated at the hepatology clinic between July 2022 and June 2025. Patients were divided into two groups: 100 who completed a 12-week resilience promotion program and 100 who received standard care only.
Core methods
Multivariable regression models were used to adjust for potential confounding factors, incorporating baseline PHQ-9, CD-RISC-10, SF-36, HBV DNA, and ALT values. Medication adherence was assessed using the Medication Possession Ratio (MPR) from pharmacy refill records, and statistical analyses were conducted using SPSS 26.0 and R 4.3.1.
Main results
At the 6-month follow-up, the observation group had lower depression scores (6.78 ± 3.45 vs. 9.21 ± 4.12) and fewer unplanned outpatient visits (adjusted rate ratio 0.72, 95% CI 0.56 to 0.93). However, the study acknowledges potential biases due to non-randomized allocation.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that the resilience-focused program may contribute to improved depressive symptoms and medication adherence, which could lead to more favorable virological and biochemical outcomes. This is a novel finding as it highlights the potential benefits of integrating psychological support into CHB treatment.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that resilience-based care could be a valuable addition to standard treatment for CHB, potentially improving patient outcomes and reducing healthcare utilization. This has implications for enhancing patient support strategies in clinical practice.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by non-randomized allocation, which may introduce self-selection bias and residual confounding. Despite these limitations, the evidence is moderate due to the use of adjusted models and complete-case analysis.

伊朗-阿富汗边境农村社区的 HBV 暴露、HBsAg 阳性和抗 HDV 血清流行率:一项基于社区的横断面研究

HBV exposure, HBsAg positivity, and anti-HDV seroprevalence in rural communities along the Iran–Afghanistan border: a community-based cross-sectional study
作者： Parvin Askari, Davod Javanmard, Motahareh Mahi-Birjand, Effat Alemzadeh, Arezou Khosrojerdi, Abdol Sattar Pagheh, Ghodsiyeh Azarkar, Soudabeh Eshaghi, Arash Ziaee, Masood Ziaee
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
伊朗东部农村地区的 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 暴露和丁型肝炎病毒 (HDV) 抗体 患病率 的特征仍未完全确定。
设计与对象
我们估计了三个边境村庄的抗 HBc 血清流行率、条件 HBsAg 阳性率和抗 HDV 血清阳性率。
核心方法
2024 年 5 月至 6 月,我们在伊朗南呼罗珊省内赫班丹县的三个村庄进行了一项基于社区的横断面血清调查,并进行了人口普查目标招募。所有可评估样本均进行了总抗 HBc 检测。仅在抗 HBc 阳性样本中测量 HBsAg,并在最终 HBsAg 阳性分类的参与者中测量抗 HDV。使用 Firth 惩罚逻辑回归估计抗 HBc 阳性率的相关性。
主要结果
在 842 名居民中,494 人参与(58.7%),489 人获得了可评估的抗 HBc 结果。抗 HBc 血清流行率 为 23.3%(114/489;95% CI 19.6%–27.3%),其中 ≤ 30 岁为 5.3%,> 30 岁为 45.3%。在抗 HBc 阳性参与者中,42/114 人(36.8%,准确 95% CI 28.0%–46.4%)HBsAg 阳性。抗 HDV 血清阳性率为 5/41 (12.2%,4.1%–26.2%)。
解释与创新
乙型肝炎病毒暴露主要集中在老年参与者中。添加教育和职业后,家族史置信区间包括 1。
科研与公卫意义
抗 HDV 血清阳性支持抗体筛查,然后进行 HDV RNA 检测以确定活动性感染。
局限与证据强度
村庄比较是描述性的,因为参与程度差异很大,而且家族史估计对模型规范很敏感。证据是中等的。

HBV exposure, HBsAg positivity, and anti-HDV seroprevalence in rural communities along the Iran–Afghanistan border: a community-based cross-sectional study

Authors: Parvin Askari, Davod Javanmard, Motahareh Mahi-Birjand, Effat Alemzadeh, Arezou Khosrojerdi, Abdol Sattar Pagheh, Ghodsiyeh Azarkar, Soudabeh Eshaghi, Arash Ziaee, Masood Ziaee
Original abstract (English)
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) exposure and hepatitis D virus (HDV) antibody prevalence remain incompletely characterized in rural eastern Iran. We estimated anti-HBc seroprevalence, conditional HBsAg positivity, and anti-HDV seropositivity in three border villages. We conducted a community-based cross-sectional serosurvey with census-targeted recruitment in three villages of Nehbandan County, South Khorasan Province, Iran, during May–June 2024. All evaluable specimens were tested for total anti-HBc. HBsAg was measured only in anti-HBc-positive specimens, and anti-HDV was measured among participants with a final HBsAg-positive classification. Correlates of anti-HBc positivity were estimated using Firth penalized logistic regression. Of 842 residents, 494 participated (58.7%), and 489 had an evaluable anti-HBc result. Anti-HBc seroprevalence was 23.3% (114/489; 95% CI 19.6%–27.3%), including 5.3% at age ≤ 30 years and 45.3% at age > 30 years. In the primary model, age > 30 years (adjusted OR 19.36, 95% CI 10.34–38.89), Khvajeh Monji Kuh residence (4.50, 2.46–8.46), and family history of HBV (2.52, 1.12–5.62) were associated with anti-HBc positivity. The family-history confidence interval included 1 after adding education and occupation. Among anti-HBc-positive participants, 42/114 (36.8%, exact 95% CI 28.0%–46.4%) were HBsAg positive. Anti-HDV seropositivity was 5/41 (12.2%, 4.1%–26.2%). Fifteen HBsAg-positive participants initiated treatment after specialist assessment. HBV exposure was concentrated among older participants. Village comparisons were descriptive because participation differed substantially, and family-history estimates were sensitive to model specification. Birth-cohort analyses did not establish an additional cohort association beyond flexible adjustment for attained age.Staged testing prevented estimation of population HBsAg prevalence. Anti-HDV seropositivity supports antibody screening followed by HDV RNA testing to establish active infection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) exposure and hepatitis D virus (HDV) antibody prevalence remain incompletely characterized in rural eastern Iran.
Design and population
We estimated anti-HBc seroprevalence, conditional HBsAg positivity, and anti-HDV seropositivity in three border villages.
Core methods
We conducted a community-based cross-sectional serosurvey with census-targeted recruitment in three villages of Nehbandan County, South Khorasan Province, Iran, during May–June 2024. All evaluable specimens were tested for total anti-HBc. HBsAg was measured only in anti-HBc-positive specimens, and anti-HDV was measured among participants with a final HBsAg-positive classification. Correlates of anti-HBc positivity were estimated using Firth penalized logistic regression.
Main results
Of 842 residents, 494 participated (58.7%), and 489 had an evaluable anti-HBc result. Anti-HBc seroprevalence was 23.3% (114/489; 95% CI 19.6%–27.3%), including 5.3% at age ≤ 30 years and 45.3% at age > 30 years. Among anti-HBc-positive participants, 42/114 (36.8%, exact 95% CI 28.0%–46.4%) were HBsAg positive. Anti-HDV seropositivity was 5/41 (12.2%, 4.1%–26.2%).
Interpretation and novelty
HBV exposure was concentrated among older participants. The family-history confidence interval included 1 after adding education and occupation.
Scientific and public-health significance
Anti-HDV seropositivity supports antibody screening followed by HDV RNA testing to establish active infection.
Limitations and evidence strength
Village comparisons were descriptive because participation differed substantially, and family-history estimates were sensitive to model specification. The evidence is moderate.

巴西东南部城市癌症患者中 疫苗 诱导的乙型肝炎免疫力的血清学证据的区域分布

Regional distribution of serological evidence of vaccine-induced immunity to hepatitis B among cancer patients in a city in southeastern Brazil
作者： de Paula V, Pinto M, Ferreira G, Battaglia D, de Oliveira R, Manchiero C, Dantas B, Tengan F, Abdala E, Magri M
摘要中文翻译本研究评估了居住在巴西圣保罗市的癌症患者中与 疫苗 诱导的乙型肝炎免疫相一致的血清学证据的区域分布。我们对 2023 年在圣保罗州癌症研究所 (ICESP) 接受乙型肝炎表面抗体 (抗 HBs) 血清学检测的成年患者进行了一项回顾性观察研究。这些发现表明,在圣保罗各地的癌症患者中,特别是老年人和男性中,与 疫苗 诱导的乙型肝炎免疫相一致的血清学证据频率较低。材料和方法研究设计这项回顾性观察研究包括 2023 年 1 月至 12 月期间在圣保罗大学医学院临床医院 (HCFMUSP) 附属的圣保罗癌症研究所 (ICESP) 接受护理的成年癌症患者。测试是在研究开始前作为常规临床护理的一部分进行的,随后从医院的病历中检索数据。伦理学本研究获得了HCFMUSP 研究项目分析伦理委员会,协议号 6.120.828 和 6.718.762(CAAE:70101521.9.0000.0068)。摘要疫苗接种是预防 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染的主要策略,但其在癌症患者中的发生率仍不充分。与 疫苗 诱导的免疫相容的血清学证据被定义为反应性抗 HBs 和非反应性乙型肝炎核心抗体(抗 HBc)。进行单变量和多变量泊松回归分析以确定相关因素。在 1,143 名患者中,平均年龄为 57.4 ± 16.42 岁,61.9% 为女性,60.6% 自认为是白人,76.8% 患有实体瘤。总体...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查巴西东南部一座城市的癌症患者中 疫苗 诱导的乙型肝炎免疫力的血清学证据的区域分布,解决了解不同地理区域和人群之间免疫力如何变化的需要。
设计与对象
对 2023 年在圣保罗州癌症研究所 (ICESP) 接受乙型肝炎表面抗体 (抗 HBs) 检测的 1,143 名成年癌症患者进行了一项回顾性观察研究。人群包括实体瘤和血液恶性肿瘤患者,人口统计和临床特征从病历中检索。
核心方法
使用单变量和多变量泊松回归分析来识别相关因素,在单变量分析中根据 p 值 < 0.20 选择变量。使用泊松回归评估居住区和血清学证据之间的关联,以估计 患病率 比率 (PR) 和 95% 置信区间 (CI)。
主要结果
总体而言,26.2% (299/1,143) 的癌症患者表现出与 疫苗 诱导的乙型肝炎免疫相一致的血清学证据。调整混杂因素后,跨区域的分布没有统计学意义,居住区域和抗 HBs 状态之间没有发现独立关联。
解释与创新
作者解释说,在调整潜在的混杂因素后,疫苗 诱导的免疫力的区域分布并未显示出与居住区的显着相关性,这凸显了在特定年龄组中开展有针对性的 疫苗接种 工作的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,乙型肝炎免疫力与年龄相关的差异非常显着,并且可能为提高高危人群(尤其是年轻人)的 疫苗接种 覆盖率的公共卫生策略提供参考。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到观察设计和潜在混杂因素的限制,但由于使用了多变量泊松回归和大样本量,且基于所采用的统计方法进行了适度的置信度评估,因此证据充足。

Regional distribution of serological evidence of vaccine-induced immunity to hepatitis B among cancer patients in a city in southeastern Brazil

Authors: de Paula V, Pinto M, Ferreira G, Battaglia D, de Oliveira R, Manchiero C, Dantas B, Tengan F, Abdala E, Magri M
Original abstract (English)
This study evaluated the regional distribution of serological evidence compatible with vaccine-induced immunity to hepatitis B among cancer patients residing in Sao Paulo city, Brazil. We conducted a retrospective observational study of adult patients who underwent hepatitis B surface antibody (anti-HBs) serological testing at the Instituto do Cancer do Estado de Sao Paulo (ICESP) in 2023. These findings indicate a low frequency of serological evidence compatible with vaccine-induced immunity to hepatitis B among cancer patients across Sao Paulo, particularly among older adults and men. MATERIALS AND METHODSStudy designThis retrospective observational study included adult cancer patients receiving care at the Instituto do Cancer do Estado de Sao Paulo (ICESP), affiliated with the Clinical Hospital of the Faculty of Medicine, University of Sao Paulo (HCFMUSP), between January and December 2023. Testing was conducted as part of routine clinical care before the study began, with data subsequently retrieved from the hospital’s medical records.EthicsThis study was approved by the Ethics Committee for Analysis of Research Projects of the HCFMUSP under protocols No 6.120.828 and 6.718.762 (CAAE: 70101521.9.0000.0068). ABSTRACTVaccination is the primary strategy for preventing hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection, but its frequency remains insufficiently characterized among cancer patients. Serological evidence compatible with vaccine-induced immunity was defined as reactive anti-HBs and non-reactive hepatitis B core antibody (anti-HBc). Univariable and multivariable Poisson regression analyses were performed to identify associated factors. Among 1,143 patients, the mean age was 57.4 ± 16.42 years, 61.9% were female, 60.6% self-identified White, and 76.8% had solid tumors. Overal...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the regional distribution of serological evidence of vaccine-induced immunity to hepatitis B among cancer patients in a city in southeastern Brazil, addressing the need to understand how immunity varies across different geographic areas and populations.
Design and population
A retrospective observational study was conducted among 1,143 adult cancer patients who underwent hepatitis B surface antibody (anti-HBs) testing at the Instituto do Cancer do Estado de Sao Paulo (ICESP) in 2023. The population included patients with solid tumors and hematological malignancies, with demographic and clinical characteristics retrieved from medical records.
Core methods
Univariable and multivariable Poisson regression analyses were used to identify associated factors, with variables selected based on p-values < 0.20 in univariable analyses. Associations between zone of residence and serological evidence were evaluated using Poisson regression to estimate prevalence ratios (PRs) and 95% confidence intervals (CIs).
Main results
Overall, 26.2% (299/1,143) of cancer patients showed serological evidence compatible with vaccine-induced immunity to hepatitis B. The distribution across zones was not statistically significant after adjustment for confounders, with no independent association found between zone of residence and anti-HBs status.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that the regional distribution of vaccine-induced immunity did not show a significant association with zone of residence after adjusting for potential confounders, highlighting the need for targeted vaccination efforts in specific age groups.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that age-related differences in immunity to hepatitis B are significant, and may inform public health strategies to improve vaccination coverage in high-risk populations, particularly younger adults.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its observational design and potential confounding factors, but the evidence is strong due to the use of multivariable Poisson regression and a large sample size, with a moderate confidence assessment based on the statistical methods employed.

利用深度学习和集成的混合方法,根据 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) X 基因的序列变异准确预测肝细胞癌

A hybrid deep learning and ensemble approach for accurate prediction of hepatocellular carcinoma from sequence variants of hepatitis B virus (HBV) X gene
作者： Sadia Ejaz, Syed Ali Nawaz
摘要中文翻译摘要:当前的研究提出了一种结合深度学习和集成学习模型的混合框架,以根据 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV)-X 基因的序列变异来预测肝细胞癌 (HCC)。这些结果强调,混合深度学习模型与集成学习和类别不平衡校​​正的集成,比传统的单一模型方法在 HBV 相关 HCC 预测方面提供了显着改进。与传统的单模型方法相比,所提出的框架将深度学习架构与数据平衡和集成学习策略相结合,以增强预测的鲁棒性和泛化性。该数据集包含 3,360 个不同的 DNA 序列,分为 HCC 和非 HCC 类别,其中显着的类别不平衡有利于非 HCC 组。为了解决这个问题,应用了合成少数过采样技术(SMOTE)来减少分类偏差并改善少数类学习。采用 One-Hot 编码和 K-mer 编码(K = 3 和 K = 6)这两种序列编码策略来捕获核苷酸组成和序列依赖性。系统地介绍了多项朴素贝叶斯(MNB)、支持媒介分类器(SVC)、Nu-Support 媒介分类器(NuSVC)、逻辑回归(LR)、XGBoost、随机森林(RF)、AdaBoost等一系列机器学习模型,以及卷积神经网络(CNN)、双向长短期记忆(BiLSTM)等深度学习模型评价。为了进一步增强预测性能,开发了一个混合堆叠集成框架,集成了 CNN-BiLSTM 架构和随机森林作为基础学习器,并以逻辑回归作为元分类器来组合它们的预测。所有方法论...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在利用 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV)-X 基因的序列变异开发肝细胞癌 (HCC) 的预测框架,以满足改进 HBV 相关 HCC 检测诊断工具的需求。
设计与对象
该研究涉及 3,360 个不同 DNA 序列的数据集,分为 HCC 和非 HCC 类别,其中明显的类别不平衡有利于非 HCC 组。该框架旨在通过深度学习和集成学习策略的集成来增强预测的鲁棒性和泛化性。
核心方法
该研究评估了一系列机器学习模型,包括多项式朴素贝叶斯 (MNB)、支持 媒介 分类器 (SVC)、Nu-Support 媒介 分类器 (NuSVC)、逻辑回归 (LR)、XGBoost、随机森林 (RF) 和 AdaBoost,以及卷积神经网络 (CNN) 和双向长短期记忆 (BiLSTM) 等深度学习模型。开发了一个混合堆叠集成框架,集成了 CNN-BiLSTM 架构和随机森林作为基础学习器,逻辑回归作为元分类器来组合它们的预测。使用 One-Hot 编码和 K-mer 编码(K = 3 和 K = 6)处理数据集,以捕获核苷酸组成和序列依赖性。
主要结果
与单独的机器学习和深度学习模型相比,混合堆叠集成模型表现出了卓越的性能。在大多数模型中,One-Hot 编码的性能始终优于 K-mer 编码。表现最好的混合堆叠集成实现了 0.9139 的准确率和 0.9126 的 F1 分数,表明分类性能和泛化之间的强大平衡。
解释与创新
作者将结果解释为,与传统的单一模型方法相比,混合深度学习模型与集成学习和类别不平衡校​​正的集成显着改善了 HBV-X 基因序列变异的 HCC 预测。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,所提出的框架可以应用于临床诊断系统,以早期、准确地检测乙型肝炎病毒相关的肝细胞癌,从而通过改进筛查和干预策略来潜在地改善公共卫生结果。
局限与证据强度
该研究的结果基于单个数据集,需要使用独立数据集进行外部验证以确认普遍性。证据是强有力的,因为所有方法步骤、模型配置和评估策略都被明确定义。

A hybrid deep learning and ensemble approach for accurate prediction of hepatocellular carcinoma from sequence variants of hepatitis B virus (HBV) X gene

Authors: Sadia Ejaz, Syed Ali Nawaz
Original abstract (English)
Abstract The current study presents a hybrid framework combining deep learning and ensemble learning models to predict hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) based on sequence variants of the hepatitis B virus (HBV)-X gene. These results highlight that the integration of hybrid deep learning models with ensemble learning and class imbalance correction provides a significant improvement over conventional single-model approaches for HBV-related HCC prediction. In contrast to traditional single-model approaches, the proposed framework integrates deep learning architectures with data balancing and ensemble learning strategies to enhance predictive robustness and generalization. The dataset comprises 3,360 distinct DNA sequences categorized into HCC and Non-HCC classes, with a significant class imbalance favoring the Non-HCC group. To address this issue, the Synthetic Minority Over-Sampling Technique (SMOTE) was applied to reduce classification bias and improve minority class learning. Two sequence encoding strategies, One-Hot encoding and K-mer encoding (K = 3 and K = 6), were employed to capture nucleotide composition and sequence dependencies. A range of machine learning models, including Multinomial Naive Bayes (MNB), Support Vector Classifier (SVC), Nu-Support Vector Classifier (NuSVC), Logistic Regression (LR), XGBoost, Random Forest (RF), and AdaBoost, as well as deep learning models such as Convolutional Neural Networks (CNN) and Bidirectional Long Short-Term Memory (BiLSTM), were systematically evaluated. To further enhance predictive performance, a hybrid stacking ensemble framework was developed, integrating a CNN–BiLSTM architecture and Random Forest as base learners, with Logistic Regression serving as the meta-classifier to combine their predictions. All methodologica...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to develop a predictive framework for hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) using sequence variants of the hepatitis B virus (HBV)-X gene, addressing the need for improved diagnostic tools in HBV-related HCC detection.
Design and population
The study involved a dataset of 3,360 distinct DNA sequences categorized into HCC and Non-HCC classes, with a significant class imbalance favoring the Non-HCC group. The framework was designed to enhance predictive robustness and generalization through integration of deep learning and ensemble learning strategies.
Core methods
The study evaluated a range of machine learning models, including Multinomial Naive Bayes (MNB), Support Vector Classifier (SVC), Nu-Support Vector Classifier (NuSVC), Logistic Regression (LR), XGBoost, Random Forest (RF), and AdaBoost, alongside deep learning models such as Convolutional Neural Networks (CNN) and Bidirectional Long Short-Term Memory (BiLSTM). A hybrid stacking ensemble framework was developed, integrating a CNN–BiLSTM architecture and Random Forest as base learners, with Logistic Regression serving as the meta-classifier to combine their predictions. The dataset was processed using One-Hot encoding and K-mer encoding (K = 3 and K = 6) to capture nucleotide composition and sequence dependencies.
Main results
The hybrid stacking ensemble model demonstrated superior performance compared to individual machine learning and deep learning models. One-Hot encoding consistently outperformed K-mer encoding across most models. The best-performing hybrid stacking ensemble achieved an accuracy of 0.9139 and an F1-score of 0.9126, indicating a strong balance between classification performance and generalization.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the results as demonstrating that the integration of hybrid deep learning models with ensemble learning and class imbalance correction significantly improves HCC prediction from HBV-X gene sequence variants compared to conventional single-model approaches.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that the proposed framework could be applied in clinical diagnostic systems for early and accurate detection of HBV-related hepatocellular carcinoma, potentially enhancing public health outcomes through improved screening and intervention strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's results are based on a single dataset and require external validation using independent datasets to confirm generalizability. The evidence is strong, as all methodological steps, model configurations, and evaluation strategies were explicitly defined.

HBeAg 阴性 慢性乙型肝炎 的有限与不定核(酸)类似物治疗:随机试验的荟萃分析

Finite Versus Indefinite Nucleos(t)ide Analogue Therapy in HBeAg ‐Negative Chronic Hepatitis B: Meta‐Analysis of Randomized Trials
作者： Demver P. Gomez, Wilmyr F. Hababag
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本研究探讨了有限与连续核苷类似物 (NA) 治疗对 HBeAg 阴性成人 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 的疗效和安全性。核心问题是,在控制病毒学复发和不良事件的同时,有限治疗是否会增加 HBsAg 消失的可能性。
证据基础与方法
证据基础包括四项平行组随机对照试验 (RCT),共有 357 名参与者。The review method involved independent screening by two reviewers and risk of bias assessment using Cochrane RoB 2. Risk ratios (RRs) were pooled using random-effects Mantel–Haenszel meta-analyses with Hartung–Knapp adjustment and Paule–Mandel estimation.使用 GRADE 方法评估证据的质量。
共识与结论
与连续治疗相比,有限 NA 治疗可能会增加 HBsAg 消失的可能性,尽管病毒学复发和再治疗的发生率较高。研究结果表明,有限疗法可能是专家中心针对 功能性治愈 的一种选择性的、以患者为中心的策略,没有证据表明存在严重伤害。
争议与缺口
HBsAg 消失的证据质量较低,且置信区间较宽。结果也存在异质性,关于 HBsAg 消失和复发之间的平衡发现相互矛盾。该研究并未涉及长期结果或有限治疗对不同 HBV 基因型的影响。
科研与实践启示
需要进一步的研究来阐明有限 NA 疗法的长期疗效和安全性,特别是在不同的 HBV 基因型和人群中。临床实践应考虑有限治疗的潜在益处和风险,尤其是在专家中心,并优先监测病毒学复发和再治疗的需要。

Finite Versus Indefinite Nucleos(t)ide Analogue Therapy in HBeAg ‐Negative Chronic Hepatitis B: Meta‐Analysis of Randomized Trials

Authors: Demver P. Gomez, Wilmyr F. Hababag
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Continuous nucleos(t)ide analogue (NA) therapy for chronic hepatitis B (CHB) effectively maintains viral suppression but rarely results in functional cure. Finite NA therapy has been proposed to increase rates of HBsAg loss; however, international guidelines provide conflicting recommendations. This study compares the efficacy and safety of finite versus continuous NA therapy in HBeAg‐negative adults using randomized controlled trials. We included parallel‐group randomized controlled trials (RCTs) comparing nucleos(t)ide analogue (NA) cessation versus continuation in non‐cirrhotic, virally suppressed HBeAg‐negative adults. Two reviewers independently screened studies and assessed risk of bias using Cochrane RoB 2. Random‐effects Mantel–Haenszel meta‐analyses with Hartung–Knapp adjustment and Paule–Mandel estimation were used to pool risk ratios (RRs) with 95% confidence intervals (CIs). Certainty of evidence was assessed using the GRADE approach. Four RCTs ( n = 357) met inclusion criteria. Discontinuation increased HBsAg loss vs. continuation (RR 5.02, 95% CI: 0.47–53.47; I 2 = 21.8%; low certainty), corresponding to ~53 additional losses per 1000 patients. However, finite therapy markedly increased virologic relapse (RR 23.09) and retreatment (RR 21.94) while reducing sustained off‐therapy virologic response (RR 0.46). Post‐cessation ALT flares were frequent but self‐limited or resolved with retreatment. No hepatic decompensation or serious adverse events occurred. In monitored, non‐cirrhotic adults, finite NA therapy may increase the probability of HBsAg loss despite predictable relapse and retreatment. With no evidence of severe harm, these findings support finite therapy as a selective, patient‐centered strategy toward functional cure in expert centers.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This study investigates the efficacy and safety of finite versus continuous nucleos(t)ide analogue (NA) therapy in HBeAg-negative adults with chronic hepatitis B (CHB). The central question is whether finite therapy increases the probability of HBsAg loss while managing virologic relapse and adverse events.
Evidence base and method
The evidence base includes four parallel-group randomized controlled trials (RCTs) with a total of 357 participants. The review method involved independent screening by two reviewers and risk of bias assessment using Cochrane RoB 2. Risk ratios (RRs) were pooled using random-effects Mantel–Haenszel meta-analyses with Hartung–Knapp adjustment and Paule–Mandel estimation. Certainty of evidence was evaluated using the GRADE approach.
Consensus and conclusions
Finite NA therapy may increase the probability of HBsAg loss compared to continuous therapy, despite higher rates of virologic relapse and retreatment. The findings suggest that finite therapy could be a selective, patient-centered strategy toward functional cure in expert centers, with no evidence of severe harm.
Controversies and gaps
The evidence for HBsAg loss is of low certainty, and the confidence intervals are wide. There is also heterogeneity in the results, with conflicting findings on the balance between HBsAg loss and relapse. The study does not address long-term outcomes or the impact of finite therapy on different HBV genotypes.
Research and practice implications
Further research is needed to clarify the long-term efficacy and safety of finite NA therapy, particularly in different HBV genotypes and populations. Clinical practice should consider the potential benefits and risks of finite therapy, especially in expert centers, and prioritize monitoring for virologic relapse and the need for retreatment.
